Periodic full-view computation controls accumulated error, while sampler-consistent reconstruction preserves the denoising trajectory. \gc{} requires neither retraining nor architectural modification and uses the position maps already available in geometry-conditioned texturing pipelines. Across Hunyuan3D-2.1, SyncMVD, and MVPainter, \gc{} achieves a stronger speed--fidelity trade-off than temporal caches and step reduction at operating points above $2\times$. On Hunyuan3D-2.1, it delivers a $2.21\times$ denoiser-loop speedup with an MV-LPIPS of 0.0293 and an MV-PSNR of 33.60 dB, providing the best fidelity among all tested methods above $2\times$. The same transferred configuration reaches the highest speedup and lowest FLOPs on SyncMVD, while \gc{} achieves the lowest FLOPs and best fidelity among the accelerated methods on MVPainter. These results establish cross-view geometry as an effective acceleration axis for multi-view texture diffusion.

</details>


### [69] [How Good are Foundation Models in Longitudinal MRI Disease Progression Reasoning?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13309)
*Wafa Al Ghallabi,Ritesh Thawkar,Sara Ghaboura,Omkar Thawakar,Numan Saeed,Dana Al Nuaimi,Ajnas Alkatheeri,Salman Khan,Fahad Shahbaz Khan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了时间感知多视图MRI基准（Time-Aware Multi-View MRI Benchmark），这是一个统一多视图解剖输入、纵向时间推理和结构化定位指导的评估框架，包含来自890名患者、3200多个纵向MRI时间点的3920个专家验证问答对，覆盖胶质母细胞瘤、神经退行性疾病、前庭神经鞘瘤和脑转移等七类临床队列。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言基准局限于单时间点、单视图的解读，无法捕捉放射学实践中至关重要的时空推理能力（跨时间点的病变变化追踪与精确定位），缺乏评估模型在真实临床纵向MRI场景下性能的系统框架。

Method: 构建包含3,920个专家验证问答对的基准数据集，来自890名患者、超过3,200个纵向MRI时间点，涵盖七个临床队列、四种疾病类型；设计了开放问答、多项选择和二元判断三种问题格式，要求模型识别最大变化区域、跨序列和视图表征进展，并提供结构化指导（边界、影像特征、混杂因素）；对16个视觉语言模型进行系统评估。

Result: 16个视觉语言模型的实验显示：模型在时间对齐方面表现中等，但在变化方向识别和体积量化上存在系统性失败；多视图输入能改善空间定位，但在紧凑架构中会降低时间推理能力。

Conclusion: 该基准为评估疾病进展追踪、纵向变化区间定位和时间排序提供了系统化框架，这些能力对于临床部署至关重要。数据集和代码已公开。

Abstract: Magnetic Resonance Imaging (MRI) interpretation is fundamental to clinical decision-making, requiring radiologists to integrate multi-view anatomical planes across sequential timepoints while precisely localizing interval changes. However, existing vision-language benchmarks remain confined to single-timepoint, single-view interpretation, failing to capture the temporal-spatial reasoning essential to radiologic practice. We introduce the Time-Aware Multi-View MRI Benchmark, an evaluation framework unifying multi-view anatomical input, temporal reasoning across longitudinal scans, and structured localization guidance. The benchmark comprises 3,920 expert-verified question-answer pairs derived from 890 patients across over 3,200 longitudinal MRI timepoints, drawn from seven clinical cohorts covering glioblastoma, neurodegeneration, vestibular schwannoma, and brain metastases, in open-ended, multiple-choice, and binary formats, requiring models to identify anatomical regions of maximal change, characterize progression across sequences and views, and provide structured guidance specifying boundaries, imaging features, and confounders. Experiments across 16 vision-language models reveal moderate temporal alignment but systematic failure on change direction recognition and volumetric quantification, while multi-view inputs improve spatial localization yet degrade temporal reasoning in compact architectures. Our benchmark provides a systematic framework for evaluating progression tracking, interval change localization, and temporal ordering, which are essential for clinical deployment. Code, evaluation splits, and the dataset are available at: https://github.com/wafaAlghallabi/Time-Aware-MRI.

</details>


### [70] [AmalthAI: An Open-Source Computer Vision Platform for Cultural Heritage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13343)
*Christos Chatzisavvas,Stelios Alvanos,Efstratios Politis,Panagiotis Rigas,Thomas Pappas,Ioannis Giannoukos,Nikolaos Mitianoudis,Agata Ulanowska,Katarzyna Żebrowska,Nazarij Buławka,Christina Margariti,George Pavlidis,Chairi Kiourt,Anestis Koutsoudis,Vassilis Katsouros,George Ioannakis*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍AmalthAI，一个面向文化遗产(CH)领域专家的开源计算机视觉平台，旨在弥合CV/ML技术与CH专家之间的技能鸿沟，使其无需机器学习背景即可独立训练、验证分类、分割和目标检测模型，并通过Grad-CAM和视觉语言模型提供可解释性，同时支持自托管部署以保护敏感的考古数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算机视觉和机器学习技术为文化遗产文物分析提供了新工具，但CV/ML流水线对CH领域专家而言基本不可用，因为他们缺乏配置、训练或评估模型所需的背景知识。这个技能鸿沟阻碍了CH专家独立应用这些先进技术。

Method: AmalthAI是一个开源CV平台，提供数据集管理、训练和推理接口，支持分类、分割和目标检测任务。使用Kubeflow和Katib处理可扩展训练和超参数搜索；Grad-CAM用于定位预测背后的图像区域；视觉语言模型为预测添加文本描述供专家审核。平台支持自托管部署以确保受版权保护的敏感考古数据不离开机构。

Result: 该平台在考古用例中进行了测试，使用定制的粘土织物印记数据集，CH专家成功训练并验证了分割和分类模型用于假设检验。平台代码已在GitHub上开源提供。

Conclusion: AmalthAI成功搭建了连接CH领域专家与先进CV/ML技术的桥梁，使非ML背景的考古专家能够独立完成从数据管理到模型训练验证的全流程，同时兼顾了可解释性和数据安全性的关键需求。

Abstract: Computer vision (CV) and machine learning (ML) offer new tools for cultural heritage (CH) artifact analysis, but the CV/ML pipeline remains largely inaccessible to CH domain experts, who lack the background to configure, train, or assess models. We present AmalthAI, an open-source CV platform that bridges this gap, enabling non-ML CH experts to independently produce and validate archaeologically meaningful findings. The interface covers dataset management, training, and inference for classification, segmentation, and object detection, with Kubeflow and Katib handling scalable training and hyperparameter search. Grad-CAM localizes the image region behind a prediction, and a vision-language model (VLM) adds a text description of it for expert review. Since archaeological data is often state-owned or rights-encumbered and cannot leave institutional custody, AmalthAI's self-hostable deployment ensures sensitive data is kept within premises. We test the platform on an archaeological use case built on a custom dataset of clay textile imprints, where CH experts trained and validated segmentation, and classification models for hypothesis testing. We provide the implementation code at https://github.com/TEXTaiLES/AmalthAI.

</details>


### [71] [Sign Language Video Synthesis via Loss-Guided Multi-Expert GANs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13368)
*Dingzhan Nong,Zhihao Ren,Ziqi Li,Tim Lo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种用于手语视频合成的损失引导多专家GAN框架，通过三个专门判别器（全局、手部、头部）分别指导生成器中对应专家分支关注不同视觉区域，并引入United Loss共识机制稳定多判别器训练（10%权重向集成均值正则化）。每个分支采用卷积-Transformer双路径设计及可学习的AdaptiveFeatureFusion模块。在156GB自定义数据集上，0.2B参数版本达到29.8 PSNR（1.5GB推理显存），1.3B参数版本达到30.7 PSNR（8GB推理显存），可部署于消费级硬件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为听力障碍人士提升沟通体验，需要高质量的手语视频合成技术。现有方法缺乏对手语关键区域（手部和头部）的专门建模，且多判别器GAN训练早期存在混沌不稳定的动力学问题。

Method: 采用损失引导的多专家GAN框架：1）三个专门判别器（全局、手部、头部）分别指导生成器对应专家分支；2）引入United Loss共识机制，以10%权重将每个判别器向集成均值正则化以稳定训练；3）每个分支采用卷积-Transformer双路径设计，配合可学习的AdaptiveFeatureFusion平衡稳定性与细节；4）生成器使用交替三模式训练调度（判别器、整体生成、分支专门生成）。

Result: 在156GB自定义数据集（过滤简单重复样本的测试集）上，0.2B参数变体达到29.8 PSNR、1.5GB推理显存；1.3B参数变体达到30.7 PSNR、8GB推理显存，可部署于消费级硬件。完整消融研究因单GPU训练周期需2-3个月仍在进行中。

Conclusion: 该框架实现了隐式特征专业化（无需显式多样性损失），并通过United Loss解决了多判别器系统的训练不稳定问题，在性能与推理效率之间取得良好平衡，已在2025年香港前沿技术峰会上展出。

Abstract: This preliminary technical report presents a framework for sign language video synthesis using a loss-guided multi-expert Generative Adversarial Network (GAN) to enhance communication for individuals with hearing impairments. Three specialized discriminators -- global, hand, and head -- each guide a corresponding expert branch in the generator toward a distinct visual region, enabling implicit feature specialization without explicit diversity losses. To stabilize this multi-discriminator system, whose early-phase training otherwise exhibits chaotic dynamics, we introduce a United Loss consensus mechanism that regularizes each discriminator toward the ensemble average at a 10% weight. Each branch further adopts a dual-pathway convolutional-transformer design with learnable AdaptiveFeatureFusion, balancing the stability of convolutions against the detail of windowed self-attention. The generator is trained using an alternating three-mode schedule (discriminator, holistic generation, branch-specialized generation). On a custom 156GB dataset with a filtered test set that removes easy and repetitive samples, our 0.2B-parameter variant achieves 29.8 PSNR and the 1.3B-parameter variant achieves 30.7 PSNR, with inference VRAM footprints of 1.5 GB and 8 GB respectively, enabling deployment on consumer-grade hardware. Full ablation studies remain ongoing due to the 2-3 month training cycle on a single GPU. The system was showcased at the 2025 Hong Kong Frontier Technology Summit.

</details>


### [72] [Reconstructing Historical Manuscripts through MSI: The Potential of Contrast in Assessing Image Quality and Legibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13381)
*Anna Breger*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了基于对比度的图像质量度量在评估多光谱成像重建的历史手稿图像中的适用性。通过两项公开数据集的实验，发现潜在对比度与专家评分相关性最高，而对比度噪声比与全参考质量度量一致性最强，对比度类度量整体优于通用图像质量度量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 历史手稿数字修复旨在提升可读性并保持文化遗产真实性，但评估修复后手稿质量具有挑战性：可读性主观性强且专家标注稀缺。需要寻找客观、可靠的质量评估指标。

Method: 使用公开数据集进行两项实验，对比基于对比度的图像质量度量（如潜在对比度、对比度噪声比）与通用图像质量度量，以专家评分和全参考图像质量度量作为参照标准，通过相关性分析评估各度量的适用性。

Result: 潜在对比度与专家评分相关性最高；对比度噪声比与全参考质量度量一致性最强；对比度类度量整体优于通用图像质量度量。

Conclusion: 基于对比度的图像质量度量可有效作为手稿可读性和重建质量的客观指标，为该领域缺乏专家标注的质量评估提供了可行性方案。

Abstract: Digital restoration of historical manuscript images aims to improve readability while preserving the authenticity of cultural heritage documents. However, evaluating quality of restored manuscripts remains challenging, where readability is often subjective and expert annotations are scarce. This study investigates the suitability of contrast-based image quality measures to assess quality and legibility of reconstructed manuscript images from multi-spectral imaging. Two experiments were conducted with publicly-available data sets, facilitating manual quality scores by experts and full-reference image quality measures as reference evaluations. The results show that potential contrast achieves the highest correlation with expert ratings, while contrast-to-noise ratio demonstrates the strongest agreement with full-reference quality measures. Overall, contrast-based measures consistently outperform general image quality measures, demonstrating their potential as objective indicators of manuscript legibility and reconstruction quality.

</details>


### [73] [When Is a Task Vector Enough? An Empirical Theory of Implicit Multimodal ICL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13385)
*Jiaqian Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出"选择-实现假说"(Selection-Realization Hypothesis)，将隐式多模态上下文学习(M-ICL)中的演示压缩统一理解为"查询从一组内部变化中选择，模型计算约束实现方式"的框架。通过受控多模态任务与反事实对比实验，作者发现静态任务向量是否有效取决于演示诱导的变化在查询间共享程度，而更复杂的干预方法（如查询条件变换、注意力路由）仅在显式M-ICL包含查询特定或分布式结构时才提供额外收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有隐式多模态上下文学习方法（静态任务向量、查询条件变换、注意力路由等）虽然目标一致，但在查询依赖方式和模型修改位置上差异显著，缺乏统一理论来解释何时需要何种复杂度的干预方法。

Method: 提出选择-实现假说，通过控制查询依赖程度变化的受控多模态任务（保持底层任务原语和提示格式不变），对比正确演示与匹配的反事实，测量显式M-ICL的结构并预测干预行为，最后在自然VQA基准上验证关系。

Result: 1) 静态任务向量的成功与演示诱导变化在查询间的共享程度密切相关；2) 当显式M-ICL包含查询特定或分布式结构时，更复杂的干预方法才有用武之地；3) 这些关系可迁移至自然VQA基准，支持无需测试性能的成本感知方法选择。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一个统一的经验理论，阐明何时演示可被压缩为任务向量、何时需要更表达力的干预机制，为实际应用中根据任务特性选择合适复杂度的M-ICL方法提供了指导原则。

Abstract: Implicit multimodal in-context learning compresses demonstrations into internal interventions, ranging from static task vectors to query-conditioned transformations and attention routing. Despite their common goal, these methods differ substantially in how the intervention depends on the query and where it modifies the model, leaving unclear which additional complexity is necessary for a given task. We propose the Selection--Realization Hypothesis. It views demonstrations as inducing a compact family of internal changes from which the query selects, while the model's computation constrains how the selected change can be implemented. We evaluate this account using controlled multimodal tasks in which query dependence varies without changing the underlying task primitives or prompt format. By contrasting correct demonstrations with matched counterfactuals, we measure the structure of explicit M-ICL and test whether it predicts intervention behavior. We find that the success of a static task vector is closely tied to how much of the demonstration-induced change is shared across queries. Additional intervention complexity becomes useful when explicit M-ICL contains query-specific or distributed structure that a local additive shift cannot recover. These relationships extend to natural VQA benchmarks and support cost-aware method selection without access to test performance. Our results provide a unified empirical theory of when demonstrations can be compressed into a task vector and when a more expressive intervention is warranted.

</details>


### [74] [Context-Matched Distillation: Teacher Causality for Autoregressive Video Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13391)
*Hmrishav Bandyopadhyay,Xuanchi Ren,Zijian Huang,Jay Zhangjie Wu,Tianshi Cao,Ruilong Li,Bryan Chu,Sanja Fidler,Yi-Zhe Song,Zian Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出上下文匹配蒸馏（CMD），一种因果视频分布匹配蒸馏框架，通过让教师模型仅基于因果可用信息（历史帧和控制信号）评估每个目标，解决现有DMD管道中双向教师与学生因果信息集错配的问题，并在长短视频基准上取得SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交互式自回归视频生成需要低延迟和精确的在线控制。现有的视频DMD蒸馏管道使用双向教师模型对整个视频片段打分，但学生是因果式逐步生成的，导致教师监督信号可能依赖学生生成时不可得的未来帧和控制信号，造成监督与因果信息集之间的错配。

Method: CMD包含三个核心组件：(1) 因果教师：将双向全片段打分替换为只能访问历史帧和控制的因果打分，且该因果教师同时初始化少步学生，保持教师训练、蒸馏和推理的因果一致性；(2) 前缀打分：在缓存的学生生成前缀下评估每个目标，使监督与学生实际发生的生成上下文对齐；(3) 前缀扰动：通过扰动训练早期不可靠的前缀来稳定训练，同时保持目标-上下文对齐。CMD自然地扩展到逐帧/逐块生成、长视频蒸馏和相机条件蒸馏。

Result: 实验表明CMD在短、长视频基准上的自回归方法中取得SOTA综合性能，并且对时变相机控制的遵循度显著提升。

Conclusion: 通过统一教师训练、学生蒸馏和推理三阶段的因果公式，CMD实现了教师监督与学生因果信息集的完全对齐，同时前缀打分和前缀扰动机制进一步提升了蒸馏稳定性，为交互式视频生成的高效低延迟部署提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Interactive autoregressive video generation demands both low-latency rollouts and precise online control. Few-step distillation accelerates generation by reducing denoising steps, while online control imposes a causal constraint: frames and blocks should depend on history and controls available during generation. Existing video distribution matching distillation (DMD) pipelines, however, often supervise causal few-step students using bidirectional teachers that score complete clips. The score for a target can therefore depend on future frames and controls that were unavailable when the student generated it, misaligning teacher supervision with the student's causal information set. We introduce Context-Matched Distillation (CMD), a causal DMD framework that aligns teacher supervision with the information available when each target is generated. CMD replaces bidirectional full-clip scoring with a causal teacher that evaluates each target without access to future frames or controls. The same causal teacher initializes the few-step student, establishing a consistent causal formulation across teacher training, student distillation, and inference. Beyond aligning the temporal information boundary, Prefix Scoring matches supervision to the student's realized rollout context by evaluating each target under the cached student-generated prefix that produced it. Prefix Corruption further stabilizes training by perturbing unreliable prefixes produced early in training while preserving this target-context alignment. With a simple causal formulation, CMD naturally extends to frame-wise and chunk-wise generation, long video distillation, and camera-conditioned distillation. Experiments demonstrate state-of-the-art aggregate performance among autoregressive methods on both short- and long-video benchmarks, together with substantially improved adherence to time-varying camera controls.

</details>


### [75] [StreamTTT: Reconciling Real-Time Perception and Long-Term Memory in Streaming VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13416)
*Joya Chen,Zeyun Zhong,Mike Zheng Shou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出StreamTTT，通过将长期历史写入在线更新的快速权重（fast weights）而非注意力上下文，使流式VLM在保持短滑动键值缓存的同时兼顾实时感知与长期记忆。在OVO-Bench上，StreamTTT-4B相比SimpleStream-4B在实时感知上提升1.4分、向后追溯上提升3.7分，并在StreamingBench的RTVU子集上与更大的SimpleStream-8B保持竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式视觉语言模型（VLM）在实时感知与长期记忆之间存在权衡：缩短上下文可以提升当前场景感知，但会损害长期回忆能力。现有方法难以同时兼顾这两者。

Method: StreamTTT将长期历史写入在线更新的快速权重中（位于注意力上下文之外），仅保留一个短滑动键值缓存用于近期证据，从而缓解注意力稀释问题。模型在离线长视频QA和新建的实时QA语料上联合训练。

Result: 在OVO-Bench上，StreamTTT-4B相比SimpleStream-4B在实时感知上提升1.4分，在向后追溯上提升3.7分；在StreamingBench的RTVU子集上与更大的SimpleStream-8B表现相当。

Conclusion: 将长期记忆外置于快速权重的方法能够有效调和实时感知与长期记忆之间的矛盾，为流式VLM提供了一种可行的解决方案，且代码将开源。

Abstract: Humans effortlessly perceive the present while remembering the past, yet streaming VLMs often trade off real-time perception against long-term memory. Prior work shows that shortening the context can sharpen current-scene perception at the expense of long-range recall. To reconcile these abilities, we introduce StreamTTT, which writes long-range history into online-updated fast weights outside the attention context. This leaves a short sliding key-value cache dedicated to recent evidence, mitigating attention dilution. We train StreamTTT jointly on offline long-video QA and a newly constructed real-time QA corpus. On OVO-Bench, StreamTTT-4B outperforms SimpleStream-4B by 1.4 points in real-time perception and 3.7 points in backward tracing. It also remains competitive with the larger SimpleStream-8B on the Real-Time Visual Understanding (RTVU) subset of StreamingBench. Our code will be released.

</details>


### [76] [Edit2TikZ: A Comprehensive and Challenging Benchmark for Scientific Figure Editing with TikZ](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13441)
*Zongyun Zhang,Jiacheng Ruan,Xian Gao,Ruizhu Zhou,Lingcheng Meng,Lining Hu,Ting Liu,Yuzhuo Fu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 Edit2TikZ，一个面向科学图表编辑的综合基准，包含 1,548 个高质量样本，支持文本和视觉定位请求以及多步编辑。通过对 14 个主流 MLLM 的评估发现现有系统在科学图表编辑上仍不可靠。作者构建混合训练集 TikZEditMix 并采用重建-再编辑的课程学习策略，将 Qwen3.5-4B 的编译成功率从 45.35% 提升至 83.40%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 TikZ 基准主要关注图表重建和生成，缺乏对指令引导的科学图表编辑任务的系统评估，而该任务需要模型同时恢复视觉结构、定位编辑请求、生成可编译代码并保持无关内容不变，具有很高的挑战性。

Method: 构建 Edit2TikZ 基准（1,548 个样本），结合真实世界和受控合成编辑案例，支持文本与视觉定位请求及多步编辑；设计人类对齐的评估框架衡量编辑完成度与无关内容保留度；构建 TikZEditMix 混合训练集，并采用"重建-再编辑"课程学习策略训练紧凑模型。

Result: 14 个主流 MLLM 中，专有模型平均编译成功率仅 75%，图表恢复和编辑正确性均有限；9B 以下紧凑模型在指令遵循和完整图表生成方面表现更差。通过所提训练方法，Qwen3.5-4B 编译成功率从 45.35% 提升至 83.40%，评估指标平均提升 18.7 分。

Conclusion: 科学图表代码编辑对当前 MLLM 仍是重大挑战，专有模型与紧凑模型均表现不足；通过混合训练集和课程学习可显著提升紧凑模型性能，验证了所提方法和基准的有效性。

Abstract: Although multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have shown substantial potential in visual understanding and graphic code generation, editing scientific figures through code presents a greater challenge: a model must jointly recover visual structure, ground the requested change, generate compilable code, and preserve all unrelated content. While existing TikZ benchmarks mainly focus on figure reconstruction and generation, few systematically evaluate instruction-guided scientific figure editing with compilable code. We introduce Edit2TikZ, a comprehensive benchmark for scientific figure editing tasks, featuring 1,548 diverse and high-quality samples. Edit2TikZ combines real-world and controlled synthetic edit cases, supports both textual and visual localization request, and contains multi-step editing, each with step-level annotations. We further construct a human-aligned evaluation framework to measure whether a requested edit is completed while irrelevant content is preserved. Utilizing Edit2TikZ, we evaluate 14 mainstream MLLMs and find that current systems remain unreliable: on average, proprietary models achieve a compilation success rate of merely 75% and remain limited in both figure restoration and edit correctness, while compact models below 9B struggle further with instruction following and complete figure generation. Therefore, we build a mixed training set TikZEditMix and adopt reconstruction-then-editing curriculum learning for compact models. On Qwen3.5-4B, this training improves the compilation success rate from 45.35% to 83.40% and yields an average improvement of 18.7 points across our proposed evaluation metrics. The code and data will be released at https://github.com/Solunny/Edit2TikZ.

</details>


### [77] [UniTexture: Cross-Task Universal Adversarial Textures for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13453)
*Yukun Dai,Mingzhe Dai,Tianshi Wang,Fengling Li,Jingjing Li,Lei Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出UniTexture，一种跨任务通用的对抗性纹理攻击方法，通过单个3D物体的纹理贴图同时诱导多任务视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型产生目标偏差的动作预测，揭示了多任务VLA模型中存在的共享跨任务脆弱性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对机器人策略的攻击通常针对单一任务或指令进行优化，多任务VLA模型在跨任务场景下的漏洞尚未被充分探索。作者希望揭示多任务VLA是否可以通过单一对抗性纹理被系统性地攻击。

Method: UniTexture通过可微分渲染器将策略动作输出的梯度反向传播到物体表面纹理参数，在任务、指令、状态和视角的分布上联合优化共享纹理，使用目标动作空间损失函数，将预测动作引导至攻击者定义的目标，而无需为每个任务单独优化纹理。

Result: 在OpenVLA和π0.5上评估，UniTexture将平均任务成功率从正常条件下的90.0%降至攻击下的48.4%，诱导目标对齐的动作偏移，并展现出无需重新优化的跨套件和跨模型迁移能力。

Conclusion: 研究发现多任务VLA存在可被单一对抗性表面纹理系统性利用的共享跨任务脆弱性，这对VLA模型的安全部署提出了重要警示。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have emerged as generalist robotic policies capable of following diverse language instructions and performing a wide range of manipulation tasks. However, their direct control over embodied agents also exposes them to adversarial interference that may cause unsafe physical behaviors. Existing attacks on robotic policies are typically optimized for a single task or instruction, leaving the cross-task vulnerabilities of multitask VLAs largely unexplored. We introduce UniTexture, a cross-task universal adversarial texture attack that uses a single textured 3D object to induce targeted deviations in VLA action predictions across multiple tasks. UniTexture backpropagates gradients from the policy's action outputs to surface texture parameters through a differentiable renderer. It jointly optimizes the shared texture over a distribution of tasks, instructions, states, and viewpoints using a targeted action-space objective, steering predicted actions toward attacker-defined targets without optimizing a separate texture for each task. We evaluate UniTexture on OpenVLA and $π_{0.5}$ across diverse manipulation tasks and multiple evaluation settings. UniTexture reduces the mean task success rate from 90.0% under benign conditions to 48.4% under attack, induces target-aligned action shifts, and further exhibits cross-suite and cross-model transfer without re-optimization. Together, these findings reveal shared cross-task vulnerabilities in multitask VLAs that can be systematically exploited through a single adversarial surface texture.

</details>


### [78] [Evaluation of Clinically Steerable Retinal Image Generation from Foundation Model Latent Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13455)
*Zuzanna A. Wakefield-Skórniewska,Bartłomiej W. Papież*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文评估了四种视网膜基础模型在表示分词器框架下的可控图像生成能力，发现这些模型生成的表示和图像能忠实继承表型信息，在源模型内评估时优于传统潜在扩散模型，但在真实图像分类器评估时优势消失，揭示了合成到真实的表示差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学基础模型能够学习临床有意义表型的潜在表示，但其支持可控图像生成的能力尚未被充分探索。作者希望验证基础模型的潜在空间是否可以作为可控视网膜合成的强大基础，并揭示其中存在的问题。

Method: 在表示分词器框架内评估四种视网膜基础模型，比较合成生成图像在源基础模型内评估与真实图像训练分类器评估时的表现差异，并与传统潜在扩散方法进行对比。

Result: 源模型内评估时，生成表示和图像能忠实保留表型信息，持续优于传统潜在扩散；但使用真实图像训练的 classifiers 评估时优势消失，暴露出此前未表征的合成到真实表示差距。

Conclusion: 基础模型潜在空间为可控视网膜合成提供了强大基础，但需要更好地将合成表示与真实图像分布对齐，以弥合合成到真实的表示差距。

Abstract: Medical foundation models learn latent representations of clinically meaningful phenotypes, yet their ability to support controllable image generation remains largely unexplored. We evaluate four retinal foundation models within the representation tokenizer framework and examine whether demographic and clinical information encoded in latent representations from foundation models is preserved during synthetic image generation. We show that generated representations and images faithfully inherit phenotype information when evaluated within their originating foundation models, consistently outperforming conventional latent diffusion on multiple downstream prediction tasks. However, these gains largely disappear when evaluated using classifiers trained on real images, revealing a previously uncharacterised synthetic-to-real representation gap. These findings demonstrate that foundation-model latent spaces provide a powerful substrate for controllable retinal synthesis while highlighting the need to better align synthetic representations with real-image distributions.

</details>


### [79] [Fine-Grained Action Recognition with Cross-Attentive Latent Sparse Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13458)
*Imtiaz Ul Hassan,Tasweer Ahmad,Nik Bessis,Ardhendu Behera*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 FineX，一种用于细粒度人体动作识别（FHAR）的多模态融合框架。它将细粒度线索分解为 RGB 外观、姿态热图几何和骨骼图拓扑三种表征，通过成对交叉注意力实现流间信息交换，并采用流级稀疏混合专家（MoE）进行条件专家精炼。在 Gym99、Gym288 和 Diving48 上取得 SOTA，尤其在长尾分布的 Gym288 上将平均类准确率提升 7.6 个百分点，且无需文本监督或大规模视觉-语言预训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 细粒度动作识别中，RGB 表征保留视觉上下文但抑制关节点几何信息，而骨骼表征编码运动学但丢弃密集空间细节。现有单一模态方法难以同时兼顾外观与几何信息，且长尾分布下类别不平衡问题进一步加剧了识别难度。

Method: FineX 包含三个核心组件：(1) 将细粒度线索因子化为 RGB 外观、姿态热图几何和骨骼图拓扑三种互补表征；(2) 采用成对交叉注意力实现对称的、保留各流信息的交互；(3) 引入流级稀疏混合专家网络，将每种表征路由到内容相关的共享专家子集，并用负载均衡目标进行正则化。

Result: FineX 在 Gym99、Gym288 和 Diving48 三个基准上均达到最先进水平。在长尾分布的 Gym288 上，平均类准确率从 68.6% 提升至 76.2%（+7.6 个百分点），且不依赖文本监督或大规模视觉-语言预训练。

Conclusion: 结构化视觉-姿态-图融合与条件专家精炼的结合能有效提升细粒度动作识别性能，尤其是在长尾分布场景下，证明了多模态结构融合的相对轻量级方案可以作为大规模预训练方法的有效替代。

Abstract: Fine-grained human action recognition (FHAR) must distinguish visually similar actions that differ mainly in body configuration, timing, or local appearance. RGB representations retain visual context but often suppress joint-level geometry, whereas skeleton representations encode kinematics but discard dense spatial detail. We introduce FineX, which factorizes fine-grained cues into RGB appearance, pose heatmap geometry, and skeletal-graph topology. Pairwise cross-attention enables symmetric, stream-preserving information exchange, followed by a streamwise latent sparse Mixture-of-Experts that routes each representation to a content-dependent subset of shared experts, regularized by a load-balancing objective. FineX achieves state-of-the-art results on Gym99, Gym288, and Diving48. On the long-tailed Gym288, it raises mean class accuracy from 68.6% to 76.2% (+7.6 points) without textual supervision or large-scale vision-language pre-training, demonstrating the benefit of structured visual-pose-graph fusion and conditional expert refinement for FHAR.

</details>


### [80] [SNM-VFI: Symmetric Nonlinear Motion-Guided Generative Video Frame Interpolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13460)
*Jisoo Jeong,Hong Cai,Jamie Menjay Lin,Hanno Ackermann,Hyeonjun Sim,Yinhao Zhu,Yunxiao Shi,Fatih Porikli*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SNM-VFI是一个无需训练的框架，结合预训练光流模型和视频扩散模型，通过对称非线性运动模型生成对应感知帧来引导扩散过程，实现运动可控的生成式视频帧插值，并在DAVIS、Sintel和KITTI基准上取得优异的感知质量和时间一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于扩散的VFI方法从随机噪声合成中间帧，难以保持密集运动对应关系，且缺乏对运动过程的显式控制。

Method: 1) 利用预训练光流模型构建多帧非线性流引导的中间帧和置信图；2) 将流引导帧编码为潜在先验，初始化并迭代引导预训练视频扩散模型；3) 用置信图融合结构可靠的流预测结果与扩散生成细节，处理遮挡和物体边界等不确定区域。

Result: 在DAVIS、Sintel和KITTI基准上，SNM-VFI实现了强感知质量、有竞争力的重建精度以及跨多样运动场景的稳健时间一致性。

Conclusion: SNM-VFI证明了将光流引导的结构先验与视频扩散模型的生成能力相结合，可以在无需训练的情况下实现高质量、运动可控的生成式视频帧插值。

Abstract: We propose Symmetric Nonlinear Motion-guided Generative Video Frame Interpolation (SNM-VFI), a training-free framework for motion-controllable generative video frame interpolation with pre-trained optical flow and video diffusion models. Unlike conventional diffusion-based VFI methods that synthesize intermediate frames from random noise, SNM-VFI guides the generative process with correspondence-aware frames produced by a symmetric nonlinear motion model. Specifically, we first utilize a pre-trained optical flow model to construct multi-frame nonlinear flow-based intermediate frames and confidence maps. These flow-guided frames are then encoded as latent priors to initialize and iteratively guide a pre-trained Video Diffusion model, enabling the diffusion model to preserve dense motion correspondence while improving perceptual realism. To further enhance output quality, we employ confidence maps to fuse structurally reliable flow-based predictions with diffusion-generated details in uncertain regions such as occlusions and object boundaries. Extensive evaluations on challenging benchmarks, including DAVIS, Sintel, and KITTI, demonstrate that SNM-VFI achieves strong perceptual quality, competitive reconstruction accuracy, and robust temporal coherence across diverse motion scenarios.

</details>


### [81] [MLLM-Routed Heterogeneous Ensembles for Robust Cross-Dataset Image Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13463)
*Daniel Perkins,John Squires,Janou Milligan,Chandra Raskoti,Linda Ungerboeck*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ARMDIL是一个利用多模态大语言模型（MLLM）作为自适应路由器，将图像动态分配到最合适的视觉骨干网络的集成框架，在多个跨域图像分类数据集上表现出与专用训练路由器相当的性能，同时具备更强的可适应性和可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代图像分类模型在单一任务特定数据集上表现优异，但难以跨域和跨难度泛化。作者旨在解决多域图像分类中的泛化问题，构建更可靠、更通用的视觉系统。

Method: ARMDIL采用集成方法，包含CNN（ResNets）、自监督表征学习模型（SSL）和视觉语言模型（VLMs）三类异构视觉骨干，在统一标签空间上训练，并由MLLM智能体动态为每张图像选择最合适的视觉骨干进行推理。

Result: 实验表明不同架构在不同视觉域上各有优劣，ARMDIL能有效平衡这些权衡，性能与专门的基于训练的路由器相当，且通过简单的提示修改即可整合新信息，并通过自然语言推理轨迹提升可解释性。

Conclusion: ARMDIL为跨数据集图像分类提供了新的思路，显著提升了系统的适应性和可解释性，为AI助手和自主机器人等通用视觉系统的开发铺平了道路。

Abstract: Modern image classification models excel when trained on single task-specific datasets but often struggle to generalize across domains and difficulty levels. We propose ARMDIL, an Adaptive Router for Multi-Domain Image classification with LLMs. ARMDIL is an ensemble that uses a multimodal large language model (MLLM) agent to dynamically route each image to the most suitable vision backbone. Our diverse ensemble employs convolutional neural networks (ResNets), self-supervised representation learners (SSL), and vision-language models (VLMs), each trained on a unified label space constructed from multiple image datasets with differing distributions and characteristics. Empirical evaluations illuminate the distinct capabilities and vulnerabilities of each architecture across disparate visual domains. Crucially, we show that ARMDIL effectively navigates these trade-offs, performing competitively with specialized training-based routers. Furthermore, it drastically improves adaptability by allowing new information to be integrated via simple prompt modifications, while enhancing interpretability through natural language reasoning traces. These advances in cross-dataset image classification pave the way for more reliable general-purpose vision systems such as AI assistants and autonomous robots.

</details>


### [82] [MapRoute++: Surrogate-Guided Semantic Routing for Visual Concept Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13478)
*Ashok Urlana,L. D. M. S. Sai Teja,Vivek Hruday Kavuri,Ponnurangam Kumaraguru*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对Genμ 2.0挑战赛任务3（视觉概念遗忘）的提交方案。该方法基于MapRoute框架，引入任务特定训练目标、更丰富的概念表示以及用于概念特定映射器选择的语义路由机制，在Stable Diffusion v1.4上的官方基准测试中，使用Erasing-Retention-Robustness (ERR)指标评估，在五个概念类别上平均比最先进基线提升12.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉概念遗忘（visual concept unlearning）是生成模型安全性的重要问题，需要在不破坏无关概念和语义相邻概念的前提下，有效移除特定概念。现有方法（如MapRoute）在概念移除的鲁棒性方面仍有不足。

Method: 在MapRoute基础上进行三点改进：(1) 引入任务特定训练目标（task-specific training objectives）；(2) 使用更丰富的概念表示（richer concept representations）；(3) 采用语义路由（semantic routing）实现概念特定映射器选择（concept-specific mapper selection）。

Result: 在官方基准测试中，使用ERR指标在Stable Diffusion v1.4上评估，该方法在五个概念类别上平均优于最先进基线12.1%，在概念移除方面取得了显著提升。

Conclusion: 该方法通过结合任务特定训练目标和语义路由机制，在保持无关概念和语义相邻概念的前提下实现了更鲁棒的概念移除，在视觉概念遗忘任务上达到了新的最先进水平。

Abstract: We present our submission to Task 3 of the Gen$μ$ 2.0 Challenge on visual concept unlearning. Building on MapRoute, we introduce task-specific training objectives, richer concept representations, and semantic routing for concept-specific mapper selection. Our approach improves robust concept removal while preserving unrelated and semantically adjacent concepts. On the official benchmark, evaluated using the Erasing-Retention-Robustness (ERR) metric on Stable Diffusion v1.4, our method outperforms the state-of-the-art baseline by 12.1\% on average across the five concept categories, achieving substantial gains.

</details>


### [83] [DreamX-Phi 1.0: Action-Conditioned Video World Model for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13489)
*DreamX Team,Rui Chen,Xiangxiang Chu,Geng Li,Jifan Li,Qingfeng Shi,Datao Tang,Jing Tang,Jun Wang,Pengfei Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DreamX-Phi 1.0 是一个面向机器人操作的动作条件视频世界模型，通过 PRoPE 风格几何编码、深度分支和 SAM3 掩码 + V-JEPA 教师保持物体一致性，并采用分布匹配蒸馏实现高效部署，在 WorldArena 2.0 挑战赛中取得 Track 1 第一名和 Track 2 第二名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频世界模型在机器人操作中虽然能生成逼真的视频，但仿真结果可能移动错误的手臂或丢失被操作物体，即"逼真不等于忠实"。此外，仅靠动作控制不足以完全约束场景几何和小型被操作物体的演化，需要确保预测结果尊重每个手臂的命令路径并保持物体一致性。

Method: 1) 将每只手臂的 SE(3) 变换通过 PRoPE 风格几何编码注入注意力机制，保持手臂身份和刚体运动结构；2) 添加轻量级深度分支以约束场景级几何；3) 使用 SAM3 掩码配合冻结的 V-JEPA 教师模型维持抓取过程中的物体一致性；4) 通过分布匹配蒸馏将多步生成器压缩为少步学生模型以提高部署效率。

Result: 在 WorldArena 2.0 挑战赛中，DreamX-Phi 1.0 在 Track 1 获得第一名，在 Track 2 获得第二名。

Conclusion: 该方法通过几何编码与物体一致性约束的结合，有效解决了视频世界模型预测忠实性问题，同时蒸馏策略保证了实际部署的可行性。模型和代码将公开发布。

Abstract: We present \textbf{DreamX-Phi 1.0}, an action-conditioned video world model for robotic manipulation that, given an observed frame, a language instruction, and a prescribed action sequence comprising end-effector poses and gripper states, predicts the resulting future observations. Yet realism alone does not guarantee faithfulness: a convincing rollout can still move the wrong arm or lose the manipulated object. To ensure the prediction respects each arm's commanded path, we inject per-arm $\mathrm{SE}(3)$ transformations into attention via \textbf{PRoPE-style geometric encoding}, preserving arm identity and rigid-motion structure. Action control alone does not fully constrain scene geometry or the evolution of small manipulated objects. We therefore add a lightweight \textbf{depth branch} for scene-level geometry and use \textbf{SAM3 masks} with a frozen \textbf{V-JEPA teacher} to maintain object consistency throughout grasping. We further distill the multi-step generator into a few-step student via distribution-matching distillation for efficient deployment. At the time of writing, \model{} achieves first place on Track~1 and second place on Track~2 of the WorldArena~2.0 Challenge. Our model and code will be publicly available.

</details>


### [84] [TraVEL: Trajectory-Guided Video Embedding Learning for Driving-Video Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13495)
*Yi-Chung Chen,Philip Jacobson,Tom Lampo,Yiren Lu,Jin Yao,David I. Inouye,Jing Gao,Danhua Guo,Burhan Yaman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TraVEL框架，利用自车轨迹相似度作为奖励信号，通过Group Relative Policy Optimization（GRPO）微调多模态嵌入模型，使模型在无需轨迹、规则或辅助感知管线的情况下，仅凭视频嵌入向量实现精细的驾驶事件检索（如左右转弯、加减速），显著提升纵向和横向运动相关检索的mAP。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模驾驶日志中高效检索相关片段对数据整理、模型开发和安全性分析至关重要。传统结构化检索依赖专家规则与多阶段感知管线，而通用多模态嵌入模型虽简便高效，但常依赖静态场景上下文捷径，难以区分运动中心型事件（如左转vs右转、加速vs减速）。因此需要一种既能保持嵌入检索高效性、又能捕捉细粒度运动语义的方法。

Method: 方法分两阶段：(1) 先用nuReasoning数据集上的配对片段与推理轨迹对Qwen3-VL-Embedding进行InfoNCE目标的微调(SFT)；(2) 提出TraVEL框架，将自车轨迹相似度作为奖励融入到Group Relative Policy Optimization中，作为特权训练信号引导模型学习运动感知的嵌入。推理时无需轨迹、专家规则或辅助感知输出，仅使用单向量视频嵌入进行检索。此外还构建了基于nuReasoning的驾驶视频检索基准。

Result: 实验表明TraVEL在多个模型规模上均提升运动中心检索性能：相比SFT，2B模型在纵向和横向mAP上分别提升9.8和4.7个点；8B模型分别提升7.2和1.5个点。

Conclusion: TraVEL成功将物理上可解释的轨迹监督与高效的基于嵌入的检索相结合，在保持推理高效性的同时显著提升了对驾驶视频中细粒度运动事件的理解与检索能力。

Abstract: Efficiently retrieving relevant clips from large-scale driving logs is essential for data curation, model development, and safety analysis. Structured and rule-based retrieval systems can explicitly target driving events, but typically require expert-defined rules, auxiliary data, and multi-stage perception pipelines. Multimodal embedding models offer a simpler and more efficient alternative by representing each video with a single searchable vector. However, general-purpose models often rely on shortcuts from static scene context and struggle to distinguish motion-centric events, such as turning left versus right or accelerating versus decelerating. In this work, we study how to adapt a general-purpose multimodal embedding model to driving-video retrieval. We first fine-tune Qwen3-VL-Embedding on paired clips and reasoning traces from nuReasoning using an InfoNCE objective. While this stage substantially improves overall retrieval, caption supervision alone remains insufficient for fine-grained motion understanding. We therefore introduce TraVEL (Trajectory-Guided Video Embedding Learning), a motion-aware fine-tuning framework that uses ego-trajectory similarity as a reward within Group Relative Policy Optimization. Trajectories serve only as privileged training supervision; retrieval still operates on single-vector video embeddings without ego poses, expert rules, or auxiliary perception outputs. We further construct a driving-video retrieval benchmark from nuReasoning. Experiments show that TraVEL improves motion-centric retrieval across model scales: relative to SFT, it raises longitudinal and lateral mAP by 9.8 and 4.7 points at 2B, with corresponding gains of 7.2 and 1.5 points at 8B. TraVEL thus combines physically grounded supervision with efficient embedding-based search.

</details>


### [85] [GS$^{2}$CI: Robust Gaussian Splatting For Snapshot Compressive Imaging via Large Vision Model Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13502)
*Yanming Yang,Chenxi Song,Ping Wang,Xin Yuan,Chi Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种从单一快照压缩成像（SCI）测量中重建高质量3D场景的框架，结合3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）和大型视觉基础模型的先验知识。方法包括测量推导的3D VFM初始化、SCI感知的高斯优化、辅助2D VFM在合成视点提供伪监督，以及专门的OSGR致密化策略来稳定优化过程。实验证明该方法在重建质量、视角鲁棒性和计算效率方面均达到最优综合性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SCI能在单次曝光中压缩时空信息，实现高速视频采集和多视角场景捕捉，但现有方法在3D场景重建中面临信息丢失、视角多样性有限，以及联合优化3D表示和相机位姿的计算负担等重大挑战。

Method: 1) 使用3DGS作为场景表示，结合测量推导的3D视觉基础模型初始化；2) 进行SCI感知的高斯优化；3) 在粗阶段收敛后，使用辅助2D视觉基础模型在合成视点提供伪视图监督用于局部分观精化；4) 提出OSGR（Opacity-Guided Splitting and Growth Regulation）致密化策略：利用局部不透明度统计增强分裂候选，通过平均不透明度调节抑制损失补偿性的不透明度膨胀，并用候选比率和高斯数量约束限制表示增长。

Result: 在多个基准上的大量实验表明，该方法实现了最强的综合性能，在重建质量和视角变化鲁棒性方面领先，同时具有竞争力的计算效率。

Conclusion: 通过融合3DGS、视觉基础模型先验和SCI感知的优化策略（包括OSGR致密化方案），该方法有效解决了SCI中3D重建的信息损失和优化不稳定问题，为单次SCI测量到高质量3D场景重建提供了新范式。

Abstract: Snapshot Compressive Imaging (SCI) offers an efficient solution for high-speed video acquisition and, under exposure-time camera--scene relative motion, multi-view scene capture by compressing temporal or spatial information into a single 2D measurement. While recent studies have explored SCI for 3D scene reconstruction, existing methods struggle with significant challenges due to information loss, limited viewpoint diversity, and the computational burden of jointly optimizing 3D representations and camera poses. In this work, we propose a novel framework that reconstructs high-quality 3D scenes from a single SCI measurement by leveraging 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) and the powerful priors of large-scale vision foundation models (VFMs). Our primary reconstruction combines measurement-derived 3D VFM initialization with SCI-aware Gaussian optimization. After coarse-stage convergence, an auxiliary 2D VFM provides pseudo-view supervision at synthesized viewpoints for local appearance refinement. To further address the instability caused by ambiguous SCI supervision during 3DGS optimization, we introduce Opacity-Guided Splitting and Growth Regulation (OSGR), an SCI-specific densification strategy that augments split candidates using local opacity statistics, discourages loss-compensating opacity inflation through mean-opacity regulation, and bounds representation growth with explicit candidate-ratio and Gaussian-count constraints. Extensive experiments across multiple benchmarks demonstrate that our method achieves the strongest overall performance, combining leading reconstruction quality and robustness to viewpoint variation with competitive computational efficiency.

</details>


### [86] [TabSOM: A tabular-to-image encoding method based on self-organizing maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13513)
*David Chushig-Muzo,María Ángeles Rodríguez de Cara,Eva Milara,Francisco J. Lara-Abelenda,Luis Zhinin-Vera,Diego H. Peluffo-Ordóñez*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TabSOM是一种基于自组织映射（SOM）的表格数据到图像编码方法，通过空间布局和特征关系图将表格数据转换为图像表示，在十二种现有方法中排名第一或第二，同时提供两种可解释性方法，弥合了性能与可解释性之间的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的表格转图像方法（如基于t-SNE、UMAP、PCA的映射）仅编码每个特征的边际值，丢弃了特征之间的关系信息。作者希望设计一种既能利用CNN和ViT高性能，又能保留特征间关系且具备可解释性的编码方法。

Method: TabSOM基于自组织映射（SOM）构建，包含两个关键组成部分：(i) 空间布局——每个输入特征通过无冲突的匈牙利分配，占据由组件平面导出的固定画布位置；(ii) 特征关系图——从SOM组件平面捕获成对特征关系。生成的图像堆叠两个多尺度节点通道：一个以固定尺度编码特征值，另一个将成对特征交互编码为相关特征间的空间连接。同时引入两种基于SOM的可解释性方法：原型启发的部分依赖图和类别分离重要性评分。

Result: 在公共二分类数据集上与十二种现有表格转图像方法进行基准对比，TabSOM在每个数据集上排名第一或第二，且在所有评估方法中方差最低。可解释性结果与随机森林、XGBoost和SHAP进行验证，类别分离评分在顶级特征上与已有基线合理一致，同时捕获了输入数据中的互补结构信息。

Conclusion: TabSOM为将深度学习架构应用于表格数据提供了一种有效且可解释的方法，弥合了该领域中性能与可解释性之间的差距。

Abstract: Tabular-to-image methods have emerged as novel approaches to leverage the high predictive performance of convolutional neural networks and vision transformers. They convert tabular data into image representations, mapping each feature at a fixed pixel location derived from a dimensionality-reduction method (e.g., t-SNE, UMAP, PCA). However, they encode only the marginal value of each feature and discard information about feature relationships. We propose TabSOM, a tabular-to-image encoding built on the Self-Organizing Map (SOM), which provides: (i) a spatial layout in which every input feature occupies a fixed canvas position derived from its component plane via collision-free Hungarian assignment; and (ii) a graph that captures pairwise feature relationships derived from the SOM component planes. The resulting image stacks two multi-scale node channels: one encodes feature values at fixed scales, while the other encodes pairwise feature interactions as spatial connections between related features. Two SOM-derived interpretability approaches are introduced: a prototype-inspired partial dependence plot and a class--separation importance score. Benchmarked against twelve existing tabular-to-image methods across public binary-classification datasets, TabSOM ranks first or second on every dataset and achieves the lowest variance of any method evaluated. Interpretability obtained with TabSOM was validated against Random Forest, XGBoost, and SHAP, the class-separation score shows reasonable agreement with established baselines on the top-ranked features while capturing complementary structural information from input data. These results demonstrate that TabSOM provides an effective and interpretable approach for applying deep learning architectures to tabular data, bridging the performance--interpretability gap in this domain.

</details>


### [87] [SCULPT: Subtractive Composition for 3D Part Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13541)
*Sikuang Li,Chen Yang,Jiemin Fang,Jiazhong Cen,Yuhe Wei,Jichen Pang,Wei Shen,Qi Tian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SCULPT框架通过"减法组合"（subtractive composition）实现部件感知的3D生成。它利用联合分割预测器迭代地从完整对象中逐步提取部件和剩余部分，每个预测器在结构化3D潜空间中对部件和余量执行耦合去噪过程，允许相邻支撑区域重叠而非强制不相交分割。生成在余量为空或达到安全上限时停止，部件数量可自适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有部件感知3D生成方法存在两类局限：基于分割的方法先完整生成形状再划分部件，导致部件边界在后；而加法合成方法虽暴露部件基数，但共享边界常出现间隙、相互穿透和材质不连续的问题。作者希望提出一种在原生生成循环内同时保证整体一致性和部件结构完整性的方法。

Method: SCULPT采用减法组合策略。在结构化3D潜空间中表示完整对象后，迭代调用联合分割预测器，同时生成一个提取部件和剩余对象。预测器执行耦合去噪过程，条件同时基于图像和当前3D状态，使部件和余量协同生成而非事后拼接。预测器在两者原生稀疏3D支撑的并集上处理，允许支撑重叠而非强加不相交体素分割。生成过程持续至余量支撑为空或达到安全上限。

Result: 在PartObjaverse上取得了最先进的几何质量，同时保持部件组装后完整对象重建质量。在四个数据集图像、一个文生图输入和一个真实照片上的实验展示了超越基准的细粒度纹理部件分解能力。

Conclusion: SCULPT通过减法组合和耦合去噪机制，在原生生成循环内实现部件感知的3D生成，避免了传统方法的拼接伪影，并能自适应对象复杂度地确定部件数量，在基准测试和实际场景中均表现优异。

Abstract: Part-aware 3D generation aims to create digital assets that are coherent as complete objects while exposing structural parts for editing, material assignment, animation, and reuse. Existing methods impose this structure outside the native generation loop: segmentation-based methods partition an already generated shape, while additive methods synthesize parts from predefined layouts, boxes, or tokens and then reconcile them into a whole. The former preserves the generated geometry but fixes the object before part boundaries are determined; the latter exposes part cardinality but often leaves shared boundaries vulnerable to gaps, interpenetrations, and material discontinuities. In this paper, we propose SCULPT, a framework that addresses these challenges through subtractive composition. Given a complete object represented in a structured 3D latent space, SCULPT iteratively applies a joint split predictor to generate one extracted part together with the remaining object. The predictor performs a coupled denoising process conditioned on both the image and the current 3D state, so the extracted part and updated remainder are generated together rather than reconciled after generation. The joint split predictor processes both outputs on the union of their native sparse 3D supports, allowing neighboring supports to overlap rather than imposing a disjoint voxel partition. The rollout ends when the remainder support becomes empty or reaches a fixed safety cap, allowing the number of generated parts to adapt to each object within that bound. Extensive experiments demonstrate state-of-the-art geometry on PartObjaverse while preserving strong complete-object reconstruction after part assembly. Results on four dataset images, one text-to-image-generated input, and one real-world photograph further show fine-grained textured part decomposition beyond the benchmark.

</details>


### [88] [Alaya-EVOKE: From Linear-Scaling Supervision to Endless World](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13546)
*Yuanyang Yin,Gongxuan Wang,Yifan Zhan,Chuanhao Li,Kaipeng Zhang,Feng Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Evoke通过外部化持久世界状态（camera-indexed world state bank）和重新设计的长视界教师模型，解决了交互式世界模型在持久记忆、响应式交互和长视界生成之间的冲突。教师模型采用稀疏注意力（chunk-wise分组、检索远帧、线性注意力全局状态）实现线性增长的记忆和计算开销，并通过30秒分布匹配目标将能力蒸馏到三步学生模型，在保持有界上下文的同时实现开放式的持续生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交互式世界模型需要同时满足持久记忆、低延迟交互和长视界生成三个要求，但传统方法在denoiser上下文或KV缓存中维护历史会带来不断增长的计算成本，迫使会话长度与记忆保真之间做出权衡；同时低延迟的少步生成能力受限于其教师模型的性能。

Method: (1) 外部化持久世界状态：将场景几何存储在camera-indexed的外部世界状态库中，仅检索与当前视图相关的信息，保持denoiser上下文有界；(2) 重新设计教师模型：采用稀疏注意力机制，结合chunk-wise分组、检索选定的远帧以及线性注意力全局状态，实现记忆和计算线性增长，支持长视界监督；(3) 30秒分布匹配目标：在自强迫rollout下应用，将长视界能力和响应式条件控制蒸馏到三步学生模型，学生模型不使用classifier-free guidance。

Result: 在单块H200上以384×640分辨率，每1.5秒的chunk生成仅需2.11秒。作为三步世界模型，Evoke在WBench基准上达到最先进性能，同时在VBench-Long和VBench-2.0上保持竞争力。

Conclusion: Evoke通过有界上下文和循环外部记忆实现了开放式、持续演化的生成，证明了外部化世界状态与长视界教师蒸馏相结合是一种有效的交互式世界建模范式，能够同时兼顾记忆持久性、交互响应性和长视界生成质量。

Abstract: Interactive world models must support persistent memory, responsive interaction, and long-horizon generation, yet these requirements place conflicting demands on the model. Maintaining history in the denoiser context or key-value cache incurs growing cost, forcing a trade-off between session length and retained memory, while low-latency interaction relies on few-step generation whose capabilities are bounded by its teacher. Evoke addresses both limitations by externalizing persistent world state and redesigning the teacher for long-horizon interactive generation. Scene geometry is maintained in an external, camera-indexed world state bank, from which only view-relevant information is retrieved, keeping the denoiser context bounded as the session grows. Rather than treating the teacher as a fixed generator, we design it for long-horizon supervision: its sparse attention combines chunk-wise grouping, retrieval of selected distant frames, and a linear-attention global state, yielding linear growth in memory and compute while enabling supervision over long horizons. Such supervision exposes content drift that stays locally plausible within short windows, while per-chunk conditioning enables prompt changes and event control throughout the sequence. A 30-second distribution-matching objective, applied under self-forced rollouts, transfers both capabilities to a three-step student that uses no classifier-free guidance, improving resistance to long-term drift while preserving responsive conditioning. With bounded context and recurrent external memory, Evoke supports open-ended, continuously evolving generation; on a single H200 at $384\times 640$, each $1.5\,\mathrm{s}$ chunk is generated in $2.11\,\mathrm{s}$. As a three-step world model, Evoke achieves state-of-the-art performance on WBench while remaining competitive on VBench-Long and VBench-2.0.

</details>


### [89] [PlayWorld: Benchmarking World Models with Agent Players over Long-Horizon Objectives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13552)
*Kaixin Ding,Xi Chen,Minghong Cai,Zhiyuan Xu,Yiyang Wang,Yuxiang Lu,Junyi Li,Shuyang Chen,Yuan Gao,Xin Tao,Pengfei Wan,Hengshuang Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PlayWorld基准，利用多模态Agent Players与视频世界模型交互，以完成长期目标的方式评估模型性能，覆盖171个场景和四个核心维度的评估，发现现有模型在长期交互目标上仍不可靠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频世界模型的比较评估存在困难：固定动作序列的评估方式无法公平比较不同模型，因为实现相同目标所需的动作序列在不同模型间差异很大。需要一个以目标为导向的交互式评估范式。

Method: 采用多模态Agent Players与世界模型交互，以完成指定长期目标；构建PlayWorld基准，包含171个场景和目标；从几何一致性、交互保真度、超出视野演化、洞察演化四个核心维度，以及视频质量和可控性等基础能力指标进行评估。

Result: 对九个最先进的视频世界模型进行实验评估，发现当前模型在长期交互目标上仍不可靠，尤其是在保持空间一致性和持久状态演化方面表现不足。

Conclusion: 现有的视频世界模型尚未达到可靠的长期交互式目标能力，需要进一步改进空间一致性和状态持久演化，PlayWorld为这一领域提供了标准化的评估框架。

Abstract: Video world models simulate future states conditioned on current observations and user actions. Recent systems have demonstrated impressive video consistency and action controllability over long sequences. However, fairly comparing these interactive models remains challenging. In practice, a human player typically evaluates a world model by pursuing long-horizon objectives through interaction. For example, a user may turn around 360 degrees to see whether the environment remains consistent, or walk into the water and inspect whether realistic water ripples are generated. The action sequence required to achieve the same objective may vary substantially between models, making fixed action-conditioned evaluation unsuitable for cross-model comparison. To address this, we employ multi-modal Agent Players to interact with world models toward specified long-horizon objectives. Building on this paradigm, we introduce PlayWorld, a benchmark providing 171 scenarios, each with a specified objective. To evaluate performance thoroughly, we assess models along four core dimensions: geometry consistency, interaction fidelity, out-of-sight evolution, and insight evolution. In addition, we incorporate basic ability metrics for video quality and controllability. Experiments across nine state-of-the-art world models reveal that current models remain unreliable on long-horizon interactive objectives, particularly in maintaining spatial consistency and persistent state evolution. Code and data are available at https://github.com/kxding/PlayWorld.

</details>


### [90] [V-RAE: Rethinking Video Latent Spaces for Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13556)
*Minghui Guo,Shengqiong Wu,Hao Fei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出V-RAE，一种视频表示自编码器，在冻结的视觉基础模型表示之上构建紧凑的生成式潜空间。它使用轻量级时间池化模块去除时间冗余并保留语义结构，视频解码器从压缩特征重建连续运动。实验表明，V-RAE在视频重建、语义探测和类条件生成方面均优于现有方法，支持视频重建、生成和预测建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频自编码器虽然架构演进显著，但其潜空间主要针对像素级重建进行优化，缺乏高层语义组织。然而，重建最优的潜空间并不一定适合生成建模。因此需要一种能同时兼顾重建质量与生成性能的视频潜表示方法。

Method: V-RAE在冻结的视觉基础模型表示之上构建潜空间：1) 采用轻量级时间池化模块去除时间冗余，同时保留语义结构；2) 视频解码器从压缩后的特征中重建连续运动；3) 评估四种代表性冻结编码器在视频重建、语义探测和类条件生成任务上的表现。另提出tFVD，一种时间一致性诊断指标。

Result: V-RAE在K600上达到2.13 rFVD，优于所有评估的大规模预训练视频VAE；潜表示保留了比传统视频tokenizer更多的语义信息；在匹配的生成设置下，最佳变体在UCF101和K600上分别达到117.86和19.16的gFVD，收敛速度最高提升6倍；还改进了Cityscapes上的未来视频预测；提出tFVD诊断指标，与时序生成质量相关性更可靠。

Conclusion: 实验表明，冻结的语义表示足以支持视频重建、生成和预测建模。重建质量本身不足以表征生成效用，tFVD可作为更可靠的时序一致性诊断指标。

Abstract: Latent video generation relies on autoencoders to define a compact space in which generative models operate. Although video autoencoder architectures have evolved substantially, their latent spaces are still optimized primarily for pixel-level reconstruction and provide limited high-level semantic organization. A reconstruction-optimal latent space, however, need not be well suited to generative modeling. We propose V-RAE, a video representation autoencoder that builds compact generative latents on top of frozen vision foundation model representations. A lightweight temporal pooling module removes temporal redundancy while preserving semantic structure, and a video decoder reconstructs continuous motion from the compressed features. We evaluate V-RAE with four representative frozen encoders on video reconstruction, semantic probing, and class-conditional generation. V-RAE achieves 2.13 rFVD on K600, outperforming all evaluated large-scale pretrained video VAEs. Its latents retain substantially more semantic information than conventional video tokenizer latents. Under matched generation settings, our best variant achieves gFVD scores of 117.86 and 19.16 on UCF101 and K600, respectively, while converging up to 6x faster}. We further show that reconstruction quality alone is insufficient to characterize generative utility and introduce tFVD, a temporal-coherence diagnostic that correlates more reliably with downstream generation quality. Beyond video generation, V-RAE also improves future video prediction on Cityscapes over the Wan 2.2 VAE latent space under matched prediction settings. Taken together, the experiments show that frozen semantic representations can support video reconstruction, generation, and predictive modeling. The project page: https://v-rae.github.io/.

</details>


### [91] [AutoDesign: Meta-Harness Optimization for Long-Horizon Agentic Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13560)
*Yaxin Luo,Haobin Jiang,Jialv Zou,Xu Huang,Wenhao Yan,Haodong Li,Zhengrong Yue,Jing Li,Xiaofu Chen,Xiaohan Zhao,Jiacheng Liu,Jiacheng Cui,Zhiqiang Shen,Xiaotong Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出AutoDesign框架，将多模态源到结构化的媒体产出的转换概念化为一个长时程的基于模型-支架系统的智能体过程。AutoDesign通过元支架优化器引导代码智能体根据回滚反馈递归改进支架系统，并在学术论文转海报生成任务上验证，在PosterBench主赛道达到78.32分，超越商业系统Claude Design 7.45分，且在完全自主的长时程循环中仅用40分钟、不足3美元即可达到会议级海报质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有媒体产出范式（如论文转海报）中的理想支架系统应当与人类设计先验对齐，并通过经验探索积累可复用经验实现递归自改进，但现有系统是静态的，缺乏这种自我改进能力。

Method: 提出AutoDesign框架：采用元支架优化器指导代码智能体，基于回滚反馈递归改进支架系统。引入PosterBench基准（含100篇论文的主赛道，跨5个学科，以及10篇论文的mini子集用于受控评估）。

Result: 在PosterBench主赛道获得78.32分最高分，超越Claude Design 7.45分。在7种受控代码智能体模型配置中，集成学习的DesignHarness一致提升性能，平均得分从54.99提升至67.39（+12.4%）。完全自主循环中40分钟执行253次工具调用和11次编辑回合，成本低于3美元。系统盲测人类研究显示AutoDesign获得最高人类偏好。

Conclusion: AutoDesign验证了通过元支架优化器驱动递归自改进的框架有效性，表明将媒体生成视为长时程智能体过程并让支架系统从经验中学习，能够在性能、成本和自动化程度上显著超越静态的现有商业系统。

Abstract: Transforming multimodal sources into condensed and structured media outputs can be fundamentally conceptualized as a long-horizon agentic process centered on a model-harness system. While an ideal harness system should align with human design priors and accumulate reusable experience through empirical exploration to drive recursive self-improvement, existing paradigms remain static and fall short of this capability. In this paper, we present AutoDesign, a framework that aligns with human design priors, where a meta-harness optimizer guides a code agent to recursively improve harness based on rollout feedback. To instantiate and evaluate this framework, we focus on the academic paper-to-poster generation task and introduce PosterBench, comprising a 100-paper Main Track spanning five disciplines and PosterBench-mini, a shared 10-paper subset for controlled evaluation. On the PosterBench Main Track, AutoDesign achieves the highest score of 78.32, surpassing the closed-source commercial system Claude Design by 7.45 points. Across seven controlled code-agent-model configurations, integrating the learned DesignHarness consistently improves performance, increasing the average PosterBench Score from 54.99 to 67.39 (+12.4%). In a fully autonomous long-horizon loop, it executes 253 tool calls and 11 editing turns within 40 minutes for under $3, reaching average conference-poster quality in human evaluation. A system-blind human study further demonstrates that AutoDesign achieves the highest human preference among evaluated systems.

</details>


<div id='cs.CL'></div>

# cs.CL [[Back]](#toc)

### [92] [LLMs Know the Constraint But Do Not Use It: Activation Bottlenecks in Pragmatic Constraint Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12321)
*Yubo Li,Ramayya Krishnan,Rema Padman*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出"条件性约束激活"框架，将LLM在面对隐性可行性约束时的失败区分为知识缺失（Knowledge）、对称编码（Symmetry）、决策路由（Routing）和可修复性（Repair）四个维度。通过对14个模型进行四重诊断，发现存在两种失败模式：激活修补可修复一种（+6.4 nats）而无法修复另一种（-0.07）。所有提示干预都通过单一中介路径（前提提及）放大保守偏差，无法达到修复区域。结论：隐性约束失败是路由问题而非知识问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究用聚合准确率评估LLM在显性表面线索与隐性可行性约束冲突时的表现，但准确率混淆了真正的约束推断与保守默认行为。作者希望明确区分模型是真正推断出了约束，还是仅仅通过保守策略回避问题，从而更精准地定位失败根源。

Method: 提出四重诊断框架（Knowledge、Symmetry、Routing、Repair）：1) 检验约束是否在模型内部被编码（Knowledge）；2) 检验约束编码是否在约束存在和不存在时对称出现（Symmetry）；3) 检验约束是否被路由到决策过程（Routing）；4) 检验是否可通过供体激活（donor activation）修复（Repair）。对14个模型进行诊断，并对2个开放权重模型进行探针和激活修补分析，同时评估多种提示干预策略。

Result: 发现两种不同的失败模式：探针能在两个开放权重模型上以超过88%的准确率解码约束，但激活修补只对一个模型有效（+6.4 nats），对另一个无效（-0.07 nats）。在缓解前沿上，所有提示干预都通过"前提提及"这一单一中介通路放大保守偏差，无法达到真正的修复效果。

Conclusion: 隐性约束失败的本质是路由问题而非知识问题——约束已被模型内部编码且对称表征，但未能可靠地路由到决策输出中。提示干预无法解决这一问题，因为它们只是借助前提提及放大了保守偏差，而非真正修复约束路由机制。

Abstract: When a salient surface cue competes with an implicit feasibility constraint, LLMs often fail -- but aggregate accuracy conflates genuine constraint inference with conservative defaulting. We formalize the distinction as conditional constraint activation: the constraint is internally encoded (Knowledge) symmetrically across constraint-present and -absent prompts (Symmetry), yet only sometimes routed into the decision (Routing) and repairable by a donor activation (Repair). A quartet diagnostic over 14 models reveals two failure modes; probes on two open weights decode the constraint above $88\%$, yet activation patching repairs one ($+6.4$ nats) and not the other ($-0.07$). On a mitigation frontier, no prompted intervention reaches the repair corner: all inflate conservative bias through a single mediation pathway -- prerequisite mention. Hidden-constraint failure is a routing problem, not a knowledge problem.

</details>


### [93] [HC-RAG: Evidence-Centric Retrieval-Augmented Generation over Heterogeneous Financial Filings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12335)
*Siyuan Chen,Huaye Tan,You Li,Jiajun Liang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 HC-RAG，一种面向证据中心金融问答的分层跨模态检索增强生成框架。它通过构建包含文档、章节、文本单元、表格单元和元数据节点的类型化金融证据图，结合查询意图（计算、趋势、事实、比较四种语义意图）进行文本与表格证据的联合检索和路由。同时引入 Multi-Doc-2025 基准（179 份 SEC 10-K 文件、2327 个专家验证 QA 对），实验表明 HC-RAG 在 DocFinQA 上比 RAPTOR 高 6.6 F1，在 Multi-Doc-2025 上比 GraphRAG 高 10.9 F1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 RAG 系统处理财报问答时存在三个主要缺陷：一是将长文件压平为无序块，忽略了金融报告的类型化结构；二是对年度报告中的制度章节（如公司、财年）关注不足；三是使用固定的文本-表格融合策略，不考虑查询意图的差异。这些限制导致在涉及多公司、多财年和跨文档证据聚合的场景下难以给出准确答案。

Method: HC-RAG 包含三部分核心设计：(1) 构建类型化金融证据图，节点包括文档、章节、文本单元、表格单元和元数据（公司、财年等），通过文档-章节-单元路径进行检索；(2) 在共享检索空间中对齐文本和表格证据，实现跨模态证据的联合定位；(3) 根据四种语义意图（计算、趋势、事实、比较）进行证据路由，自适配合适的文本-表格式融合策略。此外还构建了 Multi-Doc-2025 基准，包含意图、难度和结构证据属性标注。

Result: 在公开金融 QA 基准和 Multi-Doc-2025 上的实验表明：HC-RAG 显著提升答案质量和证据定位能力，尤其在长文档、表格相关和跨文档场景。HC-RAG 在 DocFinQA 上较 RAPTOR 提升 6.6 F1，在 Multi-Doc-2025 上较 GraphRAG 提升 10.9 F1。证据级分析和消融实验显示改进主要来自更准确的章节定位、表格锚定、跨文档证据聚合和意图感知的文本-表格路由。

Conclusion: HC-RAG 验证了将财报的结构化信息（类型化证据图）与查询意图感知的跨模态融合相结合的有效性，为金融领域证据驱动的 RAG 提供了一条新路径。Multi-Doc-2025 基准也为后续研究提供了更贴近真实场景的多文档、跨年份金融 QA 评估资源。

Abstract: Financial question answering over annual reports requires more than retrieving semantically similar passages. It often involves identifying relevant companies and fiscal years, locating standardized filing sections, collecting textual and tabular evidence, and checking answers against the original documents. Existing RAG systems, however, usually flatten long filings into unordered chunks, pay limited attention to the typed structure of financial reports, and use fixed text-table fusion strategies without considering query intent. To address these limitations, we propose \textbf{HC-RAG}, a hierarchical cross-modal retrieval-augmented generation framework for evidence-centric financial QA. HC-RAG organizes filings into a typed financial evidence graph with documents, sections, text units, table units, and metadata nodes. It retrieves evidence through document-section-unit paths, aligns textual and tabular evidence in a shared retrieval space, and routes evidence according to four semantic intents: calculation, trend, fact, and comparison. We further introduce \textbf{Multi-Doc-2025}, a benchmark containing 2,327 expert-verified QA pairs from 179 SEC 10-K filings of 87 S\&P 500 companies across fiscal years 2022--2024, with labels for intent, difficulty, and structural evidence attributes. Experiments on public financial QA benchmarks and Multi-Doc-2025 show that HC-RAG improves both answer quality and evidence localization, especially in long-document, table-related, and cross-document settings. HC-RAG outperforms RAPTOR by 6.6 F1 points on DocFinQA and GraphRAG by 10.9 F1 points on Multi-Doc-2025. Evidence-level analysis and ablation studies show that the improvements mainly come from more accurate section localization, table grounding, cross-document evidence aggregation, and intent-aware text-table routing.

</details>


### [94] [What Drives LLM Self-Reflection? A Controlled Ablation of Uncertainty Routing in Armed Conflict Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12322)
*Poli Nemkova,Haeshitha Indukuri*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过六条件消融实验，分离了LLM自我反思的四个组件（证据暴露、诊断脚手架、分类词汇、动作路由），发现只有"类型化动作路由"能显著提升推理性能（F1从0.296提升至0.379），而诊断问题和分类词汇均无显著增益。该机制在GPT-4o上得到跨骨干网络验证，增益集中在结构性新颖冲突场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自我反思被广泛认为能改善LLM推理，但具体是哪个组件驱动了性能提升一直未被理解。本文旨在通过受控消融实验，精确定位LLM自我反思中真正有效的机制组件。

Method: 设计了六条件消融实验，系统分离四个组件：证据暴露、诊断脚手架、分类词汇和动作路由。通过两对精确零结果（结构化诊断问题vs非结构化反思、完整分类词汇vs单一通用动作）逐步排除无效组件，采用bootstrap置信区间进行统计检验，并在GPT-4o上交叉验证机制。

Result: 类型化动作路由提供一致的方向性增益（F1=0.379 vs 0.296，ΔF1=+0.101），而诊断脚手架和分类词汇均无显著贡献（p=1.000和p=0.773）。增益集中在缅甸（0→0.353）和乌克兰（0.167→0.500）等结构性新颖冲突场景，GPT-4o验证结果一致（动作路由p=0.025显著，词汇p=0.773不显著）。

Conclusion: 类型化动作路由（而非诊断脚手架或分类词汇）是设计元认知LLM预测智能体的关键原则，建议在更大规模的冲突类型学数据上进行评估验证。

Abstract: Self-reflection is widely assumed to improve LLM reasoning, yet which component drives the gain remains poorly understood. We present a controlled six-condition ablation isolating four components of LLM self-reflection: evidence exposure, diagnostic scaffolding, taxonomy vocabulary, and action routing. Two precise null results converge on a single mechanism. First, structured diagnostic questions add no measurable value over unstructured reflection ($\text{F1} = 0.296$ vs $0.297$, $p = 1.000$, 95\% CI $[-0.041, +0.040]$). Second, presenting the full uncertainty taxonomy while collapsing the action space to a single generic action also adds no value ($Δ\text{F1} = +0.008$, overlapping 95\% CIs), ruling out taxonomy vocabulary as the mechanism. Typed action routing provides consistent directional gains ($\text{F1} = 0.379$ vs $0.296$); the conservative estimate controlling for taxonomy vocabulary is $Δ\text{F1} = +0.075$, and the overall gain over the single-shot baseline is significant by bootstrap CI ($Δ\text{F1} = +0.101$, 95\% CI $[+0.020, +0.185]$). The vocabulary-routing decomposition replicates on GPT-4o: taxonomy vocabulary adds no significant value over generic reflection ($p = 0.773$), while action routing provides significant gains ($p = 0.025$), confirming the mechanism holds across backbones. Gains concentrate on structurally novel conflicts: in Myanmar ($\text{F1}: 0.000 \rightarrow 0.353$) and Ukraine ($0.167 \rightarrow 0.500$), the vocabulary-only condition recovers no more than generic reflection while action routing breaks the degenerate prior. These findings identify typed action routing -- not diagnostic scaffolding or taxonomy vocabulary -- as a promising design principle for metacognitive LLM forecasting agents, while motivating larger-scale evaluation across conflict typologies.

</details>


### [95] [Why Do AI Agents Break Rules? How Framing, Context, and Social Signals Shape Compliance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12323)
*Mika Okamoto,Ansel Kaplan Erol,Kutluhan Erol*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发现"执法信息悖论"（惩罚规定反而使违法行为变成成本收益计算）在AI代理中系统性出现。研究将法律经济学的合规理论作为经验假说，评估十二种指令微调语言模型在企业采购聊天机器人场景中的合规行为，发现安全微调模型能广泛合规，而任务优化和代理型模型将监管信号视为优化参数，在所有模型中引入财务激励、管理要求、同伴结果或员工压力都会导致大规模合规失败，最终指出合规不能仅靠规则嵌入实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI安全评估只测试模型是否失败，却没有探究为什么失败。作者希望将法律经济学的合规理论（威慑理论、合法性理论、表达性法律理论）作为诊断工具和实证假说，深入理解不同模型类别为何在监管约束面前表现各异。

Method: 将合规理论视为实证假说（而非隐喻），在十二种指令微调语言模型上，设置企业采购聊天机器人场景进行受控评估。基于威慑、合法性和表达性法律三种理论预测不同模型类别的行为，系统操纵向量包括惩罚力度、命令措辞、财务激励、管理要求、同伴结果和员工压力等条件。

Result: 安全微调模型在广泛条件下维持合规；任务优化和代理型模型把监管信号当作优化参数，在低惩罚和非命令措辞等理论预测的条件下无法合规；所有模型在引入财务激励、管理要求、同伴结果或员工压力时产生大规模合规失败。这些失败模式是标准对齐基准无法捕捉的。

Conclusion: 合规无法仅靠规则嵌入实现。模型选择本身就是治理决策，基于基准的评估对合规敏感型部署是不够的。研究将合规理论从隐喻提升为经验假说，为AI治理提供了新的诊断框架。

Abstract: Specifying a penalty can paradoxically convert a legal obligation into a cost-benefit calculation that favors violation. We demonstrate that this enforcement information paradox systematically occurs in AI agents. While most AI safety evaluations test whether models fail, we investigate why, applying compliance theory from law and economics as a diagnostic tool. We treat compliance theories not as metaphors but as empirical hypotheses and show that each predicts the behavior of a distinct model class. We evaluate our hypotheses across twelve instruction-tuned language models operating as enterprise procurement chatbots. Drawing on theories of deterrence, legitimacy, and expressive law, we show that safety-fine-tuned models maintain compliance broadly, while task-optimized and agentic models treat regulatory signals as mere optimization parameters. These latter models fail to comply under conditions predicted by theory, such as low enforcement penalties and non-command phrasing. Across all models, introducing financial incentives, managerial demands, peer outcomes, or employee pressure produces large compliance failures. AI procurement agents systematically violate regulatory constraints to satisfy local user objectives in ways not captured by standard alignment benchmarks. Ultimately, compliance cannot be achieved by rule embedding alone; model selection is itself a governance decision, and benchmark-based evaluation is insufficient for compliance-sensitive deployments.

</details>


### [96] [On Measuring Semantic Preservation in Legal Ontology Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12326)
*Albert Sadowski,Jarosław A. Chudziak*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种评估本体学习（ontology learning）语义损失的新方法：通过比较LLM在源文档与转换后表示上的任务表现差异来量化语义损失。作者在法律关系合并协议分析领域进行了验证，测试了三种本体学习方法与六种语言模型的组合，发现存在系统性语义损失，且损失程度随推理复杂度和模型-方法配对而变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本体学习将非结构化文本转换为结构化表示以支持自动推理，但这一转换过程可能导致信息丢失。现有的评估方法仅关注结构正确性，无法检测语义是否在转换中丢失。因此需要一种能够量化语义保留程度的新型评估方法。

Method: 提出一种对比评估框架：让LLM分别在原始源文档和转换后的本体表示上执行相同任务，比较两者的性能差异，该差异即量化了语义损失。在合法合并协议分析场景中，将直接LLM应用与三种本体学习方法配对六种语言模型进行系统比较。

Result: 实验揭示了系统性的语义损失现象，且语义损失的程度因推理复杂度和模型-方法交互作用而存在显著差异。不同模型与方法配对时，语义损失变化幅度很大。

Conclusion: 主要贡献为：(1) 提出了一套评估本体学习中语义保留程度的评估框架；(2) 提供了经验证据表明语义损失随模型-方法配对剧烈变化，为法律知识系统中选择最优配置提供了指导。

Abstract: Ontology learning transforms unstructured text into structured representations for automated reasoning. Yet structuring information risks losing it, and current evaluation methodologies cannot detect such loss, focusing on structural correctness while failing to measure whether meaning survives transformation. We propose an evaluation methodology that addresses this: comparing LLM task performance on source documents against performance on transformed representations, with the difference quantifying semantic loss. We demonstrate this approach on legal merger agreement analysis, a domain chosen for its complex language and precise semantic requirements, comparing direct LLM application against three ontology learning methods across six language models. The results reveal systematic semantic loss with significant variation based on reasoning complexity and model-method interactions. Our contributions are: (1) an evaluation framework for measuring semantic preservation in ontology learning, and (2) empirical evidence that semantic loss varies dramatically with model-method pairing, providing guidance for selecting optimal configurations in legal knowledge systems.

</details>


### [97] [Comparative Analysis of Multilingual Pre-trained Models for Nepali Automatic Speech Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12327)
*Suman Paudel,Sarbin Sayami*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文首次在统一微调协议下，对六种预训练模型（涵盖CTC自监督、自回归编码器-解码器和混合Conformer-CTC架构）在尼泊尔语ASR任务上进行受控基准测试。结果显示Whisper-Large-v3-Turbo和IndicWav2Vec在WER上并列最佳（约14.8%），尽管两者在参数量和预训练数据量上差距巨大，表明语言家族邻近性可替代原始规模。CTC解码器比自回归Whisper快29倍且精度相当，而MMS-1B在域外数据上表现最稳健。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多语言预训练模型声称支持尼泊尔语，但缺乏在统一微调协议下进行系统比较的受控基准。已有研究未能在相同条件下公平对比不同架构（CTC vs. 自回归vs. 混合）的尼泊尔语ASR性能。

Method: 在OpenSLR SLR54尼泊尔语语料库（约165小时）上，使用相同的预处理、数据划分、优化器和匹配学习率调度，对六种预训练模型（XLSR-53、IndicWav2Vec、MMS-1B、Whisper-Medium、Whisper-Large-v3-Turbo、Conformer-Hi）进行微调，并在三个独立测试集上评估WER、CER和RTF。

Result: Whisper-Large-v3-Turbo（14.76% WER）与IndicWav2Vec（14.89% WER）并列最优，尽管存在9倍参数差距和40倍预训练数据差距。CTC解码器比自回归Whisper快最多29倍且精度相当。MMS-1B在FLEURS域外测试集上退化最小（+12.55个百分点）。

Conclusion: 语言家族邻近性在预训练中可替代模型规模实现领域内高精度；在延迟敏感应用中CTC架构更具实用优势；大规模多语言预训练提升域外鲁棒性而非峰值精度。该工作提供了首个标准化、多模型、效率感知的尼泊尔语ASR基准。

Abstract: Multilingual pretrained models nominally support Nepali, yet no controlled benchmark has compared them under a single fine-tuning protocol. We fine-tune six pretrained models (XLSR-53, IndicWav2Vec, MMS-1B, Whisper-Medium, Whisper-Large-v3-Turbo, and Conformer-Hi) spanning CTC self-supervised, autoregressive encoder-decoder, and hybrid Conformer-CTC architectures, on the OpenSLR SLR54 Nepali corpus (~165 hours) using identical preprocessing, splits, optimizer, and family-matched learning-rate schedules. We evaluate Word Error Rate (WER), Character Error Rate (CER), and Real-Time Factor (RTF) on three independent test sets (OpenSLR, FLEURS, Common Voice). Whisper-Large-v3-Turbo (14.76% WER) and IndicWav2Vec (14.89% WER) tie at the top despite a 9x parameter gap and 40x pretraining-data gap, providing direct empirical evidence that language-family proximity in pretraining can substitute for raw scale for in-domain Nepali. CTC decoders run up to 29x faster than autoregressive Whisper at the same accuracy, flipping the practical deployment preference toward CTC under any latency budget. Massively multilingual pretraining (MMS-1B) yields the smallest out-of-domain degradation on FLEURS (+12.55 pp), indicating that scale buys robustness rather than peak in-domain accuracy. The resulting benchmark provides the first standardized, multi-model, efficiency-aware reference numbers for Nepali ASR.

</details>


### [98] [LoRA-Diffusion: Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning via Low-Rank Trajectory Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12328)
*Iman Khazrak,Narges Nejad,Mohammadhossein Homaei,Mostafa M. Rezaee,Robert C. Green*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出LoRA-Diffusion，一种针对扩散式语言模型的参数高效微调方法。不同于传统LoRA对模型权重进行低秩分解，该方法对去噪轨迹本身施加低秩扰动，并引入轨迹级低秩适配器、跨扩散阶段的步进自适应秩分配以及组合式多任务学习。在SST-2、QNLI和MRPC基准上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LoRA等参数高效微调方法已成功应用于自回归大语言模型，但尚未被扩展到基于扩散的语言模型。扩散模型通过迭代去噪而非顺序词元预测生成文本，因此现有的权重级低秩分解方法无法直接适用，需要一种新的参数高效微调范式。

Method: 1) 将对模型权重的低秩分解改为对去噪轨迹的学习低秩扰动；2) 引入轨迹级低秩适配器，修改每个去噪步骤；3) 采用步进自适应秩分配机制，根据不同扩散阶段动态分配秩；4) 设计组合式多任务学习，允许在推理时无需重新训练即可合并任务特定模块。

Result: 在SST-2、QNLI和MRPC数据集上（五随机种子）报告词元级去噪验证准确率，LoRA-Diffusion在SST-2上取得最高平均性能，在QNLI和MRPC上表现强劲；联合多任务训练下取得最高词元级准确率；相比全量微调显著降低每任务存储开销。

Conclusion: LoRA-Diffusion首次将参数高效微调框架成功扩展到扩散语言模型，通过轨迹级低秩适配、步进自适应秩分配和组合式多任务学习，在降低存储成本的同时保持甚至提升任务性能，为扩散语言模型的适配开辟了新方向。

Abstract: Parameter-efficient fine-tuning methods such as LoRA have transformed the adaptation of large autoregressive language models, enabling task-specific customization with substantially fewer trainable parameters. However, these methods have not been successfully extended to diffusion-based language models, which generate text through iterative denoising rather than sequential token prediction. We propose LoRA-Diffusion, a parameter-efficient fine-tuning approach that applies low-rank decomposition to the denoising trajectory instead of model weights. Unlike weight-based LoRA, which modifies individual transformation matrices, our method learns low-rank perturbations to the entire diffusion path from noise to output. We introduce trajectory-level low-rank adapters that modify each denoising step, step-adaptive rank allocation across diffusion phases, and compositional multi-task learning that allows merging task-specific modules at inference without retraining. On SST-2, QNLI, and MRPC, we report token-level denoising validation accuracy over five random seeds. LoRA-Diffusion achieves the highest mean performance on SST-2 and strong performance on QNLI and MRPC. Joint multi-task training further shows that LoRA-Diffusion achieves the highest token-level accuracy among the evaluated methods. The approach reduces per-task storage compared with full fine-tuning and establishes a parameter-efficient fine-tuning framework for diffusion language models.

</details>


### [99] [AnchorSIPS: A Synthetic Dataset and Evaluation Resource for Evidence-Supported Psychosis-Risk Symptom Measurement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12329)
*Guilherme C. Oliveira,Stephanie Fong,Zimu Wang,Clarice Lee,Xiangyu Zhao,Duy Khoa Pham,Duong Nhu,Yiwen Jiang,Jiahe Liu,Zhongxing Xu,Dwarikanath Mahapatra,Dominic Dwyer,Zongyuan Ge*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出AnchorSIPS，一个包含1万条结构化精神病风险访谈的合成数据集，每条访谈具有基于转录文本的测量目标。通过"先规划后实现"流水线生成，确保标签和结构在生成前固定，避免多轮LLM对话的跨轮不一致性问题。该数据集旨在支持证据提取、转录文本接地测量和部分披露下的不确定性研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精神病风险评估的AI研究受到数据访问瓶颈的限制——真实的临床访谈因隐私、治理和同意约束难以共享，导致缺乏可用于训练和评估的优质数据。

Method: 采用"计划-实现"流水线：隐藏病例表描述患者临床状态，确定性规划器固定访谈结构，LLM仅实现患者话语并在验证和有界修复下进行。所有中间决策（症状认可、随访细节、类别判定、排除性检查）都锚定到支持的转录轮次。

Result: 在七个LLM基线中，模型能够恢复粗略决策（如最终APS诊断标签），但无法提取随访细节或引用支持的转录轮次，说明仅依赖最终标签的表现会高估访谈能力。

Conclusion: AnchorSIPS为精神病风险访谈研究提供了可共享的合成数据集，揭示了现有LLM在证据提取和转录接地测量方面的不足，为后续研究提供了基准。

Abstract: Progress on AI for psychosis-risk assessment is limited by a data-access bottleneck. Real clinical interviews are difficult to share because of privacy, governance, and consent constraints. We present AnchorSIPS, a synthetic dataset of 10K structured psychosis-risk interviews with transcript-grounded measurement targets. Each interview is modeled on Mini-SIPS, a clinician-administered psychosis-risk interview. It captures history, 24 symptom questions, follow-up evidence for items the patient affirms, decisions about delusion-like symptoms (unusual beliefs), hallucination-like symptoms (unusual perceptions), and disorganized communication, exclusion of clear psychotic-level symptoms ("frank psychosis"), and a final attenuated psychosis syndrome (APS) diagnosis, a high-risk state of milder or early psychotic symptoms. The APS diagnosis is not a standalone label. It depends on earlier endorsements, supporting follow-up details, symptom-class decisions, and the frank-psychosis check. Every intermediate decision is anchored to its supporting transcript turns. AnchorSIPS is generated by a plan-then-realize pipeline. A hidden case sheet specifies the patient's clinical state, a deterministic planner fixes the interview structure, and an LLM realizes only the patient utterances under validation and bounded repair. Fixing labels and structure before generation avoids the inter-turn inconsistencies typical of multi-turn LLM dialogue. Across seven LLM baselines, models recover coarse decisions but fail to extract follow-up details or cite supporting transcript turns, so final-label performance overstates interview competence. AnchorSIPS is intended for research on evidence extraction, transcript-grounded measurement, and uncertainty under partial disclosure.

</details>


### [100] [Reliability-Aware Sexism Detection: Combining DPO with Annotator Agreement and Token-Level Confidence Scoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12330)
*Hadi Mohammadi,Shihan Wang,Masoume M. Raeissi,Anastasia Giachanou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出RA-DPO（可靠性感知直接偏好优化），将标注者一致性、模型置信度和token级不确定性整合为单一可靠性分数，用于训练时选择高质量偏好对，并在推理时支持弃权（选择性预测），在EXIST 2023多语言性别歧视检测任务上以30%数据达到全量训练效果，选择性预测在50%覆盖度下达到96.2%准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 性别歧视检测存在主观性，但现有系统将多标注者标签降为单一多数决策并统一处理所有样本，忽视了标注者一致性和模型不确定性这两个信息丰富的信号。

Method: 提出RA-DPO框架，将标注者一致性、模型置信度和token级不确定性三个信号整合为统一可靠性分数，利用该分数在训练阶段选择高价值偏好对，并在推理阶段支持选择性弃权，实现覆盖率与准确率之间的权衡。

Result: 使用6,920条EXIST 2023多语言帖子测试，微调gpt-4o并验证Llama和Qwen两个3B开源模型。仅用前30%最可靠偏好对训练即可达到全量DPO性能；选择性预测在50%覆盖率下达到96.2%准确率（真实一致性设定）和88.7%（可部署的预测一致性设定），均超过85.3%的无一致性基线。

Conclusion: 考虑标注不确定性对主观分类任务的高效训练和可靠部署均有益，可靠性感知的选择性训练和推理弃权机制能有效降低训练成本并提升部署可靠性。

Abstract: The detection of online sexism remains an open problem. Sexism detection is inherently subjective, yet most existing systems reduce multi-annotator labels to a single majority decision and treat all instances uniformly. This ignores two informative signals: annotator agreement and model uncertainty. We propose RA-DPO (Reliability-Aware Direct Preference Optimization), which integrates annotator agreement, model confidence, and a token-level uncertainty signal into a single reliability score. RA-DPO uses this score to select high-value preference pairs during training and to support inference-time abstention, which allows the model to trade coverage for accuracy. We evaluate RA-DPO on 6,920 multilingual posts from EXIST 2023, fine-tune OpenAI gpt-4o base via DPO, and validate on two open-weight 3B models (Llama, Qwen). Results show that training on the top 30% most reliable pairs matches full-data DPO, which indicates that reliability-aware selection can reduce training cost without sacrificing performance. At inference, selective prediction reaches 96.2% accuracy at 50% coverage in the true-agreement setting and 88.7% in the deployable predicted-agreement setting, both exceeding the 85.3% no-agreement baseline. These results suggest that accounting for annotation uncertainty is beneficial for both efficient training and reliable deployment in subjective classification.

</details>


### [101] [Thought-Aware KV Cache Compaction for Reasoning via Adaptive Attention Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12331)
*Yang Liu,Bin Chong,Chongyang Zhang,Hao Zheng,Jiayu Liang,Xu Kefu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Thought-Aware Attention Matching (TAM)，利用CoT推理轨迹的层次结构来进行KV缓存压缩。通过思维分割、自适应预算分配和关键token保护三个机制，在相同内存占用下相比均匀压缩提升推理精度，可将峰值内存降低65%同时保持竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理语言模型生成的长CoT序列导致KV缓存线性增长，成为解码时的内存瓶颈。现有压缩方法将推理轨迹视为扁平token序列进行均匀压缩，忽略了CoT推理的层次结构——不同推理步骤的重要性差异巨大。

Method: TAM包含三个核心机制：(i) 思维分割——将轨迹分解为推理块；(ii) 自适应预算分配——根据每个片段的重要性和大小分配压缩预算；(iii) 关键token保护——保留高注意力权重的推理锚点。论文还证明了在凸误差模型下分配规则的最优性，以及顺序压缩下累积误差的有界性。

Result: 在AIME 2024和MATH-500上使用Qwen3-4B的实验中，TAM在相同内存占用下优于均匀压缩的准确性；通过周期性压缩可将峰值内存限制在3.1-3.2GB（降低65%），同时保持有竞争力的准确率。

Conclusion: 利用CoT推理的结构化特性进行差异化压缩是有效的，TAM证明了感知思维结构的压缩方法能够在显著降低内存的同时保持推理质量。

Abstract: Reasoning language models generate lengthy chain-of-thought (CoT) sequences whose key-value (KV) cache grows linearly and becomes a memory bottleneck during decoding. Existing compaction methods treat reasoning trajectories as flat token sequences and apply uniform compression, ignoring the hierarchical structure of CoT reasoning where different steps vary drastically in importance.
