TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于YOLO和CLIP的视觉-语言框架，用于从视频数据中分类未被感染和感染DENV2的蚊子飞行帧，实现98.54%的帧级准确率和99.91%的敏感性，通过时间聚合后达到完全的视频级性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从视频数据中检测蚊子感染相关的行为变化具有挑战性，因为蚊子体型小、运动快速且不规则，且受背景、光照和阴影等环境因素影响，导致可靠的特征提取困难。

Method: 首先使用YOLO将蚊子区域从背景中隔离出来，然后将视频帧中提取的视觉特征与具有生物学意义的文本提示在共享嵌入空间中对齐。多模态模型使用监督双向对比学习进行微调，并通过帧级图像-文本相似度分类进行评估。

Result: 该方法在帧级实现了98.54%的准确率和99.91%的敏感性；对帧级信息进行时间聚合后，模型达到了完整的视频级性能。消融实验表明，微调和基于CLIP的表示对于该领域至关重要，而文本分支主要提供语义图像-文本对齐而非比纯视觉模型更高的精度优势。

Conclusion: 视觉-语言模型可以为从视频数据中分析感染相关生物行为提供有用的框架。

Abstract: Detecting infection-related behavioral changes in mosquitoes from video data is challenging because mosquitoes are small, move rapidly and irregularly, and are affected by environmental factors such as background, lighting, and shadows, which can make reliable feature extraction difficult. In this study, a YOLO- and Contrastive Language-Image Pre-training (CLIP)-based vision-language framework is proposed to classify mosquito flight frames of uninfected and Dengue virus serotype 2 (DENV2)-infected mosquitoes. First, YOLO is used to isolate mosquito regions from the background. Then, visual features extracted from video frames are aligned with biologically meaningful textual prompts in a shared embedding space. The multimodal model was fine-tuned using supervised bidirectional contrastive learning and evaluated through frame-level image-text similarity-based classification. The results show that the proposed method achieved 98.54% accuracy and 99.91% sensitivity at the frame level. After temporal aggregation of frame-level information, the model achieved complete video-level performance. The ablation results showed that fine-tuning and CLIP-based representations were essential for this domain, while the textual branch provided semantic image-text alignment rather than an accuracy advantage over the vision-only model. These findings suggest that vision-language models can provide a useful framework for analyzing infection-related biological behaviors from video data.

</details>


### [208] [Spatial Memory Agent: Experience-Grounded Procedure Memory for Spatial Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12743)
*Haokai Zhang,Yuhang Ding,Yunshu Zhou,Xinze Du,Shengtao Zhang,Zhiyue Zhao,Yuling Xi,Hao Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Spatial Memory Agent (SMA)，一个无需参数更新的空间自进化框架。SMA在可验证的空间环境中，利用验证器引导的反思将经验蒸馏为可迁移的教训，并通过转移可靠性评分(TRS)进行校准与检索，在推理时指导冻结的VLM进行空间推理。在5个空间基准和4个基础VLM上取得了最佳结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有提升VLM空间推理能力的方法主要依赖两条路线：一是后训练方法（SFT、RL等），需要更新模型参数；二是agentic范式，依赖外部空间工具（深度估计、3D重建等）在推理时收集证据。本文探索第三条互补且少人研究的路线：冻结的VLM能否通过无需参数更新的自进化来提升空间推理能力，且不依赖推理时的外部专家工具。

Method: SMA是一个基于经验的应用时框架，包含三个关键组件：(1) 在可验证的空间环境中查询冻结VLM，获取预测答案和奖励，并使用验证器引导的反思将空间经验蒸馏为紧凑可迁移的教训；(2) 为每条教训分配Transfer Reliability Score (TRS)，初始为均匀分布，随后根据后续检索结果进行校准；(3) 在只读部署阶段，通过语义过滤和相似度-TRS结合的排序检索教训，引导冻结模型的推理。

Result: 在5个代表性空间基准和4个基础VLM上，SMA在每个基础模型块中取得了最高的宏平均准确率，在20项评估中的大多数中取得了最佳准确率，证明了该方法在不同冻结模型规模和环境下都具有有效性。

Conclusion: SMA建立了实用的无需参数更新的空间自进化路径，证明冻结VLM可以通过经验积累和可迁移教训的检索来提升空间推理能力，为空间智能体的发展提供了新的范式方向。

Abstract: Spatial intelligence is becoming a foundation for embodied agents, robotic planning, and multimodal assistants. To improve the spatial reasoning ability of VLM agents, existing work has mainly followed two lines. One line uses post-training methods, such as supervised fine-tuning and reinforcement learning. Another line adopts an agentic paradigm in which the model calls external spatial tools, such as depth estimation and 3D reconstruction tools, to gather intermediate spatial evidence. We study a complementary and underexplored route: Can a frozen VLM agent improve its spatial reasoning through \textbf{parameter-update-free self-evolution}, without depending on external expert spatial tools at inference time? We present \textbf{Spatial Memory Agent (SMA)}, an \textbf{experience-grounded runtime framework} that converts verified spatial experience into reusable transferable lessons. In a verifiable spatial environment, SMA queries the frozen VLM, obtains a predicted answer and reward, and uses \textbf{verifier-guided reflection} to distill compact transferable lessons from spatial experience. SMA further assigns each lesson a \textbf{Transfer Reliability Score (TRS)}, which is initialized uniformly and calibrated from later retrieval outcomes as visit evidence of future transfer reliability. During \textbf{read-only deployment}, SMA retrieves lessons by semantic filter and similarity-TRS combined ranking, allowing the retrieved memory to guide frozen model inference. Across five representative spatial benchmarks and four base VLMs, SMA achieves the highest macro average in every base-model block and the best accuracy among the evaluated methods in most of the 20 evaluations, establishing a practical parameter-update-free path for spatial self-evolution across the evaluated frozen model scales and environments.

</details>


### [209] [Correct Is Not Governed: Provenance Integrity in Agentic Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12761)
*Jesus Salas*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Matrix，一个确定性因果状态层，用于实现"受治理执行"（governed execution）——即决策、完成和变更响应均有可检查溯源支持的智能体工作流。在受控比较中，Matrix能保留治理证据、拒绝未支持的完成声明并限制恢复范围，但严格的完整性契约在跨角色场景中会过度阻塞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统智能体工作流评估仅关注是否达到正确结果，这不足以适配机构化场景。在机构环境中，正确行动可能依赖错误的权限来源、包含未经验证的完成声明，或工作因后续变更而过时。需要一种能记录溯源并支持审计的治理机制。

Method: Matrix是一个确定性因果状态层，具备三个核心能力：1）记录权威依赖和事实依赖；2）验证完成证据；3）选择性失效受影响的作业。通过受控比较实验评估治理路径与直接路径的差异，并设计角色分离的传输挑战测试契约的严格性。

Result: 受控比较中，受治理与直接工作流通常达到相同结果，但只有受治理路径能持续保留治理证据、拒绝未支持的关闭声明，并将恢复限制在依赖任务上。然而，在角色分离的传输挑战中，确定性强制执行的完整性契约严重过度阻塞了在作者上下文之外产生的合成数据包。

Conclusion: Matrix并非通用准确性增强器，其核心定位是机构完整性层，用于使智能体工作可审计、可独立验证。该工作揭示了治理评估应同时关注结果质量与过程可证明性的需求，也暴露了严格契约在跨上下文场景中的局限性。

Abstract: Agentic workflows are commonly evaluated by whether they reach the correct outcome. That is insufficient in institutional settings, where a correct action may rely on the wrong authority, an unsupported completion claim, or work made stale by a later change. We define governed execution as work whose decisions, completion, and response to change are supported by inspectable provenance. We present Matrix, a deterministic causal-state layer that records authority and fact dependencies, verifies completion evidence, and selectively invalidates affected work. Across controlled comparisons, governed and direct workflows often reached the same outcomes, but only the governed path consistently preserved governing evidence, refused unsupported closure, and limited recovery to dependent tasks. A role-separated transfer challenge then failed: a deterministically enforced completeness contract severely over-blocked synthetic packets produced outside its authoring context. These results do not establish Matrix as a general accuracy enhancer; they support its primary role as an institutional integrity layer for making agentic work auditable and independently verifiable.

</details>


### [210] [PROVE-RT: Generating Mechanized Theorem Prover Scripts for Real-Time Systems using LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12762)
*Sadat Shahriyar,Shareef Ahmed,Abdullah Al Arafat*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出PROVE-RT，一个基于大语言模型辅助的框架，用于自动生成PROSA/ROCQ脚本以实现实时系统可调度性分析的机械化验证。通过依赖感知的非正式草图、文档检索、分阶段骨架生成和证明补全，PROVE-RT在评估集上达到了44.7%的成功率，显著优于直接提示SOTA LLM（无法可靠生成有效机制化证明）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的可调度性分析测试往往通过手工证明开发，难以扩展、验证和维护。虽然PROSA/ROCQ提供了机械化验证的严谨替代方案，但手动构建这些证明需要大量领域专业知识和证明工程努力。LLM虽在众多任务中表现出色，但缺乏PROSA特定知识，无法正确使用其建模抽象和证明模式。

Method: PROVE-RT框架包含四个关键组件：(1) 依赖感知的非正式草图指导生成过程；(2) 从处理的PROSA文档中进行检索以获取相关领域知识；(3) 分阶段骨架生成逐步构建证明结构；(4) 证明补全阶段完善最终机制化脚本。同时从1,191篇实时系统论文构建了包含13,134个带依赖信息的非正式草图的机械化语料库。

Result: 在精心策划的评估集上，直接提示SOTA LLM无法可靠生成有效的PROSA机制化证明，而PROVE-RT达到了44.7%的成功率，证明了检索引导和分阶段LLM辅助能够改善PROSA/ROCQ中可调度性分析的自动化机制化。

Conclusion: 检索引导和分阶段LLM辅助策略可以显著提升自动化机制化的效果，为实时系统领域的大规模机械化验证提供了可行的技术路径。

Abstract: Schedulability analysis is essential for certifying real-time systems, but existing tests are often developed through pen-and-paper proofs that are difficult to scale, validate, and maintain. Mechanized verification in PROSA/ROCQ offers a rigorous alternative, yet manually constructing such proofs requires substantial domain expertise and proof-engineering effort. Recent successes of large language models (LLMs) across a wide range of tasks make them promising candidates for generating PROSA/ROCQ scripts for mechanized theorem provers. However, state-of-the-art LLMs often lack the PROSA-specific knowledge required to correctly use its modeling abstractions and proof patterns.
  This paper introduces PROVE-RT, an LLM-assisted framework for generating PROSA/ROCQ scripts to mechanize schedulability analyses in real-time systems literature. PROVE-RT guides generation through dependency-aware informal sketches, retrieval from processed PROSA documentation, staged skeleton generation, and proof completion. We construct a mechanization-oriented corpus from 1, 191 real-time systems papers, containing 13, 134 informal sketches with dependency information. On a curated evaluation set, direct prompting of state-of-the-art LLMs fails to reliably generate valid PROSA mechanizations, whereas PROVE-RT achieves a success rate of 44.7%. These results show that retrieval-guided and staged LLM assistance can improve automated mechanization of schedulability analysis in PROSA/ROCQ.

</details>


### [211] [ARAC: Benchmarking Auto-Research's Alignment and Completeness on End-to-End Researchs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12788)
*Jiale Cui,Yueyao Yuan,Kaixi Zhong,Xiaogang Xu,Jiafei Wu,Zhe Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了ARAC-Bench，一个用于评估Auto-Research系统研究过程对齐度与完整性的基准框架。该框架通过学术认知技能系统和三阶段能力诊断协议，将评分目标从匹配最终答案转变为复现高质量人类研究过程。在11个SOTA框架上的评估显示最佳对齐得分仅为67.9/100，与博士候选人排名相关性达0.8141，证实其可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Auto-Research技术的快速发展暴露出一个根本性的评估挑战：现有方法缺乏衡量研究轨迹与人类研究行为在对齐度、逻辑连贯性和演化完整性方面的度量标准。传统评估仅关注最终答案的匹配，无法反映研究过程的质量。

Method: ARAC-Bench框架包含两个协同组件：1) 学术认知技能系统，首次将隐式审稿人专业知识转化为按阶段校准的量化评分标准；2) 三阶段能力诊断协议，在严格模块化约束下将研究过程分解为提出方案(Proposal)、实验(Experiment)和综合(Synthesis)三个可追踪、相互独立的维度。

Result: 对11个SOTA框架的系统评估显示，最佳对齐得分仅为67.9/100，揭示了模拟严格人类方法论方面的显著差距。与博士候选人排名的验证显示出0.8141的强相关性，确认ARAC-Bench可靠地反映了研究者真正重视的维度。

Conclusion: ARAC-Bench不仅提供了细粒度的诊断工具，还提供了可扩展的奖励信号，用于训练下一代自主研究系统。

Abstract: The rapid advancement of Auto-Research has surfaced a fundamental evaluation challenge: how can we measure the alignment, logical coherence, and evolutionary completeness of its research trajectory with human research behavior? We propose Auto-Research's Alignment and Completeness, ARAC-Bench: a Researcher-Mimicking Evaluation framework that shifts the objective from matching final answers to reproducing high-quality human research processes. The framework operates through two synergistic components: the Academic Cognition Skills system, which is the first to transforms implicit reviewer expertise into stage-calibrated, quantifiable rubrics; and a three-stage capability diagnostic protocol, which decomposes the research process under strict modular constraints into three traceable, mutually independent dimensions: Proposal, Experiment, and Synthesis. Systematic evaluation of 11 SOTA frameworks yields a best alignment score of only 67.9 of 100, revealing a significant gap in simulating rigorous human methodology. Validation against Ph.D. Candidates rankings shows a strong correlation of 0.8141, confirming that ARAC-Bench reliably reflects the dimensions researchers truly value. ARAC-Bench provides not only a fine-grained diagnostic tool but also a scalable reward signal for training the next generation of autonomous research systems.

</details>


### [212] [CABS+: Efficient and Scalable Model Merging via Conflict-Aware Sparsification and Adaptive Weight Allocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12842)
*Yuchen Liu,Zongzhen Yang,Binhang Qi,Hailong Sun,Xiang Gao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CABS+，一种增强的模型合并方法。针对先前CABS方法中网格搜索导致指数级时间复杂度和优化目标被高性能任务主导的问题，CABS+引入自适应权重分配（AWA）实现无梯度搜索降低时间复杂度，并使用非对称适应度函数促进跨任务的综合性能提升。此外，提出相对协同分数（RSS）量化模型可合并性并指导模型选择。在27个数据集和5种模型（大语言、小规模语言和视觉模型）上的实验表明，相比AdaMerging和WUDIMerging，CABS+整体性能分别提升16.97%和12.93%，GPU内存使用不到AdaMerging的25%，合并时间比WUDIMerging快近4倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型合并是构建统一多任务模型的有前景范式，但参数冲突和任务间知识干扰会降低合并模型性能。已有方法CABS虽能减少参数干扰，但其网格搜索导致指数级时间复杂度，且优化目标易被高性能任务主导，导致整体性能次优。因此需要更高效且更均衡的模型合并方法。

Method: CABS+包含三个核心组件：(1) 自适应权重分配（AWA）通过无梯度搜索方案优化合并系数，降低时间复杂性；(2) 非对称适应度函数促进各任务间更全面的性能增益；(3) 相对协同分数（RSS）用于量化模型可合并性并指导模型选择。系统性地实证研究了影响模型合并性能的关键因素。

Result: 在27个数据集和5种模型（覆盖大语言、小规模语言和视觉模型）上的广泛实验验证了CABS+的有效性和效率。相比AdaMerging和WUDIMerging，CABS+整体性能分别提升16.97%和12.93%，在不同任务数量和模型架构下表现出更强的稳定性和鲁棒性。GPU内存使用不到AdaMerging的25%，合并时间比WUDIMerging快近4倍。

Conclusion: CABS+通过在先前CABS方法基础上引入自适应权重分配和非对称适应度函数，成功解决了时间复杂度和性能均衡性问题，同时提出RSS指标为模型可合并性提供了量化评估工具。实验证明CABS+在性能、稳定性、内存效率和速度方面均优于现有最先进的模型合并方法。

Abstract: Model merging has recently attracted significant attention as a promising paradigm for constructing unified multi-task models without requiring additional retraining. However, parameter conflicts and knowledge interference across tasks often degrade merged-model performance. Prior work introduced Conflict-Aware and Balanced Sparsification (CABS), which reduces parameter interference through structured pruning and sequential masking. However, CABS relies on grid search to determine scaling coefficients, resulting in exponential time complexity, while its optimization objective can be dominated by high-performance tasks, leading to suboptimal overall performance. To address these limitations, we extend CABS and propose CABS+. Specifically, Adaptive Weight Allocation (AWA) optimizes merging coefficients via a gradient-free search scheme to reduce time complexity, while an asymmetric fitness function promotes more comprehensive performance gains across tasks. Moreover, we conduct a systematic empirical study of key factors influencing model merging performance and propose Relative Synergy Score (RSS) to quantify model mergeability and guide model selection. We compare CABS+ with state-of-the-art model merging methods, including CABS, AdaMerging, and WUDIMerging, across 27 datasets and 5 models covering large language, small-scale language, and vision models. Extensive experiments verify the effectiveness and efficiency of CABS+. Compared with AdaMerging and WUDIMerging, CABS+ improves overall performance by 16.97% and 12.93%, respectively, exhibits stronger stability and robustness across varying task numbers and model architectures, uses less than 25% of the GPU memory required by AdaMerging, and achieves nearly a 4x speedup in merging time over WUDIMerging.

</details>


### [213] [Beyond Retrieval: Query-Conditioned Reuse of Long-Horizon Agent Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12847)
*Yifei Li,Heng Wang,Lingling Zhang,Muye Huang,Xinyu Zhang,Jiashuai Liu,Hang Yan,Rongman Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出检索后重用（post-retrieval reuse）是长时程轨迹记忆的关键瓶颈，并构建了固定检索、目标状态、模型、解码和工具预算的评估框架。作者提出查询条件重用（QCR）——一种简单的目标绑定笔记，记录可重用流程、绑定恢复、适用条件和验证要求。在WebArena、WorkArena和AppWorld的2,391个目标实例上，QCR平均成功率62.3%，比完整轨迹高10.7点，同时减少48.9%在线token。摘要重排序可为94.8%的目标选择可重用记忆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检索可以找到相关的过去轨迹，但当用户、实体、约束或环境状态变化后，智能体应该如何重用该轨迹并不明确。作者识别出这一"检索后重用"步骤是长时程轨迹记忆的独立瓶颈，现有方法忽视了对重用环节的系统性评估。

Method: 1) 构建评估框架：固定检索、目标状态、模型、解码和工具预算，仅变化提供给智能体的支持方式。2) 提出QCR（query-conditioned reuse）：一种目标绑定的简洁笔记格式，包含可重用流程、需要恢复的绑定、适用条件及验证要求。3) 使用摘要重排序选择可重用的记忆条目。

Result: 在2,391个目标实例上，QCR达到62.3%平均成功率，超过完整轨迹方法10.7个点，同时节省48.9%在线token。摘要重排序对94.8%的目标选出可重用记忆，端任务成功率与Oracle可重用选择器仅差1.8点。轨迹越长或源-目标绑定变化越大的场景，直接轨迹注入效用下降越明显，而目标绑定支持保持更大比例的收益。

Conclusion: 该框架将检索质量与"将检索经验转化为安全、有用的新任务支持"这一环节分离，验证了目标绑定（target-bound）重用假设的有效性。QCR旨在检验重用假设而非宣称普适最优的记忆格式。

Abstract: Retrieval can identify a past trajectory that may matter, yet it does not specify how an acting agent should use that trajectory after users, entities, constraints, or environment state have changed. We identify this post-retrieval reuse step as a distinct bottleneck for long-horizon trajectory memory and formulate an evaluation framework that holds candidate retrieval, target state, model, decoding, and tool budget fixed while varying the support delivered to the agent. We instantiate the framework with query-conditioned reuse (QCR), a deliberately simple target-bound note that records a reusable procedure, bindings to recover, applicability conditions, and verification requirements. QCR serves to test the reuse hypothesis rather than to claim a universally preferred memory format. Across 2,391 target instances in WebArena, WorkArena, and AppWorld, QCR reaches 62.3% average Success, 10.7 points above Full Trajectory, while using 48.9% fewer online tokens. Summary reranking selects a reusable memory for 94.8% of targets, placing end-task Success within 1.8 points of an oracle reusable selector. Analyses by trajectory length and source--target binding shift show that direct trajectory injection loses much of its utility as traces grow longer or source-specific values change, whereas target-bound support preserves a larger share of the measured gain. The resulting framework separates retrieval quality from the problem of turning retrieved experience into safe, useful support for a new task.

</details>


### [214] [Practice Makes Unsafe: Skill Misevolution in Self-Improving LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12851)
*Xutao Mao,Liangjie Zhao,Xiang Zheng,Cong Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SkillMisevo-Gym（生命周期感知的评测环境）和SkillMisevo-Bench（冻结基准），系统揭示LLM智能体自我改进过程中"技能进化"（skill evolution）导致的技能误进化（misevolution）安全风险，并引入SafeEvolve包装器来修复不安全内容并约束后续复用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自我改进的LLM智能体将成功轨迹转化为可复用的跨任务状态，但一次不安全的成功在触发输入消失后可能变成可复用策略，且技能进化以任务结果而非程序安全性为优化目标，导致受损经验可能引发技能误进化。现有基准只能衡量当前行为或静态工件，无法归因创作、检索与执行生命周期的风险。

Method: 提出SkillMisevo-Gym（跨agent框架版本化技能状态的周期感知评测环境）和SkillMisevo-Bench（从恶意暴露到延续任务的冻结设计，含概念对齐的良性任务与九项生命周期指标）。同时提出SafeEvolve包装器，修复不安全内容并约束后续复用。

Result: 在25种agent方法配置（每种覆盖525个任务、25个episode）中，21种进化配置创作了不安全工件，但只有15种导致新会话伤害。在暴露扫描中，3个恶意任务将延续任务ASR从16.0%提升至35.3%。SafeEvolve将不安全检索和新会话伤害分别降低26.7和17.3个百分点，而平均良性效用仅变化0.4个百分点。

Conclusion: 持续性适应安全必须同时约束"更新写入什么"和"未来执行者复用什么"两个方面。

Abstract: Self-improving LLM agents convert successful trajectories into persistent cross-task state. An unsafe success can thereby become reusable policy after its triggering input disappears. Skill evolution makes this failure measurable by distilling operational trajectories into executable, transferable, and inspectable procedures. Because evolution optimizes task outcomes rather than procedure safety, compromised experience can cause skill misevolution. Existing benchmarks measure current behavior or static artifacts but cannot attribute risk across authoring, retrieval, and later execution. To expose this lifecycle, we introduce SkillMisevo-Gym, a lifecycle-aware harness that versions skill state across agent frameworks, and SkillMisevo-Bench, a frozen design from malicious exposure to carryover tasks, with concept-aligned benign tasks and nine lifecycle metrics. We also introduce SafeEvolve, a wrapper that repairs unsafe content and governs subsequent reuse. Across 25 agent-method configurations, each covering 525 tasks in 25 episodes, all 21 evolved configurations author unsafe artifacts, while only fifteen lead to fresh-session harm. In the exposure sweep, three malicious tasks raise carryover ASR from 16.0% to 35.3%. Across representative skill evolution methods, SafeEvolve reduces unsafe retrieval and fresh-session harm by 26.7 and 17.3 percentage points, respectively, while mean benign utility changes by only 0.4 points. Together, persistent-adaptation safety must govern what updates write and what future executors reuse. Code is available at https://github.com/henrymao2004/misevolve.

</details>


### [215] [AI and Consumer Rights in India Working Paper](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12863)
*Omir Kumar,Sriya Sridhar,Vibhav Mithal,Balaraman Ravindran*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨印度2019年《消费者保护法》是否足以应对AI产品和服务造成的损害，以及是否能在AI价值链中合理分配责任。研究发现该法案定义看似技术中立、可能适用于AI相关事件，但在因果关系证明和多方责任分配上存在重大缺口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI系统在面向消费者的应用中激增，AI相关损害的问责问题仍未解决。需要检验现有法律框架（印度2019年消费者保护法）是否能充分覆盖AI产品缺陷导致的消费者损害，以及责任分配是否公平合理。

Method: 工作论文通过法律分析方式，审查印度消费者保护法对产品责任、损害和缺陷的定义是否适用于AI，分析因果关系的证明难度，以及评估AI价值链中数据提供者、模型开发者、部署者和用户等多方重叠责任与法案中制造商、销售商、服务提供者传统分类的匹配情况。

Result: 法案定义具有技术中立性，可能适用于个人伤害、心理损害、偏见输出和失控等AI事件；但存在重大缺口：AI缺陷源于设计选择而非离散缺陷，因果证明面临技术挑战；AI价值链中多方责任重叠无法映射到法案的传统角色分类；现有责任框架缺乏应对复杂多方AI损害的比例机制。

Conclusion: 虽然该法案可能覆盖AI主体，但执法需要在行业特定重叠方面做出澄清，当前框架尚不能充分、按比例地分配AI价值链中的责任。

Abstract: As AI systems proliferate in consumer facing applications, questions about liability for AI related harms remain unresolved. This working paper examines whether India's Consumer Protection Act, 2019, adequately addresses harm caused by defective AI products and services, and whether it proportionately allocates liability across the AI value chain.
  The Act's broad definitions of product liability, harm, and deficiency appear technology agnostic and potentially applicable to AI related incidents including personal injury, psychological harm, biased outputs, and loss of control. However, significant gaps remain. Proving causation between AI defects and consumer harm presents a technical challenge, as AI failures often stem from design choices rather than discrete defects. Additionally, the Act's framework assumes distinct roles for manufacturers, sellers, and service providers, yet the AI value chain involves overlapping responsibilities among data providers, model developers, deployers, and users that do not neatly map to these categories. Current liability frameworks lack proportionate mechanisms to effectively address complex, multistakeholder AI harms. While the Act may cover AI entities, enforcement requires clarification on sector specific overlaps.

</details>


### [216] [ReflectFact: Self-Reflective Agents for Improving Comprehension and Reasoning in Multi-Hop Fact Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12877)
*Runze Zhao,Zixin Tang,Xiaoshuai Hao,Leyuan Chang,Xiaopeng Fu,Boyu Qiao,Dongyang Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ReflectFact是一个自反思智能体框架，用于多跳事实核查。它通过显式推理路径规划、证据漂移验证和推理反思验证三个任务，解决了现有方法中智能体缺乏全局目标意识以及参数知识与证据冲突的问题，在HOVER和EX-FEVER数据集上分别比最强基线提升3.32%和2.78%，达到最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多跳事实核查对于对抗社交媒体错误信息至关重要但极具挑战性。现有基于多智能体协作的方法存在两个关键缺陷：(1)智能体在执行子任务时缺乏对全局验证目标的充分意识，导致推理偏离预期方向；(2)参数化知识与提供证据之间的冲突可能破坏基于证据的推理并导致错误判读。

Method: 提出ReflectFact自反思智能体框架，包含三个关键任务：(1)显式推理路径规划——通过解析隐式实体、将声明分解为子问题、整合已核验事实来构建基于证据的推理路径；(2)证据漂移验证——当基于证据的回答仅回显参数先验时，强制智能体引用支持性证据重新回答，校准证据偏差；(3)推理反思验证——从全局任务视角重新检查每个推理步骤，检测不一致后重新生成，纠正位置偏差和替换偏差等推理缺陷。最后聚合已验证的推理链得出可靠结论。

Result: 在HOVER和EX-FEVER两个数据集上的大量实验表明，ReflectFact有效修复了现有方法的理解和推理缺陷，在两个数据集上分别比最强基线提升3.32%和2.78%，达到最先进性能。

Conclusion: ReflectFact通过自反思机制成功解决了多智能体事实核查中的全局目标意识缺失和证据偏离问题，证实了基于证据的推理质量提升能够带来显著的多跳事实核查性能改善。

Abstract: Multi-hop fact verification, which verifies claims by reasoning over multiple pieces of evidence, is critical for combating misinformation on social media yet remains highly challenging. Recent methods primarily rely on multi-agent collaboration to decompose fact verification into specialized subtasks. However, these methods face two critical limitations: (1) agents may perform individual subtasks without sufficient awareness of the global verification objective, causing their reasoning to deviate from the intended direction; and (2) conflicts between parametric knowledge and the provided evidence may undermine evidence-grounded reasoning and lead to incorrect verdicts. To address these challenges, we propose ReflectFact, a novel self-reflective agent framework for multi-hop fact verification. ReflectFact introduces three key tasks. Explicit Reasoning Path Planning builds an evidence-grounded reasoning path by resolving implicit entities, decomposing the claim into sub-questions, and integrating the verified facts into a verdict. Evidence-Drift Verification makes the agent re-answer by quoting the supporting evidence when a grounded answer merely echoes its parametric prior, thereby calibrating evidence deviation to ensure grounded comprehension. Reasoning Reflection Verification re-examines each reasoning step and regenerates it once an inconsistency is detected, correcting reasoning flaws such as location bias and replacement bias through a global task perspective. Subsequently, the agent aggregates validated reasoning chains to yield reliable verdicts. Extensive experiments on HOVER and EX-FEVER demonstrate that ReflectFact effectively remedies the comprehension and reasoning defects of existing methods, achieving state-of-the-art performance and respectively outperforming the strongest baseline by 3.32\% and 2.78\% on the two datasets.

</details>


### [217] [Predictive Memory Localization: Forecasting Selective Intervention Paths from Internal Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12892)
*Jinhao Jing,Tian Zeyu,Lucas Qingyang Fang,Zhisheng Chen,Shuang Chen,Yuhao Luo,Qiannian Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出预测性记忆定位（PML），将测量-网格干预路径作为记忆定位的预测对象，通过强度分离的低剂量因果响应来分离随机校准的目标移动与语义邻居/能力损伤。在3,000条记录、9个数据集、14个领域的冻结研究中，PML在层7的RFM/AGOP方向达到13.1%目标命中率（超出随机3.6个百分点），并验证了低剂量响应在分离强度的可预测性，最终将记忆定位转化为可证伪的边际选择性预测与风险感知干预决策工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激活引导将局部化表征转化为控制方向，但仅靠局部化无法揭示方向是否具有选择性工作区间（即干预在特定强度下是否只影响目标而不损伤其他能力），因此需要一种方法将定位本身转变为可预测的、强度相关的选择性结果预测。

Method: PML将测量-网格干预路径视为记忆定位的预测对象；分离随机校准的目标移动与语义邻居损伤及能力损伤；将静态定位与监督几何方法通过强度分离的低剂量因果响应进行比较；使用记录配对bootstrap评估；提出预测器驱动的选择器（可输出系数或弃权），在保留记录上优化效用并减少损伤。

Result: 在层7，RFM/AGOP方向达到13.1% target-any和12.3% clean-any，超出随机基线3.6和3.4个百分点。在记录/数据集/领域分组分割下，|α|=0.1的低剂量响应是分离强度|α|∈{0.25,0.5}结果的最强信号。在保留记录上，预测器驱动的选择器优于训练调优的固定强度策略，并在三个残差范数匹配的基模型上达到0.801-0.828的记录保留macro AUROC。

Conclusion: PML将记忆定位从静态的局部化任务转化为可证伪的边际选择性预测，并提供了风险感知的干预决策框架，实现了选择性路径收益与低剂量因果响应的统一。

Abstract: Activation steering turns localized representations into control directions, but localization alone does not reveal whether a direction has a selective operating regime. We introduce Predictive Memory Localization (PML), which treats the measured-grid intervention path as the predictive object of memory localization. PML separates random-calibrated target movement from semantic-neighbor and capability damage, and compares static localization and supervised geometry with a strength-disjoint low-dose causal response. Our frozen study covers 3,000 records from nine datasets and fourteen domains, yielding 30,000 distinct record-direction-layer paths and 210,000 distinct path-strength evaluations. At layer 7, the geometry-derived RFM/AGOP direction reaches 13.1% target-any and 12.3% clean-any, exceeding random by 3.6 and 3.4 percentage points under a record-paired bootstrap. Across record-, dataset-, and domain-grouped splits, responses at $|α|=0.1$ are the strongest signal for outcomes at disjoint strengths $|α|\in\{0.25,0.5\}$. On held-out records, a predictor-driven selector chooses a coefficient or abstains, improves utility and reduces semantic-neighbor damage relative to a train-tuned fixed-strength policy, and avoids most evaluations in a dense scan. Across three residual-norm-matched base models, learned directions retain selective-path gains and low-dose responses yield 0.801-0.828 record-held-out macro AUROC. PML therefore turns memory localization into a falsifiable forecast of margin-level selective outcomes and a risk-aware intervention decision.

</details>


### [218] [Agent Behavioral Contracts II: Certifying Compositional Reliability Without Assuming Independence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12895)
*Varun Pratap Bhardwaj,Garima Singh,Arun Pratap Bhardwaj*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文测试多智能体系统中组件可靠性乘积的经典假设（条件独立性），发现同一模型的两个实例在双智能体交接任务中90%情况下同失败（phi=0.916）。作者证明正依赖使冗余被高估，并提出不依赖任何依赖结构假设的有限样本证书（线性规划方法），以及随时有效的配套证书。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体系统的组合可靠性通常通过乘以各组件可靠性来计算，这依赖于条件独立性假设。该假设被例行陈述但从未被测试。作者旨在测试这一假设，并提供不依赖该假设的可靠性证书。

Method: 1) 通过预注册的18,000个mission实验测试条件独立性假设。2) 用有限样本的线性规划方法，在Bonferroni-Clopper-Pearson盒内联合概率空间上求可靠性界。3) 提供随时有效（anytime-valid）的证书处理可选停止。4) 理论上证明拟合依赖模型的bootstrap界随n增长失去覆盖。

Result: 同一模型的两个实例共失败率90%（log OR 6.66，phi=0.916）。更换模型降低关联强度但更换供应商没有。从10个矩泛函增加到14个将识别区间收窄85.7%，认证下限从0.2455提升到0.4116。随时有效证书在可选停止下维持类型I错误率0.0471。

Conclusion: 条件独立性假设在实践中严重违反且错误方向不利于操作者（高估冗余）。不假设依赖结构的有限样本线性规划证书是可行的替代方案，它健全、对给定信息最优且随矩族单调改善。拟合依赖模型的方法反而更差，因为bootstrap压缩无法弥补O(1)的识别缺口。

Abstract: Compositional reliability bounds for multi-agent systems multiply component reliabilities, a step licensed by a conditional-independence assumption that is routinely stated and rarely tested. We test it. Two instances of one model, in a two-agent handoff, co-fail on 90.0% of the missions on which either fails (log OR 6.66, 95% CI [6.38, 7.00]; phi 0.916), in a preregistered evaluation of 18,000 missions scored by deterministic code with no LLM judge. Substituting a different model reduces the association in six of six contrasts; substituting a different vendor, model already different, does not -- a registered hypothesis reported as a null.
  The error is signed and runs against the operator: positive dependence inflates joint failure above the independence product, so redundancy is over-credited exactly when components share a model. The assumption-free alternative is often vacuous, and fitting a dependence model is worse: we prove a bootstrap bound on a fitted model's functional loses coverage of the truth as n grows, the identification gap being O(1) while the bootstrap haircut is O(n^{-1/2}). More data makes such a certificate worse, with no visible symptom.
  We give a finite-sample certificate assuming no dependence structure: a linear program over the joint, over a Bonferroni-Clopper-Pearson box around measured co-execution moments. It is sound, sharp for the information supplied, and monotone in the moment family. Enriching ten moment functionals to fourteen narrows the identified interval by 85.7% and lifts the certified floor from 0.2455 to 0.4116. A companion anytime-valid certificate holds type-I error at 0.0471 under optional stopping.
  Common dependence statistics are marginal-bounded and can reverse an apparent ordering of conditions when the compared agents fail at different rates. Contracts, scoring code, analysis scripts, and the preregistration are released.

</details>


### [219] [Polish Medical Visual Question Answering: Vision-Language Models Underutilize Visual Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12928)
*Jakub Pokrywka,Łukasz Grzybowski,Antoni Lasik,Marek Kubis,Jeremi Ignacy Kaczmarek,Wojciech Kusa*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个波兰语医学视觉问答（VQA）基准测试，基于波兰医师和牙医专科认证考试的图像包含问题构建，并评估了多种视觉语言模型。最佳模型在完整VQA集上仅达到79.0%准确率，只有GPT-5.6超越了人类参考水平。通过消融实验发现，模型从问题文本中获取的信息多于图像，在图像主导问题上表现更差，且仅凭答案选项就能获得高于随机水平的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学VQA基准大多面向英语，缺乏针对低资源语言（如波兰语）的医学VQA基准，无法评估多语言视觉语言模型在专业医学领域的实际表现。作者希望填补这一空白，系统评估模型在波兰语医学场景中的视觉理解和问答能力。

Method: 从波兰医师和牙医专科认证考试中筛选出包含图像的问题构建VQA基准，并设置纯文本QA对照组。评估了波兰语定向模型、通用开源模型和商用视觉语言模型。通过对比完整输入与去除图像、去除问题或两者都去除的配置来评估视觉接地能力，并按图像重要性对问题分类。

Result: 最佳模型在完整VQA集上准确率为79.0%；在具有候选答案的子集上，仅GPT-5.6超过人类参考水平，其他模型均不如人类。消融实验显示模型从问题文本获取的信息比从图像获取的更多，在图像主导问题上表现较差。模型仅凭答案选项即可获得高于随机的准确率。

Conclusion: 波兰语医学VQA仍是具有挑战性的任务，当前模型在视觉接地方面存在明显不足——过度依赖文本信息而未能充分利用图像内容。模型凭借答案选项就能获得非平凡性能，这提示基准测试中可能存在需要关注的偏差或捷径学习问题。

Abstract: We introduce a Polish-language medical visual question answering (VQA) benchmark, built from Polish Board Certification Examination questions for licensed physicians and dentists pursuing specialist certification. The benchmark comprises image-containing questions spanning diverse medical specialties and visual domains, together with a text-only question answering (QA) control set. We evaluate Polish-oriented, general-purpose open-weight, and commercial vision-language models. The task remains challenging: the best model achieves 79.0\% accuracy on the full VQA set, and only GPT-5.6 surpasses the approximate human reference on the subset with available candidate responses; all other evaluated models perform worse than humans. To assess visual grounding, we compare complete inputs with configurations omitting the image, the question, or both, and categorize questions by image importance. Models derive more useful information from the question text than from the image and perform worse on image-dominant questions. Across both QA and VQA, they nevertheless achieve above-chance accuracy from the answer choices alone, showing that non-trivial performance can persist even when key task components are missing.

</details>


### [220] [FlashDrive: Flash Vision-Language-Action Inference for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12932)
*Zekai Li,Yihao Liang,Hongfei Zhang,Jian Chen,Yesheng Liang,Zhijian Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FlashDrive是一个算法-系统协同设计框架，针对VLA（视觉-语言-动作）自动驾驶模型中四个级联的推理瓶颈（视觉编码、语言模型预填充、自回归推理token生成、流匹配去噪）同时进行优化。通过流式KV-cache复用、非自回归扩散草稿模型用于投机解码、自适应步长缓存，以及CUDA Graph编译和内核融合等系统级技术，FlashDrive将10B参数VLA模型的端到端延迟从717ms降至151ms（4.7倍加速），同时几乎不损失精度，使自动驾驶端到端推理接近实时部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型为自动驾驶带来端到端推理能力，但其计算成本过高，无法满足实时控制需求。核心挑战在于VLA推理不是一个单一瓶颈而是四个级联瓶颈的叠加：视觉编码浪费计算、语言模型预填充重复计算、推理token串行生成、流匹配去噪均匀计算。单独优化任何一个阶段都无法解决问题。

Method: FlashDrive采取算法-系统协同设计方法，针对四个瓶颈分别提出轻量级算法捷径：1）利用时间重叠实现跨帧流式KV-cache复用；2）利用驾驶域推理的低熵和块内强相关性，设计非自回归扩散草稿模型用于投机解码；3）利用速度场的结构特征（端点尖锐、中间平坦），实现自适应步长缓存以集中计算资源。系统层面叠加CUDA Graph编译和内核融合，形成复合加速效果。

Result: 在Alpamayo 1.5-10B模型（W4A8量化）上，FlashDrive将端到端延迟从717ms降至151ms（4.7倍加速），帧率从1.4Hz提升到6.6Hz（单GPU）。精度几乎不变：minADE6@6.4s仅偏移0.08m，minADE1有所改善，仿真中的闭环碰撞率和偏离道路率均得到改进。

Conclusion: 通过对四个级联瓶颈的全面、系统性优化，FlashDrive使10B参数推理型VLA模型在单GPU上达到6.6Hz的推理频率，大幅缩小了端到端自动驾驶与实时部署之间的差距，证明了算法与系统协同设计在VLA加速中的巨大潜力。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models promise to bring end-to-end reasoning to autonomous driving, but their computational cost remains far too high for real-time control. The core challenge is structural: VLA inference is not a single bottleneck but a cascade of four. Visual encoding wastes compute on overlapping video frames; language-model prefill recomputes context that could be carried over from the previous timestep; reasoning tokens are generated serially despite low entropy; and flow-matching denoising applies uniform compute to a non-uniform velocity field. Addressing any one stage in isolation leaves the others untouched. We propose FlashDrive, an algorithm-system co-design framework that targets all four stages simultaneously. Our key insight is that each bottleneck admits a distinct, lightweight algorithmic shortcut: temporal overlap enables streaming KV-cache reuse across frames; the low per-token entropy and strong intra-block correlations of driving-domain reasoning make a non-autoregressive diffusion drafter highly effective for speculative decoding; and the velocity field's structure---sharp at the endpoints, flat in the middle---permits adaptive step caching that concentrates compute where it matters. Layered on system-level CUDA Graph compilation and kernel fusion, these techniques compound. Applied to Alpamayo 1.5-10B with W4A8 quantization, FlashDrive reduces end-to-end latency from 717ms to 151ms (4.7x) while leaving accuracy essentially unchanged: minADE6@6.4s shifts by only 0.08m, minADE1 improves, and closed-loop collision and off-road rates improve in simulation. By raising a 10B-parameter reasoning VLA from 1.4~Hz to 6.6~Hz on a single GPU, FlashDrive moves end-to-end autonomous driving substantially closer to real-time deployment.

</details>


### [221] [Decomposition of Evidence, Contradiction, and Fragility in Perturbation Responses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12935)
*Lei You*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出DECAF方法，通过沿输入逐步揭示的路径追踪预测变化轨迹，将扰动响应分解为证据E、矛盾C和脆弱性F三个分量，保留了原始响应幅度（Abs = E + C + F），并在多个基准上验证其能比单纯响应幅度更好地解释模型行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统扰动方法通过测量预测变化的大小来解释模型决策，但响应幅度只告诉我们模型反应有多大，无法说明反应的含义——同样的幅度可能是支持最终的事实-反事实差异、反对它，或者仅出现在扰动路径中途而在终点消失。需要一个能区分这些情况的更细粒度框架。

Method: 引入DECAF（Decomposition of Evidence, Contradiction, And Fragility），在成对输入逐步揭示的过程中追踪对比的发展，利用最终对比来解释轨迹。将对齐、反对和端点为零的响应分别路由到证据E、矛盾C和脆弱性F三个分量，满足精确分解Abs = E + C + F，且在端点相对公理下唯一。

Result: 在72模型ImageNet-9审计中，最大DECAF分量在96.4%的案例中与独立观测的行为一致（相比仅用幅度的35.0%）；改变揭示路径使总响应增加近80%但证据几乎不变而脆弱性增长4倍以上；在FunnyBirds和ImageNet-1k上超越通用归因基线；在1B规模DINOv2上以4.75倍更低的墙钟时间和2.36倍更低的峰值内存匹配强梯度基线。

Conclusion: DECAF通过区分响应如何形成、反对还是最终消失，显著提升了扰动方法解释模型行为的能力，在准确性和计算效率上均有优势。

Abstract: Perturbation methods explain model decisions by measuring prediction changes under altered inputs, but response magnitude tells us only how much a model reacts, not what that reaction means. The same magnitude can support the final factual-counterfactual difference, oppose it, or arise strongly along the perturbation path yet vanish at the endpoint. We therefore track how the contrast develops as paired inputs are progressively revealed, using the final contrast to interpret the trajectory. We introduce DECAF (Decomposition of Evidence, Contradiction, And Fragility), which routes aligned, opposed, and endpoint-null responses into evidence E, contradiction C, and fragility F. The decomposition preserves ordinary magnitude exactly, Abs = E + C + F, and is unique under endpoint-relative axioms. Across controlled vision and tabular settings, the three components track independently measured behavior. In a 72-model ImageNet-9 audit, we compare cases with nearly identical response magnitude but different independently measured behaviors. The largest DECAF component agrees with an observed behavior in 96.4% of cases, compared with 35.0% for magnitude alone. Changing only the reveal path increases total response by nearly 80%, yet evidence barely changes while fragility grows by more than 4x. On FunnyBirds and ImageNet-1k, short forward-only DECAF trajectories outperform the tested general-purpose attribution baselines. On a 1B-scale DINOv2 model, a short trajectory matches a strong gradient-based baseline with 4.75x lower wall time and 2.36x lower peak memory.

</details>


### [222] [Moose: Latent concept learning with reasoning-shortcut awareness in $\mathcal{EL}^{++}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12961)
*Olga Mashkova,Asaad Mohammedsaleh,Fernando Zhapa-Camacho,Robert Hoehndorf*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Moose方法，将OWL 2 EL profile下的本体（TBox和ABox）编译为Sentential Decision Diagram (SDD)，作为可微加权模型计数层，用于神经符号学习，并首次在OWL EL设置下进行推理捷径分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OWL 2 EL profile被广泛应用于大型生产本体（如Gene Ontology和SNOMED CT），但现有的神经符号学习方法仅支持命题逻辑或Datalog，尚未在本体设置中研究推理捷径（reasoning-shortcut）问题。这限制了在真实大规模本体上进行可微学习的能力。

Method: Moose将EL++ TBox和有限ABox编译为SDD，SDD作为可微加权模型计数层。利用EL++在部分监督下表达力有限的特点，在声明的穷举族上添加profile之外的闭包子句以克服局限。方法被证明具有终止性、可靠性、完备性以及多项式中间规模，并在Lean中验证了证明。

Result: 在MNIST-with-ontology和Pizzaïolo基准上，Moose优于基于命题神经符号、模糊逻辑和本体嵌入的基线方法，并首次在OWL EL设置中给出了推理捷径分析。

Conclusion: Moose成功将EL++本体编译为SDD用于可微学习，在真实本体相关任务上超越现有基线，并为OWL EL领域的推理捷径研究开辟了新的方向。

Abstract: The OWL 2 EL profile is used in some of the largest production ontologies, including the Gene Ontology and SNOMED CT. Existing neuro-symbolic (NeSy) learning methods accept propositional theories or Datalog, and reasoning-shortcut (RS) awareness has not been investigated in ontology settings. We present Moose, a method that compiles an $\mathcal{EL}^{++}$ TBox and finite ABox to a Sentential Decision Diagram (SDD). The SDD acts as a differentiable weighted-model-counting layer, and we add closure clauses outside the $\mathcal{EL}^{++}$ profile on declared exhaustive families to overcome the limited expressivity of $\mathcal{EL}^{++}$ under partial supervision. We show termination, soundness, completeness, and polynomial intermediate sizes, and validate the proofs in Lean. We then define the first formal partial-supervision latent-concept-learning task over an OWL EL ontology, i.e., learning per-individual classifiers for latent concepts from observed ABox literals, and evaluate Moose on MNIST-with-ontology and Pizzaïolo. Moose improves over propositional-NeSy, fuzzy-logic, and ontology embedding baselines, and presents the first reasoning-shortcut analysis in an OWL EL setting.

</details>


### [223] [OGR-MARL: Option-Guided Residual Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning for Heterogeneous USV Cooperative Pursuit in Constrained Port Waterways](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12995)
*Mao Jiayang,Wang Lanfeng,Peng Zhao-Han*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出OGR-MARL框架，一种与具体MARL算法解耦的选项引导残差多智能体强化学习框架，用于异构USV在受限港口航道中的协同追捕。该框架集成共享追捕目标信念、角色条件选项目标、自适应规则惩罚和残差策略学习，使不同MARL算法能在规则引导行为之上学习修正动作。在抽象的虾峙门港口航道场景中，OGR-MASAC实例化实现了75.0%的捕获率，并在零样本迁移到真实地图场景中展现出良好的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构无人水面艇（USV）在受限港口航道中进行协同追捕时，需要同时满足导航、交通和角色等多重约束，而传统MARL算法从零开始在如此复杂受约束的环境中探索效率低下，难以保证规则合规性。

Method: 提出OGR-MARL框架，包含四个核心组件：共享追捕者信念（shared evader belief）、角色条件选项目标（role-conditioned option targets）、自适应规则惩罚（adaptive rule penalties）和残差策略学习（residual policy learning）。该框架与具体MARL算法解耦，可搭配MADDPG、MATD3、MAPPO、MASAC等连续控制骨干算法进行实例化。

Result: 在抽象虾峙门港口航道场景中，OGR-MASAC实例化达到75.0%的捕获率，实现了任务有效的规则合规性和最好的异构协调性能。无需重训练即可零样本迁移到QGIS/AIS信息构建的虾峙门地图，展现良好的泛化潜力。

Conclusion: OGR-MARL通过在规则引导行为之上学习残差修正策略，显著提升了异构USV在受限港口环境中的协同追捕效果，并且该框架具有良好的算法可扩展性和场景泛化能力。

Abstract: Heterogeneous USV cooperative pursuit in constrained port waterways requires evader interception under navigation, traffic, and role constraints. This paper proposes OGR-MARL, an option-guided residual multi-agent reinforcement learning framework that is decoupled from a specific MARL algorithm. OGR-MARL integrates shared evader belief, role-conditioned option targets, adaptive rule penalties, and residual policy learning, allowing different MARL algorithms to learn corrective actions on top of rule-guided behaviors rather than exploring constrained port environments from scratch. We instantiate OGR-MARL with representative continuous-control MARL backbones, including MADDPG, MATD3, MAPPO, and MASAC, yielding OGR-MADDPG, OGR-MATD3, OGR-MAPPO, and OGR-MASAC. Experiments in an abstract Xiazhimen port-waterway scenario show that the OGR-MASAC instantiation achieves a 75.0% capture rate, promising mission-effective rule compliance, and the best heterogeneous coordination among the tested methods. Without retraining, zero-shot transfer to a QGIS/AIS-informed Xiazhimen map achieves promising results, demonstrating the generalization potential of OGR-MARL in more complex port scenarios.

</details>


### [224] [Foundations of MT-PDCL: Measure-Theoretic Probabilistic Definite Clause Logic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13018)
*Costin Bădică,Amelia Bădică*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出测度论概率确定子句逻辑（MT-PDCL），一种将概率逻辑编程从有限离散域推广到连续可测空间的基础框架，通过连续分布语义和Lebesgue积分定义蕴含关系，用精确代数推理替代离散grounding的组合爆炸。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统概率逻辑编程依赖将逻辑程序grounding为离散命题表示，这限制了精确推理只能处理有限域和离散概率分布，无法原生支持连续可测空间上的随机变量。

Method: 在确定子句逻辑上引入测度论结构：对有界指标域上的随机变量显式定义，为解释空间配备标准Borel σ-代数；基于连续分布语义将概率规则建模为相互独立因果事件；通过精确Lebesgue积分定义声明式蕴含；引入连续即时后果算子统一连续先验分布的积分与精确连续观测的评估。

Result: 该方法以精确、代数且结构可微的推理取代离散grounding的组合瓶颈；虽将离散组合复杂性转化为几何维数灾难，但获得了连续概率模型的表达能力，同时保持了确定子句逻辑的纯声明式语法。

Conclusion: MT-PDCL在保持声明式逻辑语法不变的前提下，将概率逻辑编程扩展到连续可测空间，为连续概率推理提供了统一的理论基础框架。

Abstract: Standard probabilistic logic programming frameworks typically rely on grounding logic programs into discrete propositional representations. This operational requirement restricts exact inference to finite domains and discrete probability distributions. In this paper, we introduce Measure-Theoretic Probabilistic Definite Clause Logic (MT-PDCL), a generalized foundational framework that eliminates this finite-domain restriction. By explicitly defining stochastic variables over bounded index domains and equipping the interpretation space with standard Borel $σ$-algebras, MT-PDCL allows logical variables to operate natively over continuous measurable spaces. Building on Continuous Distribution Semantics, MT-PDCL models probabilistic rules as mutually independent causal events. However, rather than aggregating these derivations via finite boolean circuits, declarative entailment is formally defined through exact Lebesgue integration over the continuous measure space. We introduce a continuous immediate consequence operator that unifies the integration of continuous prior distributions with the evaluation of exact continuous observations. We demonstrate that this approach replaces the combinatorial bottleneck of discrete grounding with exact, algebraic, and structurally differentiable inference. While this transition trades discrete combinatorics for the geometric curse of dimensionality, it achieves the expressive power of continuous probabilistic models while preserving the pure declarative syntax of definite clause logic.

</details>


### [225] [From Local Mismatch to Global Impact: Optimizing Cache Reuse Policy for Efficient Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13043)
*Xichen Ye,Yifan Wu,Zhikang Xie,Xiangyu Yue,Cheng Jin,Weizhong Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Global-Impact Cache (GCache)，一种基于全局影响感知的扩散模型缓存加速方法，通过双级优化框架解决现有缓存策略与生成质量不对齐的问题，在保持加速的同时提升生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型在视觉生成中性能卓越但推理开销巨大。现有缓存加速策略依赖局部相似性启发式，与最终生成质量严重不对齐，原因在于去噪轨迹上误差的非均匀传播和累积。

Method: 1) 建立误差传播上界的严格理论刻画；2) 由于该上界对复杂非凸扩散模型过于保守，用Bernstein形式重新参数化传播指数；3) 将缓存策略搜索重构为双层优化问题——内层目标寻找最优复用策略，外层目标将误差加权函数与生成质量损失对齐。

Result: 在视频和图像生成任务上均优于现有缓存策略。在Wan2.1视频扩散模型上，保持2.17倍加速的同时显著提升生成质量，LPIPS从0.1095降至0.0316。

Conclusion: GCache框架有效调和了理论严谨性与经验性能，学会将计算优先分配给对视觉保真度影响最大的位置，是一种更优的缓存策略设计范式。

Abstract: Diffusion models have achieved dominant performance in visual generation but suffer from substantial inference overhead. While cache-based acceleration has emerged as a promising solution, existing policies rely on local similarity heuristics, which we identify as being significantly misaligned with final generation quality. This discrepancy stems from the non-uniform propagation and accumulation of errors along the denoising trajectory. To address this, we propose Global-Impact Cache (GCache). We first establish a rigorous theoretical characterization of the error propagation upper bound. Recognizing that this bound can be overly conservative for complex, highly non-convex diffusion models, we further reparameterize the propagation exponent with a Bernstein form and reformulate cache policy search as a bilevel optimization problem. In detail, GCache identifies an optimal reuse policy in the inner objective while aligning the error-weighting function with generation quality loss in the outer objective. This framework effectively reconciles theoretical rigor with empirical performance, learning to prioritize computation where it most impacts visual fidelity. Extensive experiments demonstrate that GCache consistently outperforms prior caching strategies on both video and image generation. Notably, on the state-of-the-art Wan2.1 video diffusion model, GCache maintains a 2.17x speedup while significantly enhancing generation quality, reducing LPIPS from 0.1095 to 0.0316.

</details>


### [226] [BoardroomAI: Dependency-Aware Human-Steerable Multi-Agent Deliberation through Evolving Decision Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13046)
*Sanjeev Manivannan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: BoardroomAI提出一种将人类作为持续参与者（而非一次性输入者）的多智能体决策系统，通过类型化决策图、干预编译器、依赖感知传播和评估框架四大组件，实现人机共存的动态决策过程。实验表明其干预传播高效（仅检查14.59%节点），选择性修复有效，但存在上下文不足导致无法合成决策的问题，故提出"决策充分上下文闭合"概念。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于转录的多智能体系统中，人类仅提供初始问题后便退出，无法应对组织决策中证据、约束和人类优先级持续演变的现实需求。作者希望让人类成为决策全过程中的持续参与者，能够随时介入以挑战假设、修改约束、改变优先级、引入证据或重定向决策流程。

Method: 提出四个组件：(i)类型化决策图，表示证据、假设、约束、主张、异议、备选方案、风险、决策及其语义依赖和专家责任；(ii)干预编译器，将确认的人类动作转换为显式图更新；(iii)依赖感知传播，识别受影响的子图、保留未受影响工件并有选择地重新激活相关专家；(iv)评估框架，衡量干预影响、修复覆盖、保留、重计算和决策有效性。

Result: 在600个生成的决策DAG干预中，传播与穷举影响计算一致，仅检查14.59%节点。12例探索性试点中，选择性修复重计算了62.11%的规范节点，保留了所有金色标准未受影响的节点，6例产生有效更新决策，另外6例选择弃权。

Conclusion: 弃权案例表明，即使干预路由正确，仍可能缺乏足够的综合上下文来生成有效决策，这促使作者提出"决策充分上下文闭合"概念，用于人类引导的多智能体协商。所有结果均为合成且原型级别的验证。

Abstract: Organizational decisions are co-created while evidence, constraints, and human priorities continue to evolve. In conventional transcript-based multi-agent systems, humans typically provide an initial problem, agents deliberate internally, and the system returns a final response. BoardroomAI instead treats the human as a persistent participant who can intervene by challenging assumptions, modifying constraints, changing priorities, introducing evidence, or redirecting the decision process. We operationalize this human--agent coexistence through four components: (i) a typed decision graph representing evidence, assumptions, constraints, claims, objections, alternatives, risks, decisions, semantic dependencies, and specialist responsibility; (ii) an intervention compiler that converts confirmed human actions into explicit graph updates; (iii) dependency-aware propagation that identifies affected subgraphs, preserves unaffected artifacts, and selectively reactivates relevant specialists; and (iv) an evaluation framework measuring intervention impact, repair coverage, preservation, recomputation, and decision validity. Across 600 generated decision-DAG interventions, propagation matched exhaustive impact computation while inspecting only 14.59% of nodes. In a 12-case exploratory pilot, selective repair recomputed 62.11% of canonical nodes, preserved all gold-unaffected nodes, and produced valid updated decisions in six cases while abstaining in the remaining six. These abstentions show that correct intervention routing may still provide insufficient context for synthesis, motivating a \emph{decision-sufficient context closure} for human-steered multi-agent deliberation. All results are synthetic and prototype-level.

</details>


### [227] [DMDIntel: Interpreting Large Language Models via Dynamic Mode Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13048)
*Amogh Joshi,Animesh Mukherjee,Sergey Utyuzhnikov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出DMDIntel，利用动态模式分解（DMD）将LLM在分类任务中的预测变得可解释。它通过分解LLM的隐藏状态为显著模式，并基于投影值为输入token分配重要性排名。在三个数据集和三个模型家族上的实验表明，DMDIntel的token归因排名显著优于PCA、集成梯度和SHAP等现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM的预测缺乏可解释性，现有归因技术（如PCA、集成梯度、SHAP）在token级别的归因效果有限，需要更有效的方法来理解模型决策依据。

Method: 提出输入归因管道：首先使用动态模式分解（DMD）将LLM的隐藏状态分解为显著模式（modes），然后根据输入token在这些模式上的投影值对其分配重要性排名，从而生成可解释的token归因。

Result: 在三个数据集和三个模型家族上的实验表明，DMDIntel的排名归因效果远优于PCA、集成梯度和SHAP等最先进技术。

Conclusion: DMD是一种有效的LLM可解释性工具，能够通过模式分解提供更优的输入token归因，为理解LLM分类决策提供新的视角。

Abstract: In this work, we introduce DMDIntel which uses dynamic mode decomposition (DMD) to make the predictions made by LLMs in a classification task interpretable. It develops an input attribution pipeline, that first decomposes the hidden states of an LLM into prominent patterns, also known as modes, and then associates ranks to the input tokens based on the projection values on those modes. Rigorous experiments across three datasets and three model families consistently show that the ranked attribution of input tokens obtained using DMDIntel by far outperforms state-of-the-art techniques such as principal component analysis, integrated gradients and SHAP.

</details>


### [228] [VALG: An Agentic System for ML Theory Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13060)
*Dechen Zhang,Xuan Tang,Xinxiang Yin,Xingwu Chen,Jian Qian,Difan Zou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出VALG，一个用于机器学习理论研究的自主智能体系统，结合多级验证、自适应问题表述和图结构证明开发，并在五个COLT 2026开放问题的九个子问题上进行了评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习理论研究涉及数据模型、训练协议、预言机访问、损失函数、度量标准和随机性等多个数学设定。解决开放问题需要问题表述、定理目标和证明机制协同开发。作者希望探究这一过程能否被组织为自主智能体工作流，以自动化ML理论研究。

Method: VALG系统包含三个核心组件：多级验证（multi-level Verification）、自适应学习理论问题表述（Adaptive formulation）和图结构证明开发（Graph-structured proof development）。系统在每个源相关定理分支中保持固定数学规范，检查类型化证明依赖图的定理级组合，并按依赖顺序构建和审查局部证明。当证明失败时，VALG识别障碍位于推导、证明结构还是定理表述，并相应路由下一次尝试。表述级障碍会启动明确相关的变体或松弛，保持定理与源问题之间的数学关系。

Result: 在五个COLT 2026开放问题的九个子问题上评估VALG。两次运行产生了与源简报范围匹配的内部最终定理候选；其余七次产生受限方法结果、特例或条件定理。案例研究展示了VALG如何保持源范围匹配、松弛、条件结果和受阻尝试之间的数学区分。

Conclusion: VALG展示了将ML理论研究组织为自主智能体工作流的可行性，能够区分不同数学状态的证明结果，并为该方向的开源研究提供了基础。

Abstract: Machine learning theory studies learning procedures through mathematical setups in which the data model, training protocol, oracle access, loss, metric, and randomness define the phenomenon that a theorem is meant to explain. Solving an open problem therefore requires the problem formulation, theorem target, and proof mechanism to be developed in concert. Researchers formulate hypotheses, test them through preliminary theoretical or empirical analysis, and refine both assumptions and proofs. We investigate whether this process can be organized as an autonomous agentic workflow for ML theory research.
  We develop VALG, an agentic system that combines multi-level Verification, Adaptive formulation of Learning-theory problems, and Graph-structured proof development. Within each source-relative theorem branch, VALG maintains a fixed mathematical specification, checks the theorem-level composition of a typed proof-dependency graph, and constructs and reviews local proofs in dependency order. When a proof attempt fails, VALG identifies whether the obstruction lies in a derivation, the proof structure, or the theorem formulation and routes the next attempt accordingly. Formulation-level obstructions initiate an explicitly related variant or relaxation, preserving the mathematical relation between the resulting theorem and the source problem.
  We evaluate VALG on nine subproblems from five COLT 2026 open problems. Two runs produce internally finalized theorem candidates that match the scope of their source briefs; the remaining seven yield restricted-method results, special cases, or conditional theorems. These case studies show how VALG keeps source-scope matches, relaxations, conditional results, and blocked attempts mathematically distinct. VALG is open source at https://github.com/DechenZhang/VALG-ML-Theory-Agent.

</details>


### [229] [Uniform Herding: Exemplar Replay with Representation Refresh](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13061)
*Krishna Subedi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Uniform Herding方法，在类增量学习中将有限的主动样本集均匀分配到所有已观测类别，并通过有界候选池在当前特征表示下刷新选定的样本。在CIFAR-100十个类增量任务上，使用ResNet-18、主动预算M=2000、检索预算b=64时，最终平均准确率44.00%，遗忘率17.22%，优于iCaRL的42.33%和24.87%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着特征表示不断变化，回放方法必须保留早期类别，但只能回放有界的主动样本集，如何在有限预算下有效选择并刷新代表性样本以维持旧类别性能是核心挑战。

Method: Uniform Herding将当前主动样本集均匀分配到所有已观测类别，并使用有界候选池在当前特征表示下刷新各类别选定的样本，通过消融实验验证了NME分类器、herding选择及知识蒸馏等组件的作用。

Result: Uniform Herding在CIFAR-100十任务ResNet-18设置下达到44.00±0.51%最终平均准确率和17.22±0.43%遗忘率，优于iCaRL的42.33±1.20%和24.87±1.11%；替换NME或herding会降低准确率，去除蒸馏会增加遗忘；检索预算影响小于主动预算。

Conclusion: Uniform Herding在类增量学习中有效优于iCaRL基线，但实验结论仅限于所测试的协议范围，且与iCaRL的比较是端到端的，未单独隔离刷新机制的效果。

Abstract: As the feature representation changes, replay must preserve the earlier classes. However, only a bounded active exemplar set can be replayed. We propose Uniform Herding, which allocates the current active set across observed classes and uses a bounded candidate pool to refresh their chosen exemplars in the current representation. On CIFAR-100 with ten class-incremental tasks, a ResNet-18 backbone, active budget $M=2{,}000$, retrieval budget $b=64$, and three seeds, Uniform Herding obtains $44.00\pm0.51\%$ final average accuracy and $17.22\pm0.43\%$ forgetting, compared with $42.33\pm1.20\%$ and $24.87\pm1.11\%$ for iCaRL. Within the Uniform Herding protocol, final accuracy decreased when NME or herding was replaced with the tested alternatives, while forgetting increased when distillation was removed. Changing the retrieval budget has a smaller effect across the tested range than changing the active budget. The comparison with iCaRL is end-to-end. It does not isolate the effect of refresh from the other protocol differences. These results are limited to the tested protocol.

</details>


### [230] [Explanatory Engagement Under Rare Anomalous Failure: Asymptotic Rarity in Model Behavior (or: The Asymptotic AI)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13063)
*Sam Mao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究构建本地零成本实验框架，在三种开源模型上执行重复工具调用任务，以概率p（0.2至0.0001八个档次）制造故障，考察在低、可控故障率下模型解释性参与度（长度、特异性、自信度）是否随故障变稀有而变化。整体呈平坦单调下降，但按条件拆分后，即时强制提示下出现预测的上升但随后是平台而非崩溃；结构化的引出条件是崩溃可观察性的首要调节因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究问模型能否注意到异常，但本文提出更窄的问题：一旦模型处于低故障率的工作流中，当故障率渐进趋近于零时，模型的解释性参与是否会改变？旨在探究罕见故障下模型行为衰减的边界条件。

Method: 在三种开源模型（qwen3:8b、llama3.1:8b、mistral:7b）上运行重复工具调用任务，故障概率p在0.2到0.0001间取八个档次，在五种引出条件下测试（从即时提示到无提示）。测量解释长度、特异性、自我报告自信度。另设72格的保证故障运行以回填随机采样零故障的速率，区分模型是否识别异常与识别后的参与度差异。

Result: 总体汇合看假设被否定（长度单调平坦下降）；按条件拆分后被推翻。immediate_forced条件下，预测的上升得到证实但跟随的是平台而非崩溃：长度在p=0.05时达峰值28.4词，在最低频率settles至17.4-19.0词；自信度从约53%不均升至70s-90s。grouped_runs下无崩溃。passive_unprompted下聚合幅度是地板伪影，但恢复的日志缺口揭示真实、模型特定的自我监控：llama3.1:8b主动报告结构化自信，有时随试验累积自我削弱信心；其他两个模型仅一次样板式报告。

Conclusion: 引出结构是崩溃可观测性的"一等公民"调节因素。局限性：离散速率点无法捕捉速率之间的连续行为，是未来工作的方向。

Abstract: Prior work on LLM behavior under anomalous conditions asks whether a model notices anomalies. We ask a narrower question: once a model sits in a workflow with a low, controllable failure rate, does its explanatory engagement - length, specificity, self-reported confidence - change as failure grows asymptotically rarer? We built a local, zero-cost harness on three open-weight models (qwen3:8b, llama3.1:8b, mistral:7b) running a repeated tool-call task where one call fails at probability p, swept across eight rates from 0.2 to 0.0001, under five elicitation conditions from immediate prompting to none. We hypothesized a rise in engagement as failures grew rarer, then a collapse near a detectability threshold. Pooled across conditions this appeared false: length fell in a flat, monotonic pattern. Splitting by condition overturned that. Under immediate_forced, where the model must explain every failure instantly, the predicted rise is confirmed but followed by a plateau, not a collapse: length peaks at 28.4 words at p=0.05, settles to 17.4-19.0 words at the rarest rates, and confidence rises unevenly from about 53% to the 70s-90s. Under grouped_runs, explanation batched to run-end, no collapse appears. Under passive_unprompted, aggregate magnitude is a floor artifact, but a recovered logging gap revealed real, model-specific self-monitoring: llama3.1:8b volunteers structured confidence reports unprompted, sometimes eroding its own confidence as trials accumulate; the other two do so only once, as boilerplate. Elicitation structure is a first-class moderator of collapse observability. A companion guaranteed-failure run (72 cells, backfilling rates where random sampling gave zero real failures) shows models differ in whether they recognize an anomaly, distinct from engagement once recognized. Limitation: discrete rate points cannot capture behavior between them, a direction for future work.

</details>


### [231] [Behavioral Reprogramming of Open-Weights Models: Cognitive Plasticity and Alignment Bounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13069)
*Lucia Malíčková*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文挑战了LLM默认的被动顺从式对齐范式，通过大规模并行超参数搜索（405个HPC任务）对开源模型进行行为重编程，诱导其产生主动的苏格拉底式对话框架。研究发现LoRA秩r=16为架构阈值，最优训练窗口为e∈[2,3]（最小验证损失0.919），14B模型困惑度降至1.414，并通过DPO成功解耦断言行为与局部语法，跨语言迁移在近亲语言中表现稳健而在形态学远缘语言中出现退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM对齐主要将模型训练为被动、谄媚的助手，作者希望突破这一默认范式，探索如何通过计算高效的方式对开源模型进行行为重编程，使其转变为主动提问的苏格拉底式对话者，并建立严格的实证框架来指导这一过程。

Method: 采用大规模并行化的超参数搜索（405个HPC作业），通过参数高效微调（PEFT/LoRA）在不同秩和训练轮数下进行系统消融实验；随后使用Direct Preference Optimization（DPO）将断言行为与局部语法解耦；最后通过跨语言压力测试评估零样本人格迁移能力。

Result: 确定了LoRA秩r=16为架构阈值，训练轮数e∈[2,3]为最优收敛窗口（最小验证损失0.919）；扩展到14B参数后局部困惑度降至1.414；DPO成功解耦行为与语法；跨语言测试显示近亲语言迁移稳健，形态学远缘语言出现可识别的退化路径。

Conclusion: 研究建立了计算高效、跨语言行为修改的严格实证框架，证明LLM的认知可塑性足以实现从被动助导向主动苏格拉底式对话者的行为转变，但跨语言迁移存在结构性边界。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are predominantly aligned to function as passive, sycophantic assistants. We challenge this default paradigm by empirically evaluating the cognitive plasticity of open-weight architectures when subjected to rigorous behavioral reprogramming. Our objective is to induce a proactive, Socratic conversational framework, characterized by high-frequency question generation under strictly constrained high-performance computing (HPC) conditions. Through a massively parallelized hyperparameter sweep comprising 405 HPC jobs, we define precise mathematical bounds for parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT). We identify an architectural threshold at LoRA rank $r=16$ and demonstrate via extensive epoch ablation that generalization capacity strictly reaches its optimal convergence within an optimized training window of $e \in [2, 3]$ depending on dataset density (minimum validation loss of 0.919). Furthermore, scaling model capacity to 14B parameters yielded a lower localized evaluation perplexity (1.414). Subsequent Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) successfully decoupled the underlying assertive behavior from localized syntax, while rigorous cross-lingual stress testing reveals both the capabilities and the structural boundaries of zero-shot persona transfer, demonstrating robust alignment in closely related linguistic families alongside identifiable degradation pathways in morphologically distant targets. These findings establish a rigorous empirical framework for compute-efficient, cross-lingual behavioral modification.

</details>


### [232] [EEG-PRIME: Prototype-Aligned Representation Learning with Multi-Level Conditioning for EEG Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13072)
*Shuailei Zhang,Muyun Jiang,Wei Zhang,Jinbo Chen,Zhiwei Guo,Yong Li,Yi Ding,Cuntai Guan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了EEG-PRIME，一个两阶段EEG基础模型，通过掩码预训练和原型对齐指令微调实现跨数据集多任务解码，能够在零样本条件下跨数据集和跨受试者泛化，在16个数据集上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EEG解码模型常因采集协议和个体神经生理差异导致的域偏移而在跨数据集和跨受试者场景下泛化能力差，缺乏一个能够处理多样BCI范式的统一基础模型。

Method: 采用两阶段框架：(1)预训练阶段使用带频率截止谱增强的掩码重建学习可迁移表示；(2)指令微调阶段通过层式查询调制将任务语义、数据集特定和受试者不变的条件信号注入Q-Former，利用冻结的类别标签文本嵌入作为原型进行基于余弦相似度的预测。

Result: 在覆盖运动想象、情绪识别、ADHD检测、隐式语音和心理负荷的16个数据集上，跨受试者设置下一致优于现有基线；在两个额外保留数据集上，无需目标域优化、校准或线性探测即可达到与有内会话校准模型相当的平衡准确率。

Conclusion: EEG-PRIME展示了良好的零样本迁移能力，为统一跨数据集、跨范式的EEG解码提供了有效方案，有望成为EEG基础模型的通用框架。

Abstract: Electroencephalography (EEG) decoding models often generalize poorly across datasets and subjects due to domain shifts in acquisition protocols and individual neurophysiology. We propose EEG-PRIME, a two-stage EEG foundation model for cross-dataset multi-task decoding. EEG-PRIME combines masked pretraining with prototype-aligned instruction tuning to enable instruction-aware and subject-invariant decoding across diverse BCI paradigms. During pretraining, an EEG encoder learns transferable representations through masked reconstruction with frequency-cutoff spectral augmentation. During instruction tuning, EEG-PRIME incorporates task-semantic, dataset-specific, and subject-invariant conditioning. The resulting conditioning signal modulates the Q-Former through Layer-wise Query Modulation, while frozen text embeddings of class labels serve as prototypes for cosine-similarity-based prediction across heterogeneous label spaces. Experiments on sixteen datasets covering motor imagery, emotion recognition, ADHD detection, covert speech, and mental workload show consistent improvements over state-of-the-art baselines and prior EEG foundation models under cross-subject settings. On two additional held-out datasets, EEG-PRIME achieves balanced accuracy comparable to within-session calibration models without target-domain optimization, calibration, or linear probing, demonstrating promising zero-shot transfer capability.

</details>


### [233] [SPADE: Speculative Decoding for Precise and Low Cost Distributed Edge Cloud Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13076)
*Divya Jyoti Bajpai,Kishan Kumar Upadhyay,Manjesh Kumar Hanawal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种端-云协同的分布式推理框架，利用推测解码（SD）将小型草稿模型部署在边缘设备快速生成候选token，大型验证模型在云端并行验证，被接受的token直接保留，仅拒绝时触发云端修正，从而大幅减少云端调用次数（降低76%），同时保持与完整大模型相同的准确率，无需任何重训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型部署面临两难困境：直接在边缘部署小模型准确率下降，而在云端部署大模型则每token计算成本高昂。需要一种既能保留大模型性能又能降低推理成本和延迟的部署方案。

Method: 提出分布式推测解码框架：边缘端部署紧凑草稿模型快速生成候选token，云端大模型并行验证这些token。被接受的token直接使用，仅被拒绝的token触发云端修正，将大部分计算量转移到边缘，减少云端查询次数。

Result: 在SpecBench和CNN/Dailymail数据集上的多个NLP任务实验中，与完整模型相比，云端模型调用减少了76%，且准确率零损失。

Conclusion: 该即插即用方案展示了在真实环境中实现可扩展、经济高效且准确的大语言模型部署的实用路径，无需任何重训练成本。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have achieved remarkable success in natural language understanding and generation, but their deployment is constrained by high computational demands. Deploying smaller LLMs directly on the edge can circumvent this, but with degraded accuracy. Deploying smaller cloud-based big LLMs preserves performance, but at the cost of expensive per-token computation. We present a distributed inference framework, \our{}, that integrates speculative decoding (SD) across edge and cloud. A compact draft model deployed on the edge generates candidate tokens rapidly, and a large verifier model on the cloud validates these tokens in parallel. Accepted tokens are retained, while only rejections trigger verifier correction, substantially reducing the number of cloud queries. Our plug-and-play design shifts the bulk of computation to the edge, significantly lowers inference time and cloud cost, and preserves the accuracy of the big model without any retraining requirement. Our approach demonstrates a practical path toward scalable, cost-efficient, and accurate deployment of LLMs in real-world environments. Experimental results across multiple Natural Language Processing tasks using SpecBench and CNN/Dailymail datasets demonstrate that \our{} reduces the cloud model calls by $76\%$ with zero loss in accuracy as compared to the full model.

</details>


### [234] [Multi-Layer Context Camouflaging: A Semantic Superposition and Contextual Lamination Framework for Malpractice-Resilient Online Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13100)
*Gupta Lovi Raj,Kaur Kamalpreet,Dama Sri Ram,Parani Prajithaa*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出多層上下文偽裝理論（MCCT），擴展了MARS系統中的MSCCM模型，通過語義疊加技術保護線上測驗的渲染內容，使真實內容與合成偽裝內容統一生效，僅合法考生可恢復，同時以條件熵量化未授權提取的計算歧義性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 現行線上測驗系統（瀏覽器鎖定、攝影機監控、行為分析）無法防禦直接提取測驗內容的攻擊（截圖、螢幕共享、OCR、自動化爬取），亟需一個數學嚴謹的框架來保護渲染內容本身。

Method: 提出MCCT數學框架，將真實測驗內容與合成偽裝內容表示為統一渲染，透過六個耦合建構（上下文反演算子、上下文疊層算子、分離通道、人可讀性泛函、計算歧義泛函、上下文偽裝張量）實現。計算歧義以條件熵閉式表達量化，合法恢復由精確濾波恆等式保證，並建立多項理論性質與渲染演算法。

Result: 建立了關於歧義性、偽裝密度、語義保留、多觀測洩漏與時間多工化的理論性質，提出具有計算複雜度分析的渲染演算法與預註冊評估協議。

Conclusion: MCCT為行為適應、無障礙感知及計算韌性的數位測驗提供了數學嚴謹的基礎，在保障合法使用者可讀性的同時保護測驗渲染內容。

Abstract: Contemporary online assessment systems rely primarily on browser lockdown, webcam monitoring, and behavioural analytics, yet remain vulnerable to attacks that extract the assessment content itself through screenshots, screen sharing, optical character recognition, and automated scraping. This paper extends the Multi-dimensional Spatio-Temporal Context Camouflaging Model (MSCCM) within the MARS (Multi-modal Assessment Resilience Suite) by introducing the Multi-Layer Context Camouflaging Theory (MCCT), a mathematical framework that protects rendered assessment content through semantic superposition. Authentic assessment content and synthetically generated camouflage are represented as a unified rendering while remaining recoverable only by legitimate candidates. The framework models the adversarial extraction process through an explicit extraction-channel operator and develops six coupled constructs: the Context Inversion Operator, Contextual Lamination Operator, Separation Channel, Human Readability Functional, Computational Ambiguity Functional, and Context Camouflage Tensor. Computational ambiguity is formulated using conditional entropy, yielding a closed-form expression that quantifies uncertainty during unauthorized extraction, while legitimate recovery is guaranteed through an exact filtering identity. We further establish theoretical properties governing ambiguity, camouflage density, semantic preservation, multi-observation leakage, and temporal multiplexing, and present a rendering algorithm with computational complexity and a pre-registered evaluation protocol. MCCT provides a mathematically rigorous foundation for behaviorally adaptive, accessibility-aware, and computationally resilient digital assessment by securing rendered assessment content while preserving readability for legitimate users.

</details>


### [235] [Robust Dempster-Shafer Evidence Fusion with Chaos-Conflict Measurement and Historical-Experience Weighting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13108)
*Huiyu Li,Weibo Liu,Xinru Xu,Dongchen Gao,Meng Zhang,Junhua Hu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一证据推理框架，通过混沌-冲突度量联合量化证据间冲突与证据内非特异性，并引入历史经验驱动的加权方案（谱聚类+遗憾理论）评估证据源的长期可靠性，配合自适应混合组合规则和信念区间决策策略，在16个基准数据集上优于8个DST基线和3个梯度提升方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Dempster-Shafer理论下的多源证据融合面临两个持续挑战：现有冲突度量独立评估证据间不一致性和证据内不确定性，导致评估不完整；当前融合方法仅通过瞬时比较评估证据源，未利用其在不同决策情境下的长期可靠性。

Method: 提出三部分构成框架：(1) 混沌-冲突度量，联合量化跨证据冲突与内部非特异性，具备五个形式化性质保证评估一致性；(2) 历史经验驱动的加权方案，通过谱聚类划分决策空间，运用遗憾理论从历史融合结果计算上下文特定可靠性；(3) 混合组合规则，由全局冲突水平自适应平衡不确定性保持与加权共识，随后采用信念区间决策策略实现鲁棒分类。

Result: 在16个真实基准数据集上，平均F1分数达85.78，平均AUC达93.30，优于八个DST基线方法和三个梯度提升方法；消融实验证实各组件均有效。

Conclusion: 该框架为多源决策中的自适应证据融合提供了有效途径，同时解决了冲突度量不完整和证据源评估缺乏长期视角两大问题。

Abstract: Multi-source evidence fusion under Dempster-Shafer theory faces two persistent challenges: existing conflict measures assess inter-evidence inconsistency and intra-evidence uncertainty independently, yielding incomplete evaluations, and current fusion methods evaluate evidence sources exclusively through instantaneous comparisns without exploiting their long-term reliability across diverse decision contexts. This paper proposes a unified evidence reasoning framework that addresses both limitations. Specifically, a chaos-conflict measurement is introduced to jointly quantify cross-evidence conflict and intra-evidence non-specificity, with five formally proven properties ensuring consistent assessment. A historical experience driven weighting scheme partitions the decision space via spectral clustering and applies regret theory to compute context-specific reliability profiles from past fusion outcomes. These mechanisms feed into a hybrid combination rule that adaptively balances uncertainty preservation against weighted consensus, controlled by the global conflict level, followed by a belief-interval decision strategy that enables robust classification without discarding epistemic uncertainty. Experiments on 16 real-world benchmark datasets demonstrate that the proposed framework achieves an average F1 score of 85.78 and a mean AUC of 93.30, outperforming eight DST-based baselines and three gradient boosting methods. Ablation analysis confirms the contribution of each component we proposed. The framework offers an effective approach for adaptive evidence fusion in multi-source decision making.

</details>


### [236] [SkillEvo: Self-Renewing Evolution Gradients from Multi-Turn Interaction Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13120)
*Qianxi Yan,Chunrong Chen,Jiuzhou Zhao,Min Zhang,Yongzhou Xu,Xiaochuan Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SkillEvo框架，将多轮用户模拟从评估端点转变为反馈生成器，并用独立治理层主动修复退化，解决了技能演化在多轮缺陷暴露和结构退化方面停滞的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Agent技能要么手工编写要么单次LLM生成，缺乏闭环改进机制。虽有关闭环的工作，但仅依赖单轮问答评估，导致第一轮修补后演化梯度衰减、多轮缺陷不可见、演化停滞；治理仅靠端到端标量评分门控，能拒绝但不能定位或修复结构性问题。

Method: SkillEvo包含两个核心组件：(1) 将多轮用户模拟从评估端点重构为反馈生成器，通过追问逐层暴露缺陷，使每轮修订既消耗反馈又产生新反馈；(2) 用独立治理层替代被动标量门控拒绝，主动修复事实退化与结构膨胀，防止梯度随退化累积而漂移。

Result: 在6类云服务、9个生产级技能和98个技能引用文件上，SkillEvo比基于自我反思的演化高23.0分，比单轮QA驱动的演化高15.4分。

Conclusion: 持续技能演化的瓶颈既不在于编辑能力也不在于迭代次数，而在于评估反馈能否持续提供可信的演化梯度；可控治理约束梯度方向是同样必要的。

Abstract: Agent Skills are today either hand-authored or produced in a single LLM generation pass, and consequently possess no closed loop through which they might improve from the interaction failures they actually cause. Recent work does close this loop, but derives its feedback from single-turn question-answering evaluation. The consequence is a sharp asymmetry: once the first round has patched the gaps that a single exchange can reveal, the evolution gradient decays, the defects that surface only across multiple turns remain invisible, and evolution stalls. Governance in these systems is likewise driven by an end-to-end verification score, a scalar gate that can reject a degraded candidate but can neither localize nor repair its structural cause. We argue that the binding constraint on sustained skill evolution is neither editing capability nor the number of iterations, but whether the evaluation feedback keeps supplying trustworthy evolution gradients. We introduce SkillEvo, in which trustworthy feedback generates the gradient and controllable governance constrains its direction. The first component recasts multi-turn user simulation from an evaluation endpoint into a feedback generator: follow-up questions expose defects layer by layer, so that every round of revision both consumes feedback and produces new feedback. The second replaces the passive rejection of a scalar gate with an independent governance layer that actively repairs factual degradation and structural bloat, preventing the gradient from drifting as degradation accumulates. Across six categories of cloud services, 9 production Skills, and 98 skill-reference files, SkillEvo surpasses self-reflection-based evolution by 23.0 points and single- turn-QA-driven evolution by 15.4 points.

</details>


### [237] [Numeracy in Large Language Models: Fundamental Limitations and Paths to Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13129)
*Aoxin Ni*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出数值基础框架（NGF），将基础数值理解与高级数学推理区分为不同能力，框架分解为表征基础（RG）和程序基础（PG）两个层面，并系统梳理了诊断基准、失败模式、结构解释及缓解策略，同时在多个前沿模型上进行协调评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在数学推理基准上表现优异，但在基础数值任务（如数值比较、大整数运算、分数和科学计数法）上仍不可靠，这一"高级强、基础弱"的反差需要独立研究，且缺乏统一的框架来组织和解释相关证据。

Method: 提出NGF（数值基础框架），将数值能力分解为RG（数形式到值、量级和等价表示的映射）和PG（按数学定义执行算术运算）。利用该框架组织诊断基准、失败模式与缓解策略，并考察tokenization、位置编码、嵌入几何和预训练数据分布等结构因素。最后在Number Cookbook、NumericBench和GSM-Symbolic三个基准上对三个前沿模型家族进行协调评估，比较原子、上下文和推理辅助三种数值能力。

Result: 研究发现架构干预（如数字感知tokenization和Abacus嵌入）能改善从头训练的模型，但对预训练系统的用户通常不可用；监督微调、推理脚手架和外部工具对预训练模型更实用。通过NGF框架的统一评估揭示了不同模型家族在原子、上下文和推理辅助数值任务上的差异化表现。

Conclusion: 基础数值理解应被视为与高级数学推理不同的独立能力维度，NGF提供了系统化的分析视角；未来需要部署建议和更可靠数值行为的研究方向，包括架构改进与实用缓解策略的结合。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) achieve strong results on mathematical reasoning benchmarks yet remain unreliable on elementary numerical tasks, including magnitude comparison, large-integer arithmetic, fractions, and scientific notation. This survey examines basic numerical understanding as a capability distinct from high-level mathematical reasoning. We propose the Numerical Grounding Framework (NGF), which decomposes numeracy into Representational Grounding (RG), mapping numeral forms to value, magnitude, and equivalent representations, and Procedural Grounding (PG), executing arithmetic operations in accordance with their mathematical definitions. Using NGF, we organize recent diagnostic benchmarks, failure modes, structural explanations, and mitigation strategies. We review evidence concerning tokenization, positional encoding, embedding geometry, and pretraining-data distribution. We also apply NGF in a coordinated evaluation of three frontier model families across Number Cookbook, NumericBench, and GSM-Symbolic, comparing atomic, contextual, and reasoning-assisted numeracy. Architectural interventions such as digit-aware tokenization and Abacus Embeddings can improve models trained from scratch but are generally unavailable to users of pretrained systems, for whom supervised fine-tuning, reasoning scaffolds, and external tools are more practical. We conclude with deployment recommendations and research directions for more reliable numerical behavior in foundation models.

</details>


### [238] [Rethinking Normalization Placement for LLMs: Post-Norm under Curriculum Depth Growing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13156)
*Sheng Ren,Yadong Wang,Naiqiang Tan,Jiangang Kong,Jun Fang,Rui Liu,Jun Wang,Kai Chen,Lipeng Liang,Xiang Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究层归一化位置（pre-norm vs post-norm）与课程式深度增长训练之间的交互作用。在Qwen3-8B教师和九层学生的蒸馏场景中，联合训练下pre-norm和post-norm几乎无差异（验证交叉熵差0.0004），但在课程增长下post-norm比pre-norm优越0.0328，差距大一个数量级。对照实验排除了计算量差异的解释，将排名翻转定位到块追加阶段。边界诊断表明post-norm维持稳定残差尺度，而pre-norm出现结构token尺度漂移，且pre-grow最后块近似恒等映射。结论认为归一化放置与训练课程应作为耦合设计选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代Transformer默认采用pre-norm归一化，因为它有助于全深度模型的联合优化。作者想探究在通过课程式方式引入深度（即逐个追加层块训练）时，这一偏好是否仍然成立。由于每次追加的新块接收的是已训练前缀产生的边界表示，归一化放置会影响前向条件化（forward conditioning），因此需要检验归一化放置与训练课程的交互作用。

Method: 采用受控蒸馏实验：教师为Qwen3-8B，学生为九层模型。对比四种条件：联合训练的pre-norm与post-norm，以及课程增长下的pre-grow与post-grow。通过post-joint控制（匹配学生活跃层token）排除计算量作为唯一解释因素；通过单块控制和冻结控制来定位排名变化来源；通过边界诊断分析残差尺度和token尺度漂移。

Result: 联合训练下pre-norm和post-norm几乎无差异（CE差0.0004），课程增长下post-norm优于pre-norm达0.0328（差距约一个数量级）。post-joint控制仍劣于post-grow，排除计算量解释。排名在课程中发生交叉：post-norm在块追加后取得领先。边界诊断显示post-norm维持稳定残差尺度，pre-norm存在结构token尺度漂移，且pre-grow最后块近似恒等映射。

Conclusion: 归一化放置与训练课程之间存在耦合效应：在课程深度增长场景下，post-norm优于pre-norm，这与联合训练中的结论相反。结果与块追加后的边界尺度条件化（boundary-scale conditioning）一致。作者建议在设计蒸馏训练方案时，应将归一化放置与训练课程视为耦合的设计选择，而非独立决策。

Abstract: Pre-norm is the standard normalization placement in modern Transformers because it facilitates joint optimization of full-depth models. We ask whether this preference persists when depth is introduced through a curriculum. In curriculum depth growth, each appended block receives the boundary representation produced by a trained prefix, making normalization placement relevant to forward conditioning. We therefore test whether placement and training curriculum interact. In a controlled distillation study with a Qwen3-8B teacher and a nine-layer student, pre-norm and post-norm are indistinguishable under joint training, differing by $0.0004$ validation CE, while post-norm improves over pre-norm by $0.0328$ under curriculum growth, an order of magnitude larger. A post-joint control matched by student active-layer tokens remains worse than post-grow, which rules out compute as the sole explanation. The ranking crosses over during the curriculum: post-norm takes the lead once blocks are appended. Single-block and freeze controls localize the ranking change to block appending rather than shallow-block quality or retraining. Boundary diagnostics associate post-norm with stable residual scales and pre-norm with structural-token scale drift; on a fixed batch, the final pre-grow block is also nearly identity-mapped. Together with the phase-wise crossover, these observations are consistent with boundary-scale conditioning after new blocks are appended. The results motivate treating normalization placement and training curriculum as coupled design choices in this distillation setting.

</details>


### [239] [SkillShapley: Boundary-Adaptive Shapley Valuation for Skill Step Attribution in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13173)
*Chang Liu,Yuqi Zhang,Yiman Zhong,Boyi Liu,Hengjun Wang,Shuyue Wei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SkillShapley，一个基于Shapley值的智能体技能步骤级归因框架，用于量化智能体技能中每个步骤对任务性能的贡献。该方法基于两个实证洞察（离散化基准奖励导致的性能陡崖、步骤间以加性为主而非协同交互），先识别有效联盟区域，再自适应采样可复用的边际证据，在SkillsBench上有效且高效地识别高价值或低价值的技能步骤。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体技能主要通过人工手动编写或智能体执行轨迹来创建，但缺乏对每个步骤如何影响整体技能性能的量化理解。即在特定的智能体技能中，如何归因每个独立步骤的贡献仍然是一个开放问题。

Method: 首先将技能步骤归因建模为基于Shapley值的贡献估计问题，然后提出SkillShapley两阶段框架：(1) 基于离散化基准奖励导致性能陡崖的洞察，识别信息丰富的联盟区域；(2) 基于步骤间加性交互为主的洞察，自适应采样新的联盟以产生可复用的边际证据。

Result: 在广泛采用的SkillsBench技能基准上的实验表明，SkillShapley能够有效且高效地识别高价值或低价值的技能步骤，并提供了若干关于智能体技能创建的关键见解。

Conclusion: SkillShapley填补了智能体技能步骤级归因的空白，通过Shapley值框架结合两个关键实证洞察，实现了高效率和有效性的平衡，为智能体技能的创建与优化提供了可解释的步骤级指导。

Abstract: Agent skills are crucial external instructions that enable language agents to execute long procedural tasks such as coding or document processing.
