### [339] [Where You Measure Decides What You Measure: Position Selection in Ablation-Based SAE Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13337)
*Valentin Noël*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 稀疏自编码器（SAE）的因果评估（激活/抑制潜在变量）通常在其激活最强的token位置测量，但这一位置选择由字典本身决定且几乎从不报告。作者发现即使两个SAE编码几乎相同的潜在变量，也常在不同token处被比较，导致评估结果差异。通过控制变量训练六个仅拟合选择不同的自编码器，作者证明大部分"字典分歧"实际是测量位置差异造成的（方差从7.6%和11.9%降至接近零），且增加语料规模反而加剧问题。作者提出一行代码的修正协议，并审计了五篇已发表论文。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SAE用于解释语言模型计算的内容，评估一个潜在变量是否重要通常通过"关闭它看效果"的因果测试。但潜在变量在多个token处激活，标准做法是在激活最强的token处测量，而这一位置选择由被评估的字典决定且不被报告，导致不同字典的因果评估可能在不同位置进行，使得结果不可比较。

Method: 1) 对比Google发布的两个SAE，按解码器相似度匹配潜在变量，检查它们是否选择相同token测量；2) 从同一初始化训练六个仅拟合选择不同的自编码器，确保潜在变量在各字典中含义相同，从而分离"位置效应"与"字典差异"；3) 跨16倍语料规模范围测试位置一致性；4) 提出一行代码的修正协议，并对五篇已发表论文进行审计。

Result: 两个字典即使编码几乎相同的潜在变量，也会在很大比例上选择不同的token进行测量。当八个字典在相同token处测量时，位置选择造成的方差从7.6%和11.9%降至接近零。增加语料规模不会改善位置一致性，反而使其更差。修正仅需一行评估代码。

Conclusion: 不带测量位置的因果数字描述的是测量token而非潜在变量本身。SAE评估协议必须报告测量位置并确保跨字典在相同位置比较，否则论文间的因果数字不可比。

Abstract: Sparse autoencoders are meant to name the things a language model computes, and the usual way to check that a latent matters is to switch it off and see what changes. But a latent fires at many tokens, and the effect has to be measured at one of them. The convention is to measure where the latent fires hardest. That choice is almost never reported, and it is not made by the experimenter: it is made by the dictionary under evaluation. Change the dictionary and the measurement moves to a different token. We show this is not a detail. Take two sparse autoencoders released by Google for the same model and match their latents by decoder similarity: even among the pairs the two dictionaries encode almost identically, they pick different tokens for a large share of them. Two dictionaries compared under the usual protocol are therefore very often compared at different places. To separate the convention from the dictionaries we train six autoencoders from one initialisation, differing only in fitting choices, so that a latent means the same thing in each. Most of the variance such a comparison reads as "these dictionaries disagree about this latent" turns out to be the position instead: it falls from 7.6% and 11.9% of variance to near zero once every dictionary is measured at the same token. More evaluation data does not rescue it. Across a sixteenfold range of corpus sizes the dictionaries agree less about where to measure, not more, so the problem grows with scale. The correction is one line of evaluation code. We give the protocol an ablation-based causal number must report to be comparable across papers, and an audit of five published papers against it. In short: a causal number reported without its position describes the token it was taken at as much as the latent it was taken from.

</details>


### [340] [Simulation-to-real transfer learning for infrared spectroscopic chemical sensing and analysis from molecules to complex samples](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13341)
*Yusen Tan,Yixuan Chen,Zheng Fang,Pan Liu,Yifan Li,Qinyu Guo,Zhedong Lin,Yuqiang Li,Xiangxiang Zeng,Tong Wang,Jun Xia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: UltraIR是一个拥有超过1亿参数的红外光谱基础模型，通过在大约6000万模拟红外光谱上进行预训练（结合光谱重建、分子指纹相似性对齐和官能团预测），实现从模拟到真实的迁移学习。该模型在官能团预测、分子结构解析、理化性质预测、混合物分析、细菌分类、药材溯源、微塑料分类和土壤性质预测等多项任务中均优于传统机器学习和任务特定深度学习方法，且在有限标签数据和零样本跨仪器/跨实验室场景下表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统红外光谱解释依赖专家知识且难以规模化，而现有机器学习方法针对单一任务或数据集定制，需要大量标注训练集，跨目标和跨数据集迁移能力差。作者旨在开发一个能够模拟到真实转移学习、数据高效、可适应多种分析目标的基础模型。

Method: UltraIR采用基础模型范式：首先在约6000万模拟红外光谱上进行预训练，预训练任务包括光谱重建、分子指纹相似性对齐和官能团预测；随后通过任务特定的标签或目标进行微调以适应下游任务。模型参数超过1亿。

Result: UltraIR在官能团预测、分子结构解析、理化性质预测、混合物组分鉴定与定量、细菌分类、药用植物地理来源溯源与成分定量、微塑料分类和土壤性质预测等多项任务中均优于传统机器学习和任务特定的深度学习基线。在有限标注实验光谱和跨FTIR仪器/实验室的零样本推断中表现出色。

Conclusion: UltraIR提供了一条通向灵活、数据高效的化学传感途径，能够处理来自复杂真实世界样本的红外光谱分析，验证了模拟预训练基础模型在光谱分析领域的巨大潜力。

Abstract: Infrared (IR) spectroscopy is widely used for chemical sensing, but extracting reliable chemical information from spectra remains challenging. Conventional interpretation is labor-intensive, relies on prior knowledge and reference spectra, and is difficult to scale, whereas most machine-learning methods are tailored to individual tasks or datasets, require large labeled training sets, and transfer poorly across analytical objectives and experimental datasets. Here we introduce UltraIR, a foundation model for IR spectroscopy with more than 100 million parameters that enables simulation-to-real transfer learning for chemical sensing and analysis from molecules to complex samples. UltraIR is pretrained on approximately 60 million simulated IR spectra using spectral reconstruction, molecular fingerprint similarity alignment, and functional-group prediction, then adapted to downstream objectives with task-specific labels or targets. Across functional-group prediction, molecular structure elucidation, physicochemical property prediction, mixture-component identification and quantification, bacterial classification, medicinal-herb geographic origin traceability and constituent quantification, microplastics classification, and soil property prediction, UltraIR outperforms conventional machine-learning and task-specific deep-learning baselines. It performs strongly with limited labeled experimental spectra and in zero-shot inference for the same analytical task across Fourier-transform infrared spectrometers and laboratories, providing a route to adaptable, data-efficient chemical sensing from complex real-world samples.

</details>


### [341] [When Local Variance Optimality Is Not Enough: RoPE-Aligned Q/K Rotations for Dynamic 4-Bit Quantisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13365)
*Shuhan Wang,Yilin Luo,Nan Xu,Chi Wang Cheung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究基于旋转的后训练量化中，与RoPE兼容的成对旋转变换（pairwise rotation）相比全头混合（full-head mixing）的Hadamard变换在动态W4A4KV4量化设置下的效果。结果表明成对配置无法提升精度，且增加混合支持度（从双通道扩展到全头）能持续降低量化误差和困惑度退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RoPE将每个注意力头划分为二维频率对，而现有的基于旋转的量化方法通常对整个注意力头施加正交变换。作者希望探究尊重RoPE分解结构的变换（成对旋转）能否优于全头混合，并理解结构化代理目标（如最小化通道方差）与量化器实际决策统计量不一致时为何会导致次优结果。

Method: 1) 证明在频率不同时，唯一与RoPE交换的单头正交映射就是逐对的旋转（共轭结论）；2) 在头共享参数化下，推导出最小化较大通道方差的最优旋转角度（基于汇集协方差、位置平均的代理目标），并验证实现达到解析最小值；3) 在四个检查点上对比全头Hadamard与成对旋转在动态W4A4KV4设置下的困惑度；4) 进行受控插值实验，从双通道混合逐步过渡到全头混合，观察K范围、相对量化误差和困惑度的变化。

Result: 成对旋转配置在四个检查点的短长上下文长度下均增加困惑度，无法提升精度；与Hadamard组合时满足±0.05-PPL区间标准；从K单独估计共享角度可改善成对配置但无法弥合与全头混合的差距；受控插值表明K范围、相对量化误差和困惑度退化随混合支持度增加而单调下降。

Conclusion: 对结构化代理目标的最优性并不一定能降低量化误差，原因在于代理目标（位置平均的汇集协方差二阶矩）与量化器的尺度设定统计量（基于tokenwise组范围的动态步长）不一致，且成对变换仅支持双通道混合，混合支持度不足是性能差距的根本原因。

Abstract: Rotation-based post-training quantisation commonly applies an orthogonal transform across an entire attention head to reduce outlier-induced error. RoPE instead partitions each head into two-dimensional frequency pairs, raising the question of whether a transform respecting this decomposition can improve on full-head mixing. Prior work has established the per-pair rotations that commute with RoPE. We state the converse result that, for distinct frequencies, no other single-head orthogonal map commutes with RoPE. For the head-shared parameterisation used in our experiments, we then derive the rotation angle that minimises the larger channel variance under a pooled-covariance, position-averaged surrogate and verify that the implementation attains its analytic minimum. The evaluated head-shared pairwise configuration does not improve accuracy in the tested dynamic W4A4KV4 setting. Across four checkpoints, replacing the full-head Hadamard with this configuration increases perplexity at both short and long context lengths. Composing the pairwise rotation with the Hadamard satisfies the selected $\pm0.05$-PPL interval criterion under the default estimator. Estimating the shared angle from K alone improves pairwise-only on every checkpoint but does not close its gap to full-head mixing. The analytic objective controls a position-averaged second moment of a pooled calibration covariance, whereas the dynamic quantiser sets its step from a tokenwise group range. The pairwise transform also has only two-channel mixing support. Along a controlled interpolation from two-channel to full-head mixing, K range, relative quantisation error, and perplexity degradation decrease as support increases. These results show that optimality for a structured surrogate need not reduce quantisation error when the surrogate and mixing support are misaligned with the quantiser's scale-setting statistic.

</details>


### [342] [Reduced Matrix Multiplication: Input-Adaptive Matrix-Product Reduction for LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13426)
*Zixuan Lan,Yanhong Li,Jiawei Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RMM（Reduced Matrix Multiplication），一种无需训练、输入自适应的推理加速方法，通过在收缩维度上选择信息丰富的切片来减少Transformer矩阵乘法计算，在不修改模型权重的前提下实现精度-效率的平滑权衡，并在多种模型规模、任务类型和多模态场景下验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer语言模型推理成本高昂，主要源于反复进行的高维矩阵乘法运算。现有加速方法往往需要重新训练、修改模型权重或引入额外开销，缺乏一种训练无关、输入自适应的轻量级推理优化方案，因此需要探索更简单有效的推理加速途径。

Method: RMM的核心思想是在矩阵乘法的收缩维度上选择信息丰富的切片，跳过冗余计算部分。该方法无需修改模型权重，通过一个简单的保留率（retention ratio）控制参数来调节计算量与精度之间的权衡，实现平滑可预测的精度-效率折中。作者还开发了自定义GPU内核以实现实际运行时的加速。

Result: 实验覆盖1B到70B参数的语言模型，发现缩减容忍度取决于模型家族、任务、组件和保留率，且通常随模型规模增大而提升。在适度缩减下，RMM在判别任务、自回归生成和长上下文设定中保持稳健，并成功扩展到多模态视觉-语言推理。机械消融揭示Transformer内部存在结构不对称性：注意力侧计算比MLP组件更可缩减。A100上的壁钟基准显示计算节省可转化为实际运行加速，尤其在长序列场景下。

Conclusion: RMM为输入自适应的推理时优化提供了一个可扩展的方向，其训练无关、权重不改的特性使其具有广泛的适用性。结构不对称性的发现也为未来Transformer架构设计和推理优化提供了新的洞见，即注意力计算和MLP计算在可缩减性上存在本质差异。

Abstract: Transformer-based language models achieve strong performance but incur substantial inference cost due to repeated high-dimensional matrix multiplications. We propose Reduced Matrix Multiplication (RMM), a training-free, input-adaptive inference method that reduces Transformer matrix products by selecting informative slices along their contraction dimensions, without modifying model weights. Under a simple retention-ratio control, RMM provides a smooth and predictable accuracy-efficiency trade-off. Across language models ranging from 1B to 70B parameters, we find that reduction tolerance depends on the model family, task, component, and retention ratio, although it often improves with model scale. Under moderate reduction, RMM remains robust across the evaluated discriminative, autoregressive generation, and long-context settings. We further show that the same principle extends to multimodal vision-language inference. Mechanistic ablations reveal a structural asymmetry within Transformers: attention-side computations are substantially more reducible than MLP components. Finally, wall-clock benchmarks with custom kernels on an NVIDIA A100 show that these computational savings can translate into practical runtime gains, especially at longer sequence lengths. Together, these results position RMM as a scalable direction for input-adaptive inference-time optimization.

</details>


### [343] [Symmetry-Breaking De Novo Crystal Generation via Markovian Jump Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13457)
*Van Khoa Nguyen,Alexandros Kalousis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于对称性破缺的晶体扩散生成框架（SbCD），通过从最低对称性先验出发、利用马尔可夫跳跃-扩散过程逆转对称性破缺动力学，从而生成完整晶体结构规格，在MP20和MPTS-52数据集上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有晶体生成模型只能生成到位点对称性级别，并依赖从经验分布中采样空间群，无法捕获全局对称性和结构依赖关系，限制了生成晶体的完整性。

Method: 受物理学中自发对称性破缺启发，提出从最低对称性先验出发逆转生成晶体；利用马尔可夫跳跃-扩散过程建模对称性破缺动力学，使生成过程能够在不同空间群之间以物理上合理的方式切换，显式地将空间群间转换纳入生成过程。

Result: 在MP20和MPTS-52数据集上的从头生成实验中，SbCD相比其保持对称性的基线方法取得了大幅性能提升。

Conclusion: SbCD提供了一种有前景的晶体材料生成建模新视角，通过物理启发的对称性破缺机制实现更完整的晶体结构生成。

Abstract: Generating crystals has recently attracted significant interest due to their broad applications in materials science. However, existing generative models struggle to produce complete crystallographic specifications, limiting their ability to capture global symmetry and structural dependencies. In particular, current state-of-the-art approaches generate crystals only up to site symmetries and rely on sampling space groups from empirical distributions during generation. Inspired by \emph{spontaneous symmetry breaking} in physics, where crystals break symmetries under external conditions, we propose a novel diffusion-based framework that generates full structure specifications by reversing from the lowest-symmetry priors. Our method leverages a Markovian jump-diffusion process to model these symmetry-breaking dynamics, enabling it to traverse different space groups in a physically motivated manner. Our model, dubbed \emph{Symmetry-breaking Crystal Diffusion} (SbCD), introduces a principled approach to explicitly incorporate inter-space-group transitions into the generative process. In de novo generation experiments on MP20 and MPTS-52, SbCD outperforms its symmetry-preserving counterpart by a substantial margin, offering a promising perspective for generative modeling of crystalline materials.

</details>


### [344] [Doubly Robust Estimation of Causal Effect on CVR with Targeted Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13461)
*Jiayi Dan,Bo Li,Lu Deng,Yong Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从半参数理论视角重新审视链式结构（如CVR）的因果效应估计问题，提出一个新的双重稳健（doubly robust）因果效应估计器，其收敛速度优于nuisance参数估计，对神经网络等灵活非参数估计器更稳健，并基于目标正则化框架提升数值稳定性与实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CVR（点击后转化率）是电商和广告中的关键指标。直接对点击样本应用现有因果推断方法会引入样本选择偏差与方差增大问题。虽然近期研究提出"理想损失"方法来无偏估计全样本损失，但损失的无偏性并不能保证最终估计器的无偏性。因此需要从更严谨的理论视角重新设计估计器。

Method: 1. 从半参数理论出发，针对CVR这类链式结构结果变量开发新的双重稳健因果效应估计器；2. 详细推导其理论性质，证明其收敛速度优于nuisance参数估计；3. 基于目标正则化设计框架，改善数值稳定性和实际应用性。

Result: 在合成数据和真实数据上的大量实验验证了方法的有效性与稳健性。实验还发现，朴素地将损失去偏与标准因果估计器结合的效果不如本文方法，凸显了针对CVR目标定制新估计器并具备扎实理论保证的必要性。

Conclusion: 针对CVR这类链式结构因果效应估计，仅靠损失去偏是不够的，需要从半参数理论出发设计专门的双重稳健估计器，才能同时保证理论性质和实际性能。

Abstract: Post-click conversion rate (CVR) is a key metric in various scenarios including e-commerce and advertising, reflecting the efficiency and user experience in the second stage of the conversion process. Estimating the causal effect on CVR is therefore of great practical importance. However, directly applying existing causal inference methods to clicked samples introduces sample selection bias and increased variance due to the exclusion of non-click data. Recent studies on CVR prediction introduce "ideal loss", which optimizes model parameters using an unbiased estimate of the loss over the full sample. Nevertheless, there is no guarantee that unbiasedness of the loss implies unbiasedness of the final estimator.
  We revisit this challenge from the perspective of semiparametric theory. Specifically, we develop a new doubly robust causal effect estimator for chain-structured outcomes such as CVR, and derive its theoretical properties in detail. It achieves a faster convergence rate compared to nuisance parameters estimation and is therefore more robust when using flexible nonparametric estimators, including neural networks. Based on these theoretical findings, we further design a framework based on targeted regularization to improve numerical stability and practical applicability.
  Extensive experiments on synthetic and real-world data demonstrate the effectiveness and robustness of our method. In addition, we find that naively combining loss debiasing with standard causal estimators underperforms our method, highlighting the necessity of developing the new estimator tailored to this CVR-style objective with solid theoretical guarantees.

</details>


### [345] [Concept Drift Detection and Adaptive Retraining of Malware Classification Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13465)
*Christofer Washington Berruz Chungata,Martin Jurecek,Katerina Potika,William B. Andreopoulos,Mark Stamp*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究恶意软件分类模型中的概念漂移检测问题，比较了三种检测方法（基于OCSVM的新方法、基于MB-Means的已有方法、以及MMD统计方法），并结合四种学习模型在三种训练场景（静态、周期、漂移感知）下进行大量实验。结果表明三种检测方法在分类准确率上与周期性重训练相当，但训练效率显著更高，其中OCSVM方法整体表现最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 恶意软件检测模型特别容易受到概念漂移的影响，因为攻击者不断修改现有恶意软件。随着时间推移，数据的统计特性发生变化，导致基于历史数据训练的模型性能下降。因此需要有效的概念漂移检测机制来自动识别何时需要重新训练模型，以在保持准确率的同时提高训练效率。

Method: 提出了基于One-Class Support Vector Machines (OCSVM)的概念漂移检测新方法，并与Minibatch K-Means (MK-Means)和Maximum Mean Discrepancy (MMD)方法进行比较。实验覆盖四种学习模型（MLP、Random Forest、SVM、XGBoost），在静态、周期性和漂移感知三种场景下评估，并使用Pareto Front分析权衡准确率与训练效率。

Result: 三种概念漂移检测技术均能达到与周期性重训练相当的分类准确率，同时在需要重训练的模型数量上具有显著更高的效率。基于OCSVM的漂移感知重训练方法整体上优于MK-Means和MMD方法。

Conclusion: 研究提供了有力证据表明可以有效检测恶意软件分类模型中的概念漂移，且OCSVM方法在准确率和效率的权衡上表现最佳，为实际部署中的自适应模型重训练策略提供了参考。

Abstract: Concept drift refers to changes over time in the statistical properties of data, as compared to the data that was used to train a learning model. Machine learning models for malware detection or classification are particularly susceptible to performance degradation caused by concept drift, as attackers constantly modify existing malware. In this chapter, we analyze two machine learning-based approaches to automated concept drift detection-a novel approach based on One-Class Support Vector Machines (OCSVM) and a previously-studied technique based on Minibatch K-Means (MK-Means). For comparison we also consider Maximum Mean Discrepancy (MMD), a statistical technique for detecting changes in multidimensional data. We conduct an extensive series of experiments comparing the effectiveness of four learning models, namely, Multilayer Perceptron, Random Forest, Support Vector Machines, and eXtreme Gradient Boosting. For each of these models, we consider three distinct scenarios: A static scenario where no model retraining occurs, a periodic scenario where models are constantly retrained irrespective of concept drift, and a drift-aware scenario where models are only retrained when concept drift is detected. Under the drift-aware scenario, we analyze the tradeoff between accuracy and training efficiency using Pareto Front analysis. We find that all three concept drift detection techniques achieve classification accuracy comparable to periodic retraining, while offering substantially greater efficiency in terms of the number of models that must be retrained. In addition, drift-aware retraining based on our OCSVM technique generally outperforms the MK-Means and MMD approaches. Overall, these results provide strong evidence that we can accurately detect concept drift in malware classification models.

</details>


### [346] [Active-Trace Complexity Bounds for Moreau--Yosida Unadjusted Langevin Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13467)
*Yuchen Xin,Zhihua Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文分析了求解非光滑复合目标分布的Moreau-Yosida非调整Langevin算法（MYULA）的离散化误差。作者发现MYULA的主导离散化误差由参考活动迹B_ref控制，而非全局曲率界d/λ。针对分段线性、lasso、组和全变差等结构化惩罚，曲率-管估计使B_ref独立于λ，从而将精度依赖性从Õ(ε⁻³)提升至Õ(ε⁻²)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非光滑复合目标分布（如f+g形式）在贝叶斯统计和机器学习中广泛出现，其中g通常是非光滑的。MYULA通过Moreau包络平滑g来实现采样，但传统的离散化误差分析依赖于全局曲率界d/λ，这导致需要大量迭代次数。作者希望通过更精细的"参考活动迹"概念来改进误差控制，尤其针对结构化惩罚函数获得更优的复杂度保证。

Method: 论文首先将MYULA的离散化误差与沿着热子步的参考活动迹B_ref（即a_λ的平均值）建立联系，取代传统的全局曲率上界d/λ。然后利用Moreau包络的弱Hessian的迹信息以及曲率-管估计（curvature-tube estimates），针对特定的结构化惩罚（分段线性、lasso、组、全变差）推导出B_ref与λ无关的上界，从而改进复杂度分析。

Result: 主要结果包括：(1) 建立MYULA的迭代复杂度N的上界，其中离散化误差由B_ref控制；(2) 证明Moreau偏置界√m W₂(π_λ,π)≤G²λ/4，从而通过选择λ≍ε/G²获得端到端保证；(3) 通用估计B_ref≤d/λ给出Õ(ε⁻³)精度依赖性，而对结构化惩罚则获得Õ(ε⁻²)的最优精度依赖性。

Conclusion: 本文通过精细化分析MYULA的离散化误差，证明了对于结构化非光滑惩罚，经典MYULA核可以获得与光滑情形相当的最优精度依赖性Õ(ε⁻²)，而非传统的Õ(ε⁻³)。这为该类算法的实际应用提供了更坚实的理论基础。

Abstract: We study the Moreau--Yosida unadjusted Langevin algorithm (MYULA) for the nonsmooth composite target \[ π(dx)\propto \exp\{-f(x)-g(x)\}\,dx, \qquad x\in\mathbb R^d, \] where \(f\) is \(m\)-strongly convex with \(L_f\)-Lipschitz gradient and \(g\) is convex and \(G\)-Lipschitz. Let \(g_λ\) be the Moreau envelope of \(g\), \(π_λ\) the corresponding smoothed target, and \(a_λ=\operatorname{tr}H_λ\), where \(H_λ\) is the a.e./weak Hessian of \(g_λ\). We show that the leading MYULA discretization error is controlled by the reference active trace \(B_{\mathrm{ref}}\), the average of \(a_λ\) along the heat substep of one MYULA update started from \(π_λ\), rather than by the global curvature bound \(d/λ\). If \(M_λ\) is an a.e. upper bound for \(a_λ\), then, up to logarithmic factors, \[ N \lesssim \frac{1}{m} \left[ L_f + \frac{ τ_f+G^2+B_{\mathrm{ref}} }{ \varepsilon_{\mathrm{alg}}^2 } + \frac{M_λ}{\varepsilon_{\mathrm{alg}}} \right], \qquad τ_f:= \sup_x\operatorname{tr}\nabla^2 f(x), \] iterations suffice to ensure \(\sqrt m\,W_2(μ_N,π_λ)\leq\varepsilon_{\mathrm{alg}}\), where \(μ_N\) is the law of the \(N\)-th iterate and \(W_2\) is the quadratic Wasserstein distance. We also prove the Moreau-bias bound \[ \sqrt m\,W_2(π_λ,π) \leq \frac{G^2λ}{4}. \] Thus, choosing \(λ\asymp\varepsilon/G^2\) gives an end-to-end guarantee for \(π\). The universal estimate \(B_{\mathrm{ref}}\leq d/λ\) yields \(\widetilde O(\varepsilon^{-3})\) accuracy dependence. For the structured piecewise-linear, lasso-type, group, and total-variation penalties considered here, curvature--tube estimates make \(B_{\mathrm{ref}}\) independent of \(λ\), yielding \(\widetilde O(\varepsilon^{-2})\) for the same classical MYULA kernel.

</details>


### [347] [Synthetic Persona Pretraining: Alignment from Token Zero](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13482)
*Julian Minder,Viktor Moskvoretskii,Raghav Singhal,Difan Jiao,Andy Arditi,Shaobo Cui,Yiderigun Borjigin,Kartik Bali,Stefan Krsteski,Harsh Raj,Huu Nguyen,Jannik Brinkmann,Ashton Anderson,Roland Aydin,Robert West*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出合成人格预训练(SPP)方法，在预训练初始阶段就将期望的助手人格植入模型，而非在预训练后添加。通过在预训练文档上标注符合价值观的第一人称反思，并在预训练中同时学习原始文档与反思，再通过后训练将人格绑定到助手身份。实验表明SPP能提升宪法遵循、越狱鲁棒性，降低分布外道德困境中的错位率，同时保持能力，且早干预效果优于晚干预。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI对齐通常在预训练后通过后训练引入，这使得价值观像"薄覆盖层"而非深层根基，容易导致后续错位。作者质疑这种范式，主张应该在预训练从第一个token起就建立期望助手人格，使价值观深植于模型内部。

Method: SPP包含三步：(1) 用规范性价值宪法对预训练文档标注对齐价值观的第一人称反思；(2) 用标准交叉熵损失同时预训练原始文档与反思，在众多人格中植入期望人格；(3) 用用户-助手对话数据后训练，将期望人格绑定到助手身份（人格绑定）。

Result: 在多达3B参数、500B token的预训练中，SPP提升宪法遵循和越狱鲁棒性，降低分布外道德困境的错位率且不损害能力。与token零对齐相比，仅在预训练结束时引入SPP则宪法遵循更弱、价值优先级不改变、困境选择更不对齐。该优势依赖人格绑定，且随预训练预算增加而增强。

Conclusion: 早期塑造价值观对对齐至关重要，预训练阶段的人格干预是有效的对齐手段。实验证据表明，价值观在预训练早期植入的效果远优于后期叠加，这为LLM对齐提供了新的范式方向。

Abstract: As language-model-based AI is increasingly deployed in autonomous settings, aligning its goals and values with those of humans becomes critical. Today, alignment, and the assistant identity itself, are typically introduced only after pretraining, once behavioral priors are already established. This can make values a thin overlay, rather than deeply rooted, and facilitate subsequent misalignment. Pursuing a different paradigm, we introduce Synthetic Persona Pretraining (SPP), which installs the desired assistant persona from token zero in pretraining. First, we annotate pretraining documents with value-aligned first-person reflections derived from a normative value constitution. Second, we pretrain via the standard cross-entropy loss on standard pretraining documents as well as their reflections, which installs the desired persona among a multitude of other personas. Finally, we post-train on user-assistant dialogue data, which binds this desired persona to the assistant identity, a process we call persona binding. By pretraining models up to 3B parameters on 500B tokens, we show that SPP improves constitution following and jailbreak robustness, and reduces the misalignment rate in out-of-distribution moral dilemmas, while preserving capabilities. Early intervention matters: compared with alignment from token zero, introducing SPP only at the end of pretraining yields weaker constitution adherence, does not shift value priorities, and leads to less aligned choices in dilemmas. This advantage depends on persona binding and, importantly, increases with pretraining budget. Overall, our results show that shaping values early is critical for alignment and establish pretraining-time persona interventions as an effective approach to do so.

</details>


### [348] [Sparse Orthogonal Regression Technique: A Spectral Framework for Equation Discovery, Approximation, and Integration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13504)
*Sabin Roman,Ljupco Todorovski,Saso Dzeroski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出稀疏正交回归技术(SORT)，一种用于从噪声和不规则采样数据中学习正交基展开的稀疏谱框架。SORT通过L1正则化回归直接估计展开系数，避免了显式求积或解析内积计算，核心应用是数据驱动的常微分方程发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的符号回归、语法发现和SINDy等稀疏识别方法依赖于有限的候选函数库，当库中缺少问题特定的非线性项时模型可能失败。作者希望提供一种互补路线：先恢复紧凑的谱表示，再指导搜索更简单的解析形式，将问题从对通用项的脆弱选择转变为对问题域自适应的基设计。

Method: SORT的核心是将向量场表示为所选正交基中的稀疏系数展开，通过L1正则化回归直接从观测数据估计展开系数，无需显式求积或解析内积计算。该框架支持从原始观测中直接学习。

Result: 在动力学系统实验中，当基适应问题时SORT匹配或优于基于库的稀疏回归基线；在稀疏采样、噪声导数和表示失配下表现出更稳定的退化。实验显示低阶主导系数随模型阶数增加而保持，支持阶数一致的模型增长。同一学习展开还支持非线性逼近和复杂高维积分的估计。

Conclusion: SORT提供了一种可复用的中间表示，用于系统识别、逼近和积分，同时将基设计作为科学建模问题的显式组成部分。它不是对失配免疫，但将问题从对通用项的脆弱选择转变为对问题域自适应的基设计。

Abstract: We develop the Sparse Orthogonal Regression Technique (SORT), a sparse spectral framework for learning orthonormal-basis expansions from noisy and irregularly sampled data. SORT estimates expansion coefficients directly from observations using L1-regularized regression, avoiding explicit quadrature or analytic inner-product evaluation. The central application is data-driven discovery of ordinary differential equations: vector fields are represented in chosen orthogonal bases and learned as sparse coefficient expansions. This provides a complementary route to symbolic regression, grammar-based discovery, and SINDy-style sparse identification by first recovering a compact spectral representation, which can later guide searches for simpler analytic forms. Across the dynamical-system experiments, SORT matches or improves upon library-based sparse-regression baselines when the basis is well adapted to the problem, and shows more stable degradation under sparse sampling, noisy derivative estimates, and representation mismatch. Specific examples illustrate why this representation is useful: if a finite library misses the problem-specific nonlinearity, the resulting model can fail. SORT is not immune to mismatch, but it shifts the problem away from brittle selection among generic terms to basis design adapted to the problem domain. The experiments also show that dominant low-order coefficients persist as model order increases, supporting order-consistent model growth. Beyond equation discovery, the same learned expansion supports nonlinear approximation and estimation of complex, high-dimensional integrals by coefficient readout. Overall, SORT provides a reusable intermediate representation for system identification, approximation, and integration, while making basis design an explicit part of the scientific modeling problem.

</details>


### [349] [Intern-S2-Preview: Scientific Agentic Foundation Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13505)
*Lei Bai,Jiaqi Cao,Chiyu Chen,Guanzhou Chen,Kai Chen,Guangran Cheng,Erfei Cui,Xuanlang Dai,Shengyuan Ding,Shangheng Du,Yanhui Duan,Yue Fan,Youqing Fang,Quan Gan,Yuanyuan Gao,Jiaye Ge,Lixin Gu,Yuzhe Gu,Qipeng Guo,Junjun He,Xin Hong,Ming Hu,Zhouqi Hua,Haian Huang,Junhao Huang,Zixian Huang,Minxi Jin,Lingkai Kong,Alexander Lam,Zehao Li,Zonglin Li,Tianhao Liang,Dahua Lin,Junyao Lin,Tianyang Lin,Zhouhan Lin,Jiangning Liu,Jin Liu,Kuikun Liu,Wenran Liu,Yifei Liu,Yuhong Liu,Yuhong Liu,Zhoumianze Liu,Ziyan Liu,Ziyu Liu,Haijun Lv,Han Lv,Chengqi Lyu,Le Ma,Ningsheng Ma,Zerun Ma,Haoyang Peng,Runyu Peng,Jifei Shan,Zixin Shang,Kou Shi,Xiang Shi,Qisheng Su,Xuerui Su,Hao Sun,Xiao Sun,Yanan Sun,Yu Sun,Huanze Tang,Yinghao Tang,Wenhui Tian,Zhongbo Tian,Bingli Wang,Haomin Wang,Jiarui Wang,Jingzhi Wang,Rui Wang,Xiquan Wang,Yi Wang,Zhecan Wang,Ziyi Wang,Zun Wang,Rubin Wei,Lianyi Wu,Wen Wu,Yue Wu,Yuhan Wu,Zhenyu Wu,Zijian Wu,Shuhao Xing,Jun Xu,Xingle Xu,Xuenan Xu,Xiangchao Yan,Ziang Yan,Bowen Yang,Danni Yang,Lin Yang,Zhiqi Yang,Qian Yao,Haochen Ye,Peng Ye,Jinhui Yin,Jiashuo Yu,Dingbo Yuan,Fei Yuan,Yuhang Zang,Bo Zhang,Chao Zhang,Chen Zhang,Hongjie Zhang,Junming Zhang,Wenlong Zhang,Wenwei Zhang,Yiming Zhang,Zhuo Zhang,Ziyang Zhang,Haiteng Zhao,Penghao Zhao,Yibo Zhao,Zhonghan Zhao,Zhihang Zhong,Bowen Zhou,Peiheng Zhou,Xin Zhou,Xinyu Zhou,Yunhua Zhou,Dongsheng Zhu,Yicheng Zou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Intern-S2-Preview系列科学智能体基础模型，通过科学多模态预训练和统一后训练流程（包括监督微调、多任务强化学习、黑盒/白盒智能体RL和在线蒸馏），支持多模态科学理解、推理、生成和长时程任务。397B参数版本在多个基准上取得领先结果，附加的Memory Decoder路径可在不修改冻结主干的前提下快速实现科学领域特化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学发现日益需要AI系统能够对异构模态的科学证据进行推理、与科学工具和环境交互，并在长任务时间跨度上维持进展。现有模型在这些方面仍存在不足，需要专门的科学智能体基础模型来支撑这些能力。

Method: 训练流程分为两阶段：(1) 科学多模态预训练，基于渲染的科学文档、交错图文数据和多样化科学语料；(2) 统一后训练流程，包括监督微调、可扩展多任务强化学习、黑盒和白盒智能体RL以及同策略蒸馏。配套技术包括部分rollout与离策略校正、自适应长度正则化、在线投机解码、稳健多任务优化和轨迹感知的经验组装。架构上，397B模型扩展了时序建模能力（从长序列理解到数值预测），并单独研究了Memory Decoder作为记忆增强路径。

Result: 在科学、多模态、智能体和通用基准上的评估显示，Intern-S2-Preview-397B在多个设置下取得了具有竞争力或领先的结果。时序模块提升了在SciTS上的科学信号理解和预测能力；独立的Intern-MemDec-4B扩展在不修改冻结的397B主干的情况下，将Biology-Instructions平均得分从56.92提升到60.32。

Conclusion: Intern-S2-Preview系列展示了科学智能体基础模型在异构证据推理、工具交互和长时程任务上的可行性。Memory Decoder路径验证了在不改变大规模主干模型的前提下快速实现科学领域特化的有效方案，为科学AI的模块化发展提供了新思路。

Abstract: Scientific discovery increasingly requires AI systems that can reason over scientific evidence of heterogeneous modalities, interact with scientific tools and environments, and sustain progress across long task horizons. We present Intern-S2-Preview, a series of scientific agentic foundation models designed to support multimodal scientific understanding, reasoning, generation, and long-horizon tasks. The training pipeline begins with scientific multimodal pre-training over rendered scientific documents, interleaved image-text data, and diverse scientific corpora. Starting from the pretrained checkpoint, we apply a unified post-training pipeline consisting of supervised fine-tuning, scalable multi-task reinforcement learning (RL), black- and white-box agentic RL, and on-policy distillation. This pipeline is supported by practical techniques that improve rollout and training stability and efficiency, including partial rollout with off-policy correction, adaptive length regularization, online speculative decoding, robust multi-task optimization, and trace-aware experience assembly for agentic tasks. At the architecture level, Intern-S2-Preview-397B extends time series modelling from efficient long-sequence understanding to numerical forecasting, while Memory Decoder is studied as a separate memory-augmented path for rapid scientific specialization without modifying the frozen 397B backbone. Evaluations across scientific, multimodal, agentic, and general-purpose benchmarks show that Intern-S2-Preview-397B achieves competitive or leading results in multiple settings. The time series modules improve scientific signal understanding and forecasting on SciTS, while the separate Intern-MemDec-4B extension improves the Biology-Instructions average score from 56.92 to 60.32 without modifying the frozen 397B backbone.

</details>


### [350] [Intervention-Aware Clinical World Model for Post-Op Outcome Forecasting in Cardiology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13518)
*Yunsung Chung,Yingshuo Liu,Abboud F. Hassan,Han Feng,Mary M. Maleckar,Nassir Marrouche,Jihun Hamm*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个干预感知的临床世界模型，将患者表示为结构化潜在状态，并通过时间排序的术后事件动态演化该状态，应用于房颤消融术后90天恢复窗口内的复发风险预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有临床预测模型将术后结局视为从基线测量到未来终点的单步映射，忽略了恢复过程中异步记录的临床观察、药物变化、重复干预和生理测量等不规则轨迹对风险评估的动态影响。

Method: 模型首先将基线影像编码为3D空间潜在状态，然后利用手术上下文、静态协变量、经过时间和围事件生理嵌入来更新该状态。随访影像仅通过潜在预测目标提供训练监督（推理时不需要随访MRI强度）。

Result: 在DECAAF-II数据集上重复内部交叉验证中，复发预测AUROC达0.756、AUPRC达0.777；疤痕范围MAE为2.971个百分点。学习到的状态支持不同时间范围的复发风险查询以及空白期记录的回顾性输入编辑。

Conclusion: 不规则术后记录中的过程性信息为长期复发风险提供了临床意义的证据，该世界模型能在推理时无需随访MRI的情况下实现准确的复发预测和疤痕范围估计。

Abstract: Many clinical prediction models treat post-intervention outcomes as a one-step mapping from baseline measurements to a future endpoint. However, recovery after a procedure often unfolds as an irregular trajectory: clinical observations, medication changes, repeat interventions, and physiological measurements are recorded asynchronously and can change risk assessment over time. We propose an intervention-aware clinical world model that represents each patient with a structured latent state and evolves it through time-ordered post-intervention events. The model first encodes baseline imaging into a 3D spatial latent state. It then updates this state using procedural context, static covariates, elapsed time, and peri-event physiological embeddings. Follow-up imaging provides training-only supervision through a latent forecasting objective. We apply the framework to atrial fibrillation ablation. During the 90-day recovery window, irregular post-procedure records provide clinically meaningful evidence for long-term recurrence risk. In repeated internal cross-validation on DECAAF-II, our model achieves AUROC 0.756 and AUPRC 0.777 for recurrence prediction. It also achieves a scar-extent MAE of 2.971 percentage points without requiring follow-up MRI intensities at inference. The learned state supports recurrence-risk queries at different horizons and retrospective input editing of blanking-period records.

</details>


### [351] [The data geometry of masking diffusion: Certified-optimal schedules via unmasking growth complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13520)
*Martin J. Wainwright*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了离散采样的掩码扩散（masking diffusion），提出了一个称为"解掩码增长复杂度"（UGC）的路径级数据几何度量。其局部增量直接控制KL离散化误差，为Bernoulli子集和固定基数两种解掩码方案提供了统一分析。在log-reveal-odds坐标下可优化单块和多块调度方案，并将UGC增量估计与KL增量联系起来，从而构建了认证最优的采样器，其迭代复杂度在常数因子内达到oracle过程水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散扩散模型的采样效率高度依赖于解掩码（unmasking）调度策略，而现有分析缺乏一个统一的理论框架来理解不同调度方案下的KL误差行为，也无法量化调度策略相对于数据几何的自适应收益。

Method: 提出UGC（Unmasking Growth Complexity）作为数据几何的路径级度量；利用log-reveal-odds坐标进行单块和多块调度优化；通过耦合揭示轨迹上的KL增量从样本中估计UGC增量；在精细划分极限下，UGC密度的平方根积分的平方决定最优Euler离散化误差。

Result: 推导出保证高概率KL误差的认证最优采样器，其迭代复杂度在常数因子内匹配oracle过程；建立了UGC与传统多元依赖度量及先前离散扩散复杂度的联系；展示了常数个自适应放置块即可实现约Ω(√d)维度的显著增益。

Conclusion: UGC提供了一个统一的、可估计的数据几何框架，用于理解和优化离散掩码扩散的调度策略，从理论上证明了自适应调度相对于粗粒度固定调度的维度级优势。

Abstract: We study masking diffusion for discrete sampling and introduce a path-resolved measure of data geometry called the \emph{unmasking growth complexity} ({\textsf{UGC}\xspace}). Its local increments directly control Kullback--Leibler (KL) discretization error, yielding a unified analysis of Bernoulli-subset and fixed-cardinality unmasking schemes. In log-reveal-odds coordinates, this structure yields optimized single-block and multi-block schedules, and quantifies the gains from adapting computational effort to data geometry. Crucially, we show how {\textsf{UGC}\xspace} increments can be estimated from samples via KL increments along coupled reveal trajectories. This leads to \emph{certified-optimal} samplers that achieve a prescribed KL error with high probability and iteration complexity within a constant factor of the corresponding oracle procedure. Collapsing the \ugc path yields the aggregate {\textsf{UGC}\xspace} mass, which connects to classical multivariate dependence measures and complexity measures from previous analyses of discrete diffusion. In the fine-partition limit, the squared integral of the square-root {\textsf{UGC}\xspace} density determines the sharp leading-order optimal Euler discretization error. Examples exhibit substantial dimension-dependent gains over coarse schedules, including $\widetildeΩ(\sqrt{d})$ improvements achievable with a constant number of adaptively placed blocks.

</details>


### [352] [DARTree: Speculative Diffusion Decoding with Autoregressive Draft Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13524)
*Tianyi Li,Yaxin Luo,Xinyi Shang,Zhiqiang Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DARTree是一种无需训练的投机解码方法，将预训练的自回归（AR）修正头从链扩展到树结构，通过批量展开固定宽度候选树并结合最佳优先剪枝选择验证树，在数学、代码和聊天等七个基准上实现最高平均接受长度和加速比，最高可达9.73倍无损加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有投机解码方法存在局限：扩散式起草者预测整个token块，但其位置分布是边缘分布而非沿草稿路径的条件分布；现有的循环修正只在单条草稿链上携带因果信息，而扩散式树构建虽扩大了候选覆盖但未沿各分支传递因果修正。需要一种方法结合两者的优势。

Method: DARTree首先构造固定宽度的候选树，在单一批次中同时展开并评分每层所有节点；然后仅使用最佳优先剪枝来选择验证树，将AR头推理与顺序堆操作解耦。该方法无需训练，直接扩展预训练的AR修正头从链到树结构。

Result: 在七个数学、代码和聊天基准上，DARTree在四种模型-温度配置中均取得最高平均接受长度和加速比，每轮验证最高接受12.97个token，比DFlash多98.6%，比Domino多27.9%，相对本地自回归解码达到9.73倍无损加速。

Conclusion: DARTree通过将AR修正头从链扩展至树，有效结合了扩散式草稿者的并行块预测优势与循环修正的因果信息，实现了无需训练的、高效且无损的投机解码加速。

Abstract: Speculative decoding losslessly accelerates autoregressive language models by verifying multiple draft tokens in parallel. Diffusion-based drafters further reduce proposal latency by predicting an entire token block in parallel, but their position-wise distributions are marginal rather than conditioned on tokens selected along each draft path. Existing recurrent correction incorporates causal information along a single draft chain, whereas diffusion-based tree construction broadens candidate coverage without carrying this correction along individual branches. We introduce DARTree, a training-free speculative decoding method that extends a pretrained AR correction head from chains to trees. DARTree first constructs a fixed-width candidate tree by expanding and scoring all nodes at each depth in a single batch, and then only applies best-first pruning to select the verification tree, decoupling AR-head inference from sequential heap operations. Across seven math, code, and chat benchmarks, DARTree achieves the highest average acceptance length and speedup in all four model--temperature configurations, accepting up to 12.97 tokens per verification round, 98.6\% more than DFlash and 27.9\% more than Domino in the same setting, and reaching up to 9.73$\times$ lossless speedup over locally measured autoregressive decoding.

</details>


### [353] [Exponential Convex Calibration Dimension for the Multi-Label Jaccard Measure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13549)
*Mingyuan Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了多标签分类和二元分割中每实例Jaccard分数（IoU）损失矩阵的代数结构与校准性质。作者证明了Jaccard分数、平移损失和普通损失矩阵在约定Jac(∅,∅)=1下均为非奇异矩阵，其损失列仿射维数为2^s-1。在精确校准方面，给出了CCdim的下界2^{s-1}和上界2^s-1，证明任何恰好校准的凸代理都需要指数数量的预测坐标。同时，论文提供了两个多项式维度的近似保证：一个F1到Jaccard的转换实现了渐进Jaccard遗憾上界3-2√2，另一个MinHash平方损失代理可对任意条件标签分布实现均匀的Jaccard遗憾界α，直接构造维度为O((s²+s·log(1/ρ))/α²)，带符号变体维度为O((s+log(1/ρ))/α²)。核心结论是：零遗憾校准需要指数维度，而任何固定加性遗憾容差只需多项式预测维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Jaccard分数（IoU）是多标签分类和语义分割中最广泛使用的评估指标之一，但其损失矩阵的结构特性及其与校准凸代理之间的关系尚未被系统研究。理解损失矩阵的代数结构（非奇异性和仿射维数）是深入分析校准性质和代理损失设计的理论基础。此前工作主要关注F1等指标的代理损失，对Jaccard指标缺乏全面的校准分析，特别是精确校准所需的预测维度尚未被刻画。

Method: 论文采用以下技术方法：(1) 利用有限MinHash Gram表示与布尔Möbius反演相结合，证明Jaccard分数、平移损失和普通损失矩阵的非奇异性及仿射维数；(2) 通过构造因子加权分布（含2^{s-1}+1个支持结果和贝叶斯最优报告）建立精确校准维度的下界；(3) 提出F1到Jaccard的遗憾转换方法，将现有(s²+1)维F1代理转化为多项式时间规则；(4) 设计基于MinHash的平方损失代理及其带符号变体，实现均匀遗憾界。

Result: 主要结果包括：(1) 在Jac(∅,∅)=1约定下，Jaccard分数损失矩阵非奇异，损失列仿射维数为2^s-1；(2) 精确校准维度满足2^{s-1}≤CCdim(L^Jac)≤2^s-1，意味着任何恰好校准的凸代理需要指数维坐标；(3) F1到Jaccard转换使渐进Jaccard遗憾达到3-2√2（约0.1716）；(4) MinHash平方损失代理对任意α>0和0<ρ<1实现均匀Jaccard遗憾界α，直接构造维度O((s²+s log(1/ρ))/α²)，带符号变体维度O((s+log(1/ρ))/α²)；(5) 建立了零遗憾校准需指数维度、固定加性遗憾容差只需多项式维度的二分律。

Conclusion: 本文完整刻画了Jaccard分数损失矩阵的代数结构，并在校准维度问题上建立了清晰的"指数-多项式"二分律：追求零遗憾的精确校准不可避免地需要指数维预测，而实际中通常可接受的固定加性遗憾容差则只需多项式维。这一理论结果为多标签分类中Jaccard指标代理损失的设计提供了坚实的理论指导，表明在实际应用中应优先考虑多项式维度的近似校准方法而非精确校准。

Abstract: The per-instance Jaccard score, or intersection over union (IoU), is standard in multi-label classification and binary segmentation. With $s$ labels, its loss matrix has $2^s$ outcomes and reports. Under the convention $\mathrm{Jac}(\varnothing,\varnothing)=1$, we prove that the Jaccard score, shifted-loss, and ordinary loss matrices are nonsingular and that the loss columns have affine dimension $2^s-1$. The proof combines a finite MinHash Gram representation with Boolean Möbius inversion. For exact calibration, we prove $2^{s-1} \leq \mathrm{CCdim}(L^{\mathrm{Jac}}) \leq 2^s-1$. The lower bound uses a factorially weighted distribution with $2^{s-1}+1$ supported outcomes and Bayes-optimal reports. Consequently, every exactly calibrated convex surrogate requires exponentially many prediction coordinates. We also give two polynomial-dimensional approximation guarantees with explicit regret transfers. A new $F_1$-to-Jaccard transfer turns an existing $(s^2+1)$-dimensional $F_1$ surrogate into a polynomial-time rule with asymptotic Jaccard regret at most $3-2\sqrt{2}$. For any $α>0$ and $0<ρ<1$, a MinHash square-loss surrogate attains Jaccard-regret floor $α$ uniformly over arbitrary conditional label distributions. With probability at least $1-ρ$, the direct construction has dimension $O((s^2+s\log(1/ρ))/α^2)$, while a signed variant has dimension $O((s+\log(1/ρ))/α^2)$. Thus zero-regret calibration requires exponential dimension, whereas every fixed additive regret tolerance admits polynomial prediction dimension.

</details>


### [354] [Defensive Boosting for Online Probabilistic Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13554)
*Georgy Noarov,Aaron Roth*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Defensive Booster算法，通过防御性预测（defensive forecasting）统一了在线梯度提升（Brier分数竞争性保证）和在线弱到强提升（弱学习条件下的分类误差保证）两种不相容的保证，且仅需访问一个弱类学习器，效率远高于先前需要维护大量弱学习器集成的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的在线提升技术分别提供两种不相容的保证：在线梯度提升在Brier分数上与H的跨度诱导的最佳预测器竞争，但跨度不包含准确预测器时无保证；在线弱到强提升在弱学习条件下将分类误差降至零，但条件不成立时几乎没有保证。研究者希望同时获得这两种保证。

Method: 提出Defensive Booster算法，将提升的"对偶视角"操作化：当算法的随机分类误差持续较高时，其错误权重形成平滑的重新加权，使得每个弱假设在该权重下具有低边缘（low edge），从而提供事后硬核证书证明弱学习条件不成立。算法还发展出强自适应变体，在每段时间间隔上同时满足两种保证。该算法仅访问一个弱类学习器。

Result: Defensive Booster在每条自适应序列上与在线梯度提升相同的速率下，使Brier分数与H的跨度诱导的最佳预测竞争；同时当实现转录满足平滑弱学习条件时，其Brier分数和随机分类误差满足与在线分类提升相同的速率保证。在合成和真实数据流上的实验表明其预测性能强大（有时大幅超越所有先前基线），运行速度快了多个数量级。

Conclusion: Defensive Booster通过防御性预测框架巧妙地将两种不相容的在线提升保证统一起来，在理论和实践上都优于现有方法，且效率极高。这为在线概率预测提供了统一且实用的框架。

Abstract: We study online probabilistic forecasting of binary outcomes chosen by an adaptive adversary. Given an online learning algorithm for a weak hypothesis class $H$, we would like to efficiently obtain two incomparable guarantees that existing online boosting techniques provide separately. Online gradient boosting competes in Brier score with the best predictor induced by the span of $H$ on every sequence, but promises nothing when the span does not contain an accurate predictor. Online weak-to-strong boosting drives classification error to zero under a weak-learning condition, but promises little when that condition fails.
  We give a simple defensive forecasting algorithm, the Defensive Booster, that obtains both guarantees. On every adaptive sequence, its Brier score is competitive with the best prediction induced by the span of $H$ at the same rate as online gradient boosting; simultaneously, whenever the realized transcript satisfies the smooth weak-learning condition, its Brier score and randomized classification error satisfy the same rate guarantee as online classification boosting. This is achieved by operationalizing the "dual view" of boosting: When the algorithm's randomized classification error is persistently high, its mistake weights form a smooth reweighting on which every weak hypothesis has low edge, yielding an ex-post hard-core certificate that the weak-learning condition fails. We also develop a strongly adaptive variant, which satisfies both guarantees on every time interval. The Defensive Booster is very efficient: it accesses just one weak-class learner, whereas the prior online boosting methods we compare against maintain large weak-learner ensembles. Experiments on synthetic and real data streams demonstrate its strong predictive performance (sometimes substantially improving over all prior baselines) coupled with orders-of-magnitude faster runtime.

</details>


<div id='cs.GT'></div>

# cs.GT [[Back]](#toc)

### [355] [A Repeated-Game Framework for Incentives in Decentralized Infrastructure Protocols](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12576)
*Mustafa Qazi*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个重复动态激励框架，用于分析去中心化物理基础设施网络（DePIN）中"合规"（即全力诚实提供服务）何时具有激励相容性。核心贡献是"威慑比"Γ（偏离的私人收益与边际检测概率之比的最坏情况），并基于此给出了合规序贯激励相容的充要条件，进而转化为协议设计问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DePIN中需要确保服务提供商按照协议要求诚实且尽全力提供服务（合规），但协议与每个提供商之间存在重复道德风险问题。现有研究缺乏一个系统框架来刻画在质押削减和声誉降级（降入试用层）等惩罚机制下，合规何时能被激励相容地执行。

Method: 将DePIN中的质量控制建模为协议与每个提供商之间的重复道德风险问题。合规通过两种机制强制执行：(1) 质押抵押品的削减（slashing）；(2) 在声誉阶梯上降级到试用层的折现威胁。引入"威慑比"Γ，定义为一次偏离的私人收益与其边际检测概率之比的最坏情况。当所有有利可图的偏离都有正检测差距且没有弱代价更高的行动更不易失败时，证明合规序贯激励相容的充要条件为：即时削减加上折现声誉损失在每个声誉层级上至少等于Γ。据此构建协议设计问题，将服务原语映射到质押要求、奖励计划、试用规则和审计频率。

Result: 主要发现：(1) 如果任一有利可图的偏离不会相对于合规增加失败概率，则二元公开结果协议无法威慑该偏离；(2) 在满足正检测差距和单调性条件下，合规是序贯激励相容的当且仅当每个声誉层级上的即时削减与折现声誉损失之和不小于威慑比Γ；(3) 基于该条件可构建协议设计优化问题，映射服务原语到具体的设计参数（质押、奖励、试用规则、审计频率）。

Conclusion: 本文为DePIN的合规激励机制提供了严谨的理论基础，给出了可操作的协议设计条件，并揭示了二元公开结果协议在检测能力不足时的局限性，为设计更有效的DePIN质量控制机制提供了指导原则。

Abstract: We introduce a repeated dynamic incentive framework for characterizing when "compliance", or full-effort honest service provision, is incentive compatible in Decentralized Physical Infrastructure Networks (DePIN). We model quality control in this setting as a repeated moral-hazard problem between the protocol and each provider, where compliance is enforced by both slashing posted collateral and the discounted threat of demotion to probation tiers on a reputation ladder. Our main contribution is the "deterrence ratio" $Γ$, the worst-case ratio of a deviation's private gain to its marginal probability of detection. We find that if any profitable deviation does not increase the fail probability relative to compliance, then binary public-outcome protocols cannot deter it. When all profitable deviations have positive detection gaps and no weakly costlier action is less likely to fail, compliance is sequentially incentive compatible if and only if immediate slashing plus discounted reputation loss is at least $Γ$ at every reputation tier. We use this condition to formulate a protocol design problem mapping service primitives to stake requirements, reward schedules, probation rules, and audit frequency.

</details>


### [356] [Error-Aware Reverse Auction Mechanism for Large Language Model Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12719)
*Haolong Chen,Zhengyuan Xin,Liang Zhang,Lei Xue,Guangxu Zhu*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于市场的LLM路由范式，通过反向拍卖机制（EA-RAM）让LLM提供商自行投标预测成功率与执行成本，取代集中式任务中心的性能预测，从而解决信息-风险不匹配和可扩展性瓶颈问题。EA-RAM显式建模了提供商预测与中心评估的固有双重误差，并证明其在贝叶斯激励相容性、个体理性及中心理性方面的特性，实验表明其在成本-性能帕累托前沿上优于集中式基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM路由器依赖集中式任务中心预测模型性能，导致两个问题：(1) 信息-风险不匹配——中心不具备提供商内部信息的完整视图；(2) 可扩展性瓶颈——模型池扩大时集中预测成本激增。因此需要更分散、可扩展的路由机制。

Method: 提出市场化的反向拍卖路由范式：提供商以自预测成功概率和执行成本进行投标，中心基于投标分配查询。引入EA-RAM机制显式建模双重误差（提供商预测误差与中心评估误差），并推导其博弈论性质（贝叶斯激励相容、个体理性、中心理性充分条件及福利损失上界），同时分析误差抵消、饱和效应等鲁棒性机制。

Result: 证明EA-RAM具备贝叶斯激励相容与个体理性；识别出鲁棒性效应（反向误差抵消、logistic等消失尾链接函数在明确情况下通过饱和稳定化、额外噪声平滑信念图）；仿真与真实基准实验表明EA-RAM对双重误差鲁棒，且优于集中式基线的成本-性能帕累托前沿，提供商提供本地信息时收益更大。

Conclusion: 市场化的反向拍卖范式为LLM路由提供了比集中式预测更可扩展且成本效益更优的替代方案，EA-RAM在理论上保证了激励兼容性，在实践上展现出鲁棒性和更优的帕累托性能，验证了其实际有效性。

Abstract: Routing each query to a cost-effective large language model (LLM) is critical for balancing quality and cost, yet most routers rely on a centralized task center to predict model performance, creating an information-risk mismatch and a scalability bottleneck as the model pool grows. We propose a market-based routing paradigm that shifts ex-ante prediction to LLM providers via a reverse auction, where providers bid with self-predicted success probabilities and execution costs. To account for inherently noisy provider predictions and center evaluations, we introduce the \textit{\textbf{E}rror-\textbf{A}ware \textbf{R}everse \textbf{A}uction \textbf{M}echanism} (EA-RAM), which explicitly models this inherent Dual Error. We prove that EA-RAM is Bayesian incentive compatible and individually rational under the Dual Error, establish sufficient conditions for center rationality, and derive an explicit welfare-loss bound. We further identify robustness effects: opposite-signed errors can cancel, vanishing-tail link functions (e.g., logistic) stabilize clear-cut cases via saturation, and extra noise smooths belief maps, reducing the gains from marginal manipulation. Experiments on simulations and real-world benchmarks show that EA-RAM is robust to the Dual Error and achieves a better cost--performance Pareto frontier than centralized baselines, with additional gains when providers contribute local information, validating its practical effectiveness.

</details>


### [357] [Representation in Peer Selection: A Liquid Democracy Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13085)
*Davide Grossi,Grzegorz Lisowski,Georgios Papasotiropoulos*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文研究同行选举问题：一组同行需从自身中选出固定规模的委员会。作者引入一类基于批准的偏好特征（称为"流动特征"），其灵感来自流动民主中的委托图和信任的可传递性。在每个流动特征中，每位投票者指定一位信任的代理人，并间接批准该代理人信任的所有人。论文建立了该特征与标准受限偏好特征的关系，考察其计算性质，重点研究新的和已有的比例性公理在该领域中的表现及其相互关系，并评估各种投票规则（包括受流动特征结构启发的新规则）对它们的满足程度。研究发现这一新领域可实现特别强的比例性保障，展示了通过可传递委托应用于真实世界同行选举问题的前景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同行选举问题在现实中有广泛应用（如学术评审委员会、项目评审等），但传统模型难以捕捉信任的传递性特征。流动民主中的委托图概念启发了一种新的偏好结构，使得比例性保障在这一受限领域中可能更容易实现，因此值得系统性研究。

Method: 1) 提出"流动特征"类别的批准型偏好特征：每位投票者指定单一信任代理人，并批准该代理人信任的所有人。2) 建立与标准受限偏好特征（如单峰、单跨等）的关系。3) 考察流动特征的计算性质。4) 分析新老比例性公理在该领域中的行为及其相互关系。5) 评估各种投票规则（含新提出的受流动特征启发的规则）对这些公理的满足情况。

Result: 流动特征领域可实现特别强的比例性保障。研究发现该领域能同时满足多个强比例性公理，且新提出的投票规则在此领域表现良好，证明了可传递委托结构的理论价值。

Conclusion: 流动特征是一种有前景的偏好受限领域，它源自流动民主的委托思想，能够实现强比例性保障，为真实世界的同行选举可传递委托应用提供了理论和算法基础。

Abstract: We study the peer selection problem in which a group of peers must select a fixed-size committee from among themselves. To this end, we introduce a class of approval-based preference profiles, inspired by delegation graphs in liquid democracy and the transitivity of trust therein, which we call ``liquid profiles''. Under this new domain restriction, each voter designates a single trusted agent and, in turn, approves all those trusted by that individual. After establishing relationships to standard restricted preference profiles and examining computational properties of liquid profiles, we focus on understanding how novel and established proportionality axioms behave in this domain and how they relate to one another. Then, we study their satisfaction under various voting rules, including new ones that are directly inspired by the structure of liquid profiles. Our findings reveal that particularly strong proportionality guarantees are attainable in this new domain, showing promise for applications to real-world peer selection problems via transitive delegations.

</details>


### [358] [Power in Liquid Democracy: A Network Centrality Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13188)
*Davide Grossi,Andreas Nitsche,Georgios Papasotiropoulos,Oskar Skibski,Piotr Skowron,Tomasz Wąs*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个计算上易处理的框架，用于衡量液态民主（支持传递性和可暂停委托的集体决策平台）中选民的权力。该框架采用Random Walk Decay中心性来捕捉影响力在委托网络中的传播，并提出了选择有影响力参与者代表性名单的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 液态民主平台允许多层级、可暂停的委托，需要一种计算可行且直观合理的方法来衡量参与者在这类集体决策中的实际权力，并选择有代表性的关键参与者。

Method: 采用Random Walk Decay中心性来建模影响力在委托网络中的传播；通过公理化表征论证其在权力度量类中的合理性，并与PageRank等替代方案进行比较；同时提出（公理化与算法层面）选择代表性参与者名单的方法。

Result: 该框架在计算上可行，Random Walk Decay中心性被证明具有直观吸引力和公理化支持，且优于PageRank等替代方案；选择代表性参与者的方法也在公理与算法层面得到研究。

Conclusion: 该框架不仅提供了度量液态民主中选民权力的理论基础，还可扩展为分析此类平台权力分布的实际工具。

Abstract: This paper develops a computationally tractable framework for measuring voters' power in collective decision-making platforms that support transitive and suspendable delegations---commonly known as liquid democracy. We employ Random Walk Decay centrality to capture how influence propagates through delegation networks, argue for its intuitive appeal in this context and its advantages over alternatives such as PageRank, and derive a natural axiomatic characterization within the class of power metrics. Moreover, we conceptualize how the framework can be extended into a practical tool for analyzing power distributions in such platforms, and propose and study, both axiomatically and algorithmically, methods for selecting representative slates of influential participants.

</details>


### [359] [Keep, Customize, or Exit: Default Design and Token Pricing in LLM Reasoning Services](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13315)
*Ahmet Bugra Gundogan,Yigit Turkmen,Melih Bastopcu*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文研究了一个大语言模型（LLM）服务商的定价与推理token分配策略问题。服务商设定单位token价格和默认推理token分配量，用户可选择接受默认、定制分配或退出。作者将此建模为Stackelberg博弈，推导出用户最优定制分配的闭式解，刻画了服务商最优默认分配的三区制规则，并证明均衡的存在性。实验基于两个紧凑型开源推理模型和五个数学/科学基准验证了模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM服务中，推理token的分配直接影响准确率、成本和延迟，但服务商与用户之间的利益冲突和交互策略缺乏理论分析。本文旨在从博弈论角度系统研究定价与推理token分配的最优策略。

Method: 将服务商与用户的交互建模为Stackelberg博弈（服务商为领导者设定价格和默认分配，用户为跟随者选择接受/定制/退出）；推导用户最优定制分配的闭式解；通过三区制规则刻画服务商最优默认分配；将均衡计算降维为单变量价格优化并证明均衡存在性；使用两个开源推理模型在五个基准上进行实验验证。

Result: 对于任意价格，可接受的默认分配要么为空集，要么为紧致区间；默认分配仅在用户重视避免定制的便利性时才影响实际推理分配，否则服务提供结果都实现用户最优定制分配；实验结果支持准确率-成本模型，并揭示了模型与任务特征如何决定均衡价格、默认分配和推理分配。

Conclusion: 论文为LLM服务的推理token定价与分配提供了完整的博弈论分析框架，证明了均衡存在性，并通过实验验证了理论模型的有效性，为实际LLM服务商的定价与资源配置决策提供了理论指导。

Abstract: We study a large language model (LLM) service in which a provider chooses a per-token price and a default reasoning-token allocation, while a user may accept the default, customize the allocation, or exit. Larger allocations can improve accuracy but increase token cost and latency. We model this interaction as a Stackelberg game and derive the user's unique optimal customized allocation in closed form. For any price, the acceptable defaults form either an empty set or a compact interval. We characterize the provider's optimal default through a three-regime rule, reduce equilibrium computation to a one-dimensional price optimization, and prove the existence of the equilibrium. We further show that defaults affect the implemented reasoning allocation only when users value the convenience of avoiding customization; otherwise, every service-providing outcome implements the user's optimal customized allocation. Experiments with two compact open-weight reasoning models on five mathematics and science benchmarks support the accuracy-token model and show how model and task characteristics determine equilibrium prices, defaults, and reasoning allocations.

</details>


<div id='cs.MM'></div>

# cs.MM [[Back]](#toc)

### [360] [MASCOT: Model-Aware Submodular Coverage for Composite-Attribute Text-to-Image Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12532)
*Aaryan Sharma,Vishak Prasad C,Virendra Singh,Ganesh Ramakrishnan*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 本文提出MASCOT方法，将多属性结果多样化问题建模为资源分配问题，通过软分箱空间和查询驱动重要性加权，替代传统流形排斥策略（如MS-DPP），显著提升复合属性约束下的早排名召回率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型在语义相关检索中表现出色，但实际系统中"相关性"往往不够，还需要跨复合属性（如地理、时间）的结果多样化（RD）。现有重排序方法（如MS-DPP）依赖流形排斥，虽在广泛探索中有效，但在需要对离散元数据执行多样性降低任务时，早期排名召回率严重退化。

Method: MASCOT将多属性多样化形式化为资源分配问题，将属性投影到由查询驱动重要性加权的软分箱空间中，替代基于流形的排斥策略。其核心思想是将复合属性多样性的控制从几何排斥转向覆盖优化的资源调度。

Result: 在PixelProse三个多样性降低任务上，MASCOT的R@10平均为88.58%，远高于MS-DPP的67.63%。在复合约束（PP_geo_hour）下差距更大：MS-DPP的R@10从0.9737崩至0.4931且R@1=0.23，而MASCOT保持R@10=0.9410、R@1=0.7202且多样性指标超过无约束基线。作者不主张全面优越性：在聚合多样性-相关性得分上，简单的消融方法在三个降低任务上获得更高调和均值，MASCOT的优势特定于复合约束下排名1之后的召回。

Conclusion: MASCOT在复合属性约束的多样性降低任务中显著优于基于流形的方法，但其优势具有特定性——集中在复合约束下的早期召回（R>1）而非聚合相关性-多样性得分。这揭示了流形方法与覆盖方法在不同评估维度上的本质权衡。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) are highly effective in retrieving semantically relevant images. However, in practice, relevance alone is often insufficient. Systems must also achieve Result Diversification (RD) across composite attributes such as geography and time, a task for which precise control remains challenging. Current re-ranking methods, such as Multi-Source Determinantal Point Processes (MS-DPP), address this using manifold-based repulsion over similarity representations. Although this strategy is effective for broad exploration, it exposes a key limitation in manifold-based models: when subjected to diversity-decrease tasks on discrete metadata, they suffer substantial degradation in early-rank recall.
  To bridge this gap, we introduce MASCOT (Model-Aware Submodular Coverage for Composite-Attribute Text-to-Image Retrieval). Instead of relying on manifold repulsion, MASCOT formulates multi-attribute diversity as a resource allocation problem, projecting attributes into a soft-binning space weighted by query-driven importance. Averaged across the three PixelProse diversity-decrease tasks, MASCOT preserves an early-rank recall (R@10) of 88.58%, while MS-DPP retains 67.63%. The margin widens under composite constraints: on PP_geo_hour, where temporal and geographic diversity must be suppressed simultaneously, MS-DPP's recall collapses from 0.9737 to 0.4931 and its top-ranked result degrades to R@1 = 0.23, while MASCOT holds R@10 = 0.9410 and R@1 = 0.7202 at a diversity metric above the unconstrained baseline. We do not claim uniform superiority: on aggregate diversity-relevance scores our own simpler ablations attain higher harmonic means on all three decrease tasks, and MASCOT's advantage is specific to recall beyond rank 1 under composite constraints.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [361] [Test-Time Optimization of Query Embeddings with Ranking Aware Reward Maximization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12569)
*Tianyu Chen,Jiaxing Wu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出TTT-Embed框架，在冻结的嵌入模型的输出空间中优化一个轻量级学习向量，将从重排器或LLM裁判中获得的排序奖励蒸馏进去。无需访问模型权重、真实标签或修改索引，仅凭标量排序分数训练。通过单一作用域参数控制奖励复用范围（全局/任务/查询级），在15个MTEB检索任务、5个嵌入模型上最高提升+8.36 nDCG@10，并能泛化到未见查询和任务，同时避免灾难性遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有密集检索器利用向量相似度排序，虽然测试时的重排奖励能提升效果，但现有方法在单次查询后即丢弃该信号。直接更新检索器权重虽可复用奖励，但需要参数访问权限（闭源模型不可用）且计算代价过高。因此需要一种无需权重访问、可复用排序奖励的高效测试时适应方法。

Method: TTT-Embed在冻结模型的输出嵌入空间中学习一个轻量级向量，该向量通过优化检索器自身候选文档的标量排序分数来训练。它不依赖模型权重、真实标签或索引修改。一个作用域参数控制奖励在全局、任务或查询级别的共享程度，实现可复用性与特定性之间的权衡。在固定奖励计算预算下，奖励预算增长时最优共享范围从全局→任务→查询动态转移。

Result: 在5个嵌入模型、15个MTEB检索任务上，测试时检索最高提升+8.36 nDCG@10。学习到的状态对未见查询（+8.57）和未见任务（+4.71）均有良好泛化。通过完全冻结基础权重，恢复了退化的一般能力（+8.00，甚至超过原始基础模型），同时保留领域内专长，有效解决灾难性遗忘问题。

Conclusion: 该工作确立了排序奖励可作为可复用的测试时状态，支持任何嵌入模型（包括闭源API）的预算高效适应。通过轻量级向量蒸馏奖励信号，在保持冻结基础模型的同时实现了可泛化的检索能力提升，为闭源模型场景下的测试时优化提供了新范式。

Abstract: Dense retrievers rank documents using vector similarity between a frozen encoder and a precomputed index. While test-time ranking rewards from a reranker or LLM judge can improve results, existing methods discard this signal after a single query. Updating the retriever's weights makes rewards reusable, but this requires parameter access, which is unavailable for closed-source models, and is computationally prohibitive. We propose TTT-Embed (Test-Time Tuning of Embeddings), a framework that distills ranking rewards into a lightweight, learned vector within the output embedding space of a frozen model. This vector is optimized purely from scalar ranking scores assigned to the retriever's own candidate documents, requiring no access to model weights, ground-truth labels, or modifications to index. A single scope parameter controls rewards reuse (global, task, or query), enabling a principled trade-off between reusability and specificity under a fixed reward computation budget. We demonstrate that as the available reward budget scales, the optimal sharing scope shifts dynamically from global-wise to task-wise and finally to query-wise. Evaluated across five embedding models and 15 MTEB retrieval tasks, TTT-Embed improves test-time retrieval by up to +8.36 nDCG@10. Crucially, the learned states generalize effectively to unseen queries (up to +8.57 nDCG@10) and unseen tasks (up to +4.71 nDCG@10). Furthermore, TTT-Embed successfully resolves catastrophic forgetting: by leaving base weights entirely frozen, it recovers degraded general capabilities (up to +8.00 nDCG@10, even surpassing the original base model) while preserving in-domain specialization. These results establish ranking rewards as a reusable test-time state, enabling budget-efficient adaptation for any embedding model, including closed-source APIs.

</details>


### [362] [Knowledge Synthesis Review Framework: Task-Level Benchmarking of LLM-Based Systems for Multi-Source Evidence Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12741)
*Wafa Shafqat,Mark Patterson,Steven N. Liss*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出知识综合综述（KSR）框架，一种人在回路的证据综合方法，将综合任务分解为筛选、提取、分析和综合四个阶段，并分别对多个LLM系统进行基准测试和路由。在244篇文档基准上，没有任何单一系统在所有任务上领先，解释性分析和跨来源综合仍需要专家参与。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 快速发展的领域中，证据分散于学术研究、行业报告、政策文件和媒体等多来源，质量、结构和目的各异，及时综合困难。LLM可能加速此工作，但其在不同认知任务中的可靠性尚不确定。

Method: KSR框架将证据综合分解为筛选、提取、分析、综合四个任务，对GPT-5、Claude Sonnet 4、Gemini 2.5 Pro和NotebookLM进行基准测试，与专家参考标准对比（92.2%一致性，kappa=0.80），并路由各任务至最佳系统，在专家持续验证下运行。

Result: 无单一系统在所有任务中领先。Claude Sonnet 4筛选准确率最高（82.8%），GPT-5召回率最高（91.8%）但特异性较低。提取任务在标题和来源字段超过90%一致性，但在作者和参考文献字段退化。解释性分析和跨来源综合表现下降最明显。污染检查显示先前暴露未夸大结果。在完整语料库应用中，路由工作流揭示出单来源综合会遗漏的跨来源不对称和盲点。

Conclusion: KSR提供了一个透明、可审计、模型无关的框架，用于治理研究综合中的LLM辅助，同时保持人类问责制。

Abstract: Evidence in rapidly evolving fields is fragmented across academic studies, industry reports, policy documents, and media sources that differ in quality, structure, and purpose, making timely synthesis difficult. Large language models (LLMs) may accelerate this work, but their reliability across the distinct cognitive tasks of a review remains uncertain. We introduce the Knowledge Synthesis Review (KSR), a human-in-the-loop framework that decomposes evidence synthesis into screening, extraction, analysis, and synthesis, benchmarks LLM-based systems on each task against expert reference standards, and routes each task to the best-performing system under continuous expert validation. We evaluated GPT-5, Claude Sonnet 4, Gemini 2.5 Pro, and NotebookLM on a 244-document benchmark subset drawn from a 1,893-document corpus on AI and work spanning four source types, against a gold standard with high inter-rater reliability (92.2% agreement, kappa = 0.80). No system led on all tasks. Claude Sonnet 4 achieved the highest screening accuracy (82.8%) and GPT-5 the highest recall (91.8%) at the expense of lower specificity. Extraction exceeded 90% agreement for titles and sources but degraded in author and reference fields. Performance declined most in interpretive analysis and cross-source synthesis, where expert judgment remained essential. A contamination check on post-cutoff documents showed no evidence that prior exposure inflated results. Applied to the full corpus, the routed workflow surfaced cross-source asymmetries and blind spots that single-source synthesis would miss, including worker well-being, small firms, and the Global South. KSR offers a transparent, auditable, model-agnostic framework for governing LLM assistance in research synthesis while preserving human accountability.

</details>


### [363] [DrEM: Dual-Side Robust Ensemble Ranking from Noisy User Preference Predictions in Video Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12778)
*Canwei Huang,Tiantian He,Xiaoxiao Xu,Jun Zhang,Ziran Deng,Weike Pan,Chunjie Chen,Kaiqiao Zhan*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出DrEM框架，针对工业视频推荐系统集成排序阶段中上游多任务模型预测信号(pxtrs)存在噪声的问题，从监督和特征两个侧面对抗噪声：通过风险去噪鲁棒损失修正经验风险，并通过偏好保持的排序一致性正则化提升特征侧输出稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在集成排序阶段，上游多任务模型的预测结果(pxtrs)同时作为输入特征和构造代理偏好的来源，但这些预测不可避免地包含噪声。噪声在监督侧会导致代理偏好翻转并引入错误梯度，在特征侧会传播并破坏排序分数稳定性。现有方法将pxtrs视为可靠信号，忽略了预测噪声的影响。

Method: DrEM框架包含两个核心组件：(1) 风险去噪鲁棒损失——利用估计的偏好翻转概率来修正经验风险；(2) 偏好保持的排序一致性正则化——从预测噪声分布中采样扰动，增强特征侧输出稳定性。理论上推导了预测噪声的近似分布，并证明了鲁棒损失在翻转概率估计误差下仍然保持优越性。

Result: 通过大规模离线实验和在线A/B测试验证了DrEM框架的有效性和鲁棒性，表明该方法在工业视频推荐场景中能有效提升集成排序性能。

Conclusion: DrEM针对集成排序中上游预测噪声的负面影响，从监督侧和特征侧双向进行去噪处理，为工业推荐系统的鲁棒性提供了系统性的解决方案，并具备理论保证。

Abstract: Industrial video recommendation systems typically adopt a multi-stage architecture. At the ensemble ranking stage, multi-dimensional user preference predictions (pxtrs) from an upstream multi-task model are fused into a unified ranking score to reflect user satisfaction. Since users' true satisfaction is difficult to observe directly, ensemble ranking models commonly use pxtrs both as input features and as a source for constructing proxy preferences. However, as outputs of an upstream prediction model, pxtrs inevitably contain prediction noise, which propagates to downstream learning across two sides. On the supervision side, noisy pxtrs may flip proxy preferences and introduce erroneous gradients. On the feature side, pxtr noise may propagate through model inputs and destabilize ranking scores. Existing ensemble ranking methods typically treat pxtrs as reliable signals and overlook such prediction noise. To address this, we propose DrEM, a dual-side robust ensemble ranking framework. Our DrEM introduces a risk-denoising robust loss that corrects the empirical risk using estimated preference flip probability. Meanwhile, it samples perturbations from the distribution of prediction noise and introduces a preference-preserving ranking consistency regularizer to improve feature-side output stability. Theoretically, we obtain an approximate distribution of the prediction noise and prove that the robust loss remains superior under flip probability estimation error. Extensive offline experiments and large-scale online A/B tests demonstrate the effectiveness and robustness of our DrEM.

</details>


### [364] [A Comprehensive Empirical Evaluation of Vector Database Systems for Approximate Nearest Neighbor Search: Performance, Quality, and Resource Trade-offs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12812)
*Ashen Rashmiks,Tiroshan Madushanka*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文对七个主流向量数据库系统（FAISS、Qdrant、Milvus、Weaviate、Chroma、pgvector、LanceDB）进行了系统性的实证评估，涵盖6个数据集、超过400万个向量、15项指标，分别从检索质量、查询性能、资源消耗三个维度展开全面对比，并发布了开源的基准测试框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 向量数据库已成为RAG、语义搜索和推荐系统等现代AI应用的关键基础设施，但现有研究缺乏一个综合、可复现的基准测试来同时评估检索质量、查询延迟、吞吐量和资源利用等多个维度，导致从业者难以在系统选型时做出明智决策。

Method: 在6个多样化数据集（涵盖SIFT、GIST等经典CV描述符以及MS MARCO、GloVe等Transformer文本嵌入）上，对7个向量数据库系统进行统一评估。数据集包含超过400万个不同维度（96至960）的向量。测量15个指标，包括检索质量（Recall@K、Precision@K、MRR、NDCG@K、Hit Rate@K）、查询性能（延迟百分位、QPS、冷启动延迟）和资源消耗（索引构建时间、内存、存储）。

Result: FAISS在SIFT1M上达到最高单节点吞吐量（866 QPS）但缺乏数据库运维功能；Weaviate在开箱即用的召回率上表现最佳（>99%）；Qdrant在完整数据库中延迟最优（中位数4.55ms）；LanceDB以牺牲检索质量为代价换取显著更快的索引构建速度。

Conclusion: 没有单一系统在所有维度上胜出，不同系统各有优势与权衡。作者据此为从业者提供了系统选型指南，并将基准测试框架作为开源软件发布，以填补该领域综合可复现基准的空白。

Abstract: Vector databases have emerged as critical infrastructure for modern artificial intelligence applications, particularly retrieval-augmented generation (RAG), semantic search, and recommendation systems. Despite their growing importance, there remains a significant gap in comprehensive, reproducible benchmarks that jointly evaluate retrieval quality, query latency, throughput, and resource utilization. We present a systematic empirical evaluation of seven prominent vector database systems: FAISS, Qdrant, Milvus, Weaviate, Chroma, pgvector, and LanceDB. Our methodology spans six diverse datasets, from classical computer-vision descriptors (SIFT, GIST) to transformer-based text embeddings (MS MARCO, GloVe), encompassing over 4 million vectors at dimensionalities from 96 to 960. We measure 15 metrics spanning retrieval quality (Recall@K, Precision@K, MRR, NDCG@K, Hit Rate@K), query performance (latency percentiles, QPS, cold-start latency), and resource consumption (index build time, memory, storage). On SIFT1M, FAISS achieves the highest single-node throughput (866 QPS) but lacks database operational features; Weaviate provides the best out-of-the-box recall (> 99%); Qdrant offers the best latency among full databases (4.55~ms median); and LanceDB trades retrieval quality for substantially faster index construction. We derive system-selection guidelines for practitioners and release our benchmarking framework as open-source software.

</details>


### [365] [Query Translation vs. Cross-Lingual Embeddings for Sinhala-Tamil E-Government Information Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12820)
*Dharshi Balasubramaniyam,Tiroshan Madushanka*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文比较了跨语言信息检索（CLIR）方法在用僧伽罗语和泰米尔语查询检索英文政府信息时的表现。实验对比了查询翻译（QT：Google Translate、NLLB、mBART50）和跨语言嵌入（CLE：LaBSE、多语言E5、BGE-M3）两种范式，以单语英语检索为基线。在基于斯里兰卡政府信息中心（GIC）构建的500对人工验证问答基准上，BGE-M3表现最佳（僧伽罗语-英语Recall@15达96.2%，泰米尔语-英语达95.6%），优于最佳查询翻译方法（Google Translate：92.4%和93.0%），且避免了翻译开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低资源语言（如僧伽罗语和泰米尔语）在政府信息检索领域面临严重的数据稀缺问题，检索增强生成（RAG）系统的跨语言能力尚缺乏系统性的实证比较研究。作者旨在评估不同CLIR范式在低资源政府领域中的有效性，为实际部署提供依据。

Method: 构建了包含500对僧伽罗语、泰米尔语和英语问答对的人工验证基准数据集（源自1,699个GIC文档段落）。系统比较两大CLIR范式：查询翻译范式（使用Google Translate、NLLB和mBART50进行翻译）与跨语言嵌入范式（使用LaBSE、多语言E5和BGE-M3），并以单语英语检索作为基线。评估指标为Recall@k（k=1,3,5,10,15）。

Result: 单语检索性能较差（Recall@15<10%），所有CLIR方法均显著提升检索准确率。BGE-M3取得最高性能，对僧伽罗语-英语达96.2% Recall@15，泰米尔语-英语达95.6%，优于最佳QT方法（Google Translate分别92.4%和93.0%），且无需额外翻译步骤。

Conclusion: 多语言嵌入模型（特别是BGE-M3）是低资源政府领域跨语言RAG更有效且可扩展的解决方案，相较于查询翻译方法更具优势，既提升了检索性能又避免了翻译开销。

Abstract: This paper presents a comparative evaluation of cross-lingual information retrieval (CLIR) methods for retrieving English government information using Sinhala and Tamil queries. Two CLIR paradigms are investigated: Query Translation (QT), employing Google Translate, NLLB, and mBART50, and Cross-Lingual Embeddings (CLE), using LaBSE, multilingual E5, and BGE-M3, with monolingual English retrieval as the baseline. Experiments are conducted on a human-verified benchmark comprising 500 Sinhala, Tamil, and English question-answer pairs derived from 1,699 segmented contexts from Sri Lanka's Government Information Center (GIC). Retrieval performance is evaluated using Recall@k (k = 1, 3, 5, 10, 15). Monolingual retrieval performs poorly (Recall@15 <10%), whereas all CLIR approaches substantially improve retrieval accuracy. Among them, BGE-M3 achieves the highest Recall@15, reaching 96.2% for Sinhala-English and 95.6% for Tamil-English, outperforming the best QT approach (Google Translate: 92.4% and 93.0%) while avoiding translation overhead. These results demonstrate that multilingual embedding models provide a more effective and scalable solution for cross-lingual retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) in low-resource government domains.

</details>


### [366] [FSGR: Mitigating Token Frequency Bias for Fair SID-Based Generative Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12845)
*Yuchen Zheng,Sihan Xu,Jingwen Yang,Xiangrui Cai,Haiwei Zhang,Xiaojie Yuan*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出FSGR框架，解决基于语义ID（SID）的生成式推荐系统中存在的Token频率偏差公平性问题。该框架在SID构建阶段采用OT分配优化和双准则重锚定机制构建更均衡的SID表示空间，在推荐训练阶段采用两阶段训练策略和层级频率校准实现层级别的公平性微调，在三个公开数据集上实现了超过20%的Gini公平性提升，同时保持有竞争力的推荐准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SID生成式推荐方法存在被忽视的公平性问题——Token频率偏差：高频SID token被系统性过度预测，低频token被欠预测。该偏差源于SID构建时语义码本不均衡，以及训练时流行度偏差与最大似然估计目标的共同作用，导致不同物品类别间的曝光不公平。现有方法要么只关注码本质量，要么将LLM去偏方法直接应用于SID推荐效果不佳（因SID token具有层级语义结构）。

Method: FSGR框架包含两大阶段：(1) SID构建阶段：采用基于最优传输（OT）的分配优化和双准则重锚定机制，形成更均衡的SID表示空间；(2) 推荐训练阶段：采用两阶段训练策略，并引入层级频率校准（Hierarchical Frequency Calibration）进行层特定的公平性微调。

Result: 在三个公开数据集和三个骨干模型上的实验表明，FSGR缓解了token频率偏差，实现了平均超过20%的Gini公平性提升，同时保持了有竞争力的推荐准确率。

Conclusion: FSGR有效解决了SID生成式推荐中的Token频率偏差公平性问题，证明了在SID构建和推荐训练两个阶段同时进行公平性优化的可行性，为生成式推荐系统的公平性研究提供了新思路。

Abstract: Semantic ID (SID)-based generative recommendation has recently achieved remarkable success. However, existing methods suffer from a previously overlooked fairness issue, which we term \textbf{Token Frequency Bias}, where high-frequency SID tokens are systematically over-predicted while low-frequency SID tokens are under-predicted. This bias originates from the combined effects of imbalanced semantic codebooks during SID construction, and popularity bias together with the maximum likelihood estimation objective during recommendation training, resulting in unfair exposure across item categories. Existing SID methods mainly focus on improving codebook quality and overlook the impact of token frequency imbalance on downstream recommendation fairness, while LLM debiasing methods often yield suboptimal results when directly applied to SID-based recommendation, due to the hierarchical semantics of SID tokens. To address this issue, we propose \textbf{FSGR}, a fairness optimization framework for SID-based generative recommendation. During SID construction, FSGR employs OT-based Assignment Optimization and Dual-Criteria Re-anchor mechanism to form a more balanced SID representation space. During recommendation training, it adopts a two-stage training strategy and introduces Hierarchical Frequency Calibration for layer-specific fairness fine-tuning. Experiments on three public datasets with three backbone models demonstrate that FSGR mitigates token frequency bias and delivers an average Gini fairness improvement of over 20\% while maintaining competitive recommendation accuracy.

</details>


### [367] [STAR: Structured Tokenization and Target-Aware Interest Representation for PCVR Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12986)
*Yimeng Xu,Haorui Zhang,Yingqi Song,Ying Jiang,Lan Ma*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文介绍STAR框架，一种用于KDD Cup 2026腾讯UniRec挑战赛的工业推荐系统点击后转化率预测框架。STAR结合结构化特征tokenization和target-aware兴趣表示，基于HyFormer风格多序列骨干网络，引入高基数信号恢复、显式用户-物品交互token、target-aware序列解码和加权用户-物品对比辅助目标。实验表明时间上下文带来最大增益，对比对齐、target-aware兴趣编码和高基数序列特征恢复也有贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代工业推荐系统中的排名模型需要同时捕获异质非序列特征、多行为用户序列和目标物品感知的用户兴趣，同时还要对高基数稀疏特征、缺失值和训练-推理不一致性保持鲁棒性，这是一个复杂的综合挑战需要统一的解决方案。

Method: STAR框架包含以下核心组件：(1)结构化特征tokenization处理异质特征；(2)HyFormer风格多序列骨干网络建模多行为用户序列；(3)高基数信号恢复处理稀疏特征；(4)显式用户-物品交互token；(5)target-aware序列解码捕获目标物品感知的用户兴趣；(6)基于InfoNCE的加权用户-物品对比辅助目标；(7)通过重建特征映射表和结构超参数对齐训练与推理流程。

Result: 在挑战赛数据集上的实验识别出最稳定提升排名AUC的组件。主要消融研究显示时间上下文带来最大增益，对比对齐、target-aware兴趣编码和高基数序列特征恢复提供较小但有用的贡献；LogLoss作为校准诊断指标报告。

Conclusion: STAR是一个实用的工业级PCVR预测框架，通过综合多种创新组件成功应对了工业推荐系统中的复杂挑战，特别强调了训练-推理一致性对齐和组件消融分析的重要性。

Abstract: Post-click conversion rate (PCVR) prediction is a core ranking task in industrial recommender systems. Modern ranking models must jointly capture heterogeneous non-sequential features, multi-behavior user sequences, and target-item-aware user interests, while remaining robust to high-cardinality sparse features, missing values, and train-inference inconsistencies. In this paper, we present STAR (Structured Tokenization and Target-Aware Interest Representation), a practical framework for the KDD Cup 2026 Tencent UniRec Challenge. STAR combines structured feature tokenization with target-aware interest representation on top of a HyFormer-style multi-sequence backbone. It introduces high-cardinality signal recovery, explicit user-item interaction tokens, target-aware sequence decoding, and a weighted user-item contrastive auxiliary objective inspired by InfoNCE. We further align the training and inference pipelines by reconstructing feature remapping tables and structural hyperparameters from the saved training configuration. Experiments on the challenge dataset identify the components that most reliably improve ranking AUC, while LogLoss is reported as a calibration diagnostic. The main ablation study shows a large gain from temporal context, with smaller but useful contributions from contrastive alignment, target-aware interest encoding, and high-cardinality sequence feature recovery.

</details>


### [368] [Generative Universal Multimodal Retrieval with Dual-role Identifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12987)
*Kaipeng Li,Haitao Yu,Xuanchen Zhou*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: DrIG是一个新型的通用多模态生成式检索框架，通过为每个候选分配一个双重角色的残差量化标识符，同时支持序列化自回归解码和基于集合的前缀无关相关先验，在M-BEIR基准和文本到图像评估数据集上超越了现有生成式多模态检索基线，并通过混合重排序实现了与传统稠密检索方法相媲美的效率-效果权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式信息检索（GIR）面临三个主要挑战：(1)约束自回归解码易受前缀级错误和局部最优影响；(2)大多数GIR研究局限于单模态，缺乏跨文本、图像和混合图文项的指令感知检索；(3)基于离散标识符的GIR检索精度仍落后于先进的稠密向量检索方法。

Method: DrIG框架为每个候选分配一个残差量化标识符，该标识符承担双重角色：在序列角色中，标识符被自回归解码，首token显式建模模态信息，后续token捕获逐渐精细的语义；在基于集合的角色中，相同token被重新解释为无序集合，提供前缀无关的相关性先验，用于引导约束束搜索并缓解局部最优错误。

Result: 在M-BEIR基准和文本到图像评估数据集上的大量实验表明：(1)DrIG在多种任务上持续优于最先进的生成式多模态基线，混合重排在效率-效果之间取得了优于强稠密检索器的权衡；(2)消融和扩展分析揭示了基础LMM、束大小、重排深度和融合策略对检索性能的影响。

Conclusion: DrIG通过双重角色标识符的设计有效解决了生成式检索中的前缀错误和局部最优问题，实现了跨模态的统一检索能力，并为系统设计提供了实用指导。

Abstract: Generative information retrieval (GIR) has emerged as a compelling alternative to the conventional index-retrieve-then-rank retrieval pipeline by training a generator to produce the identifiers of relevant items directly. Despite its promise, a number of open challenges still remain. First, constrained left-to-right decoding is vulnerable to prefix-level errors and local optima. Second, most prior GIR research remains largely unimodal, leaving instruction-aware retrieval across text, image, and mixed image-text items underexplored. Third, although discrete identifier-based GIR offers higher efficiency, its retrieval accuracy still lags behind that of the cutting-edge dense-vector-based retrieval methods. Motivated by these challenges, we propose DrIG, a novel Generative framework for universal multimodal retrieval featuring Dual-role Identifiers, which supports diverse retrieval tasks across multiple modalities and domains. Each candidate is assigned a single residual-quantized identifier that serves two complementary roles. In its sequential role, the identifier is decoded autoregressively, where the first token explicitly models modality and the remaining tokens capture progressively finer semantics. In its set-based role, the same tokens are reinterpreted as an unordered set to provide a prefix-independent relevance prior, which guides constrained beam search and alleviates local-optimum errors. Extensive experiments on the M-BEIR benchmark and the text-to-image evaluation datasets show that:(1)DrIG consistently outperforms state-of-the-art generative multimodal baselines across diverse tasks, while hybrid reranking achieves a favorable efficiency-effectiveness trade-off against strong dense retrievers. (2)Ablation and scaling analyses reveal how the base LMM, beam size, reranking depth, and fusion strategy affect retrieval performance, providing practical guidance for system design.

</details>


### [369] [When Should Multi-Round RAG Stop? Structured Stopping Judgments and Retrieval Reduction in Search-R1](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13237)
*Weimeng Luo*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文针对多轮检索增强生成（RAG）中的停止决策问题，将S2G-RAG的结构化充分性与缺口判断适配到冻结的Search-R1流水线上，训练了一个Qwen3.5-2B判断器，在900个不重叠的HotpotQA问题上基于3,009个状态进行训练。确认性测试集上，该策略相比原生Search-R1减少了77次（3.70%）检索调用，官方精确匹配仅下降0.625个百分点，在基本保持答案准确性的同时降低了检索开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多轮RAG需要决定何时停止搜索，因为部署策略由每条轨迹上首次STOP决定，这本质上是一个序列选择问题而非独立的状态分类任务。现有方法未充分考虑这一时序决策特性。

Method: 将S2G-RAG的结构化充分性与缺口判断适配到冻结的Search-R1流水线，训练Qwen3.5-2B判断器（使用3,009个状态、来自900个不重叠的HotpotQA问题），在分组验证上选择判断器检查点和停止阈值，并在确认性评估前冻结。Search-R1的推理器、检索器、语料库、提示词和搜索预算均保持不变。

Result: 确认性测试集上，训练后的S2G风格结构化判断器使检索调用减少77次（3.70%），官方精确匹配仅下降0.625个百分点。结果表明在广泛保持答案准确性的同时减少了检索。

Conclusion: 训练后的S2G风格结构化判断器能在降低检索开销的同时广泛保持答案准确性。但结果并不意味着准确率不变或提升、安全停止或总推理成本降低。

Abstract: Multi-round retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) must decide when to stop searching as evidence accumulates. Because the deployed policy is determined by the first STOP on each trajectory, this is a sequential selection problem rather than an independent state-classification task. We adapt S2G-RAG's structured sufficiency-and-gap judgment to a frozen Search-R1 pipeline and train a Qwen3.5-2B judge on 3,009 states from 900 disjoint HotpotQA questions. Search-R1's reasoner, retriever, corpus, prompt, and search budget remain unchanged, while the judge checkpoint and stopping threshold are selected on grouped validation and frozen before confirmatory evaluation. On the confirmatory test set, the resulting policy reduces retrieval calls by 77 (3.70\%) relative to Native Search-R1, while Official Exact Match decreases by 0.625 percentage points. Thus, the trained S2G-style structured judge reduces retrieval while broadly preserving answer accuracy. The result does not imply unchanged or improved accuracy, safe stopping, or lower total inference cost.

</details>


### [370] [Structure then Query: Enabling Precise Analytical Queries over Unstructured Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13384)
*Teng Lin,Yuyu Luo,Nan Tang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: AnnoIndex是一个面向非结构化文档的结构化数据系统，通过Annotation Index（基于SchemaLoop自动生成层次化标注模式）和Structured Query Engine（SQL扩展执行计划）实现对文本的高精度过滤和查询，在三个真实数据集上达到最高平均F1分数0.87，优于现有最先进基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非结构化文档占企业及网络数据的大多数，但主流检索方法依赖基于向量相似度的模糊匹配，难以实现精确信息获取和结构化分析推理。

Method: 包含两个核心组件：(1) Annotation Index：通过SchemaLoop模块从原始语料自动创建层次化标注模式，使用轻量级语言模型提取特定值，构建物化结构化索引；(2) Structured Query Engine：将用户问题编译为基于SQL扩展的执行计划，先用标注索引精确过滤文档，再按成本升序逐步应用提取操作，仅对剩余少量需深度语义理解的语料调用LLM，提取的属性会合并回标注索引以降低未来查询成本。

Result: 在三个真实数据集上的实验表明，AnnoIndex持续优于最先进的基线方法，达到最高平均F1分数0.87，并在复杂多跳连接和渐进推理查询上保持稳健性能。

Conclusion: AnnoIndex通过将属性抽取成本从在线查询摊销到一次性构建，避免向量相似度的黑盒匹配，实现了对非结构化文档的高效、精确和可扩展的结构化分析与查询。

Abstract: Unstructured documents constitute the majority of enterprise and web data. With the rapid development of large language models(LLMs), researchers have started to build data systems that analyze unstructured textual documents like operating on databases. However, because mainstream retrieval methods still relies on fuzzy matching based on vector similarity, accurately obtaining information and performing structured analysis and reasoning remains a major challenge. To address these limitations, AnnoIndex introduces two core fundamental components. The first is Annotation Index. The system uses a module called SchemaLoop to automatically create hierarchical annotation schemas from the raw corpus, and then uses lightweight language model to extract specific values. It turns scattered unstructured text into a materialized, structured index that enables low-cost filtering and querying. The annotation index avoids the black-box matching of vector similarity and amortizes attribute extraction costs from online queries to a one-time build. The second innovation is a Structured Query Engine. It compiles user questions into execution plans based on SQL extension. It first uses the Annotation Index for precise documents filtering, then gradually applies extraction operations in ascending order of cost, resorting to LLMs only for the remaining minimal fraction of the corpus that require deep semantic understanding. The extracted attributions are merged into the annotation index, reducing the cost of future queries. Experiments on three real-world datasets demonstrate that AnnoIndex consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, achieving the highest average F1 score (0.87) while maintaining robust performance on complex multi-hop join and progressive reasoning queries.

</details>
